<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对一篇涉及旋转Unruh效应的已发表文档中的若干陈述提出评论，该主题不仅对加速器物理（高能存储环）重要，也对天体物理（中子星磁场中的电子）有启示意义。

Method: 通过对目标文档（arXiv:2607.25004v1）中关于旋转Unruh效应的具体陈述进行逐条回顾与分析，以存储环中的电子/正电子发射光子案例为切入点，并扩展到天体物理场景。

Result: 指出了目标文档中若干有讨论空间的陈述，并强调该问题在加速器物理与天体物理中的跨领域相关性。

Conclusion: 旋转Unruh效应的讨论需要严谨审视，其在存储环与中子星磁场环境中的适用性值得进一步深入探讨。

Abstract: We comment on various statements in a recent document (A.~Deur, S.~J.~Brodsky, C.~D.~Roberts and B.~Terzi{ć}, Regarding the Rotational Unruh Effect, \textit{arXiv:2607.25004v1 [hep.ph]}, (2026)). The examples cited treat electrons (or positrons) circulating and emitting photons in high-energy storage rings. The topic is also of interest in astrophysics, for electrons orbiting in magnetic fields around neutron stars.

</details>


### [134] [Near-threshold resonances in $e^+e^-$ annihilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08769)
*S. G. Salnikov,A. E. Bondar,A. I. Milstein*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了近阈值强子对产生过程中的近阈值共振现象，指出这些共振主要由末态慢速强子之间的相互作用引起，能够解释大量实验中反应截面在阈值附近的非平凡能量依赖行为，并强调对近阈值共振的全面研究可以为理解强子在大距离处的相互作用提供新信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近阈值共振已在许多强子对产生过程中被发现，导致反应截面在低相对速度下显著增大。作者旨在通过综述证明，在所有已知案例中，末态相互作用都能成功解释这些实验观测到的截面能量依赖现象。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统地考察了多种强子对产生过程中的近阈值共振现象，从末态相互作用的角度统一解释实验数据，并据此梳理末态相互作用作为近阈值共振自然机制的证据。

Result: 综述表明，在几乎所有已知的近阈值共振案例中，末态相互作用都能成功解释实验中截面随能量非平凡变化的观测结果。

Conclusion: 对各类过程中近阈值共振的综合研究，能够为揭示强子在大距离（长程）尺度上的相互作用性质提供新的信息与洞察。

Abstract: Near-threshold resonances have been discovered in many hadron pair production processes, resulting in a significant increase in reaction cross sections at low relative velocities of the produced particles. The natural cause of such resonances is the interaction between slow hadrons in the final state. Our review demonstrates that, in virtually all known cases, final-state interactions successfully explain the results of numerous experiments demonstrating a nontrivial energy dependence of cross sections for processes near reaction thresholds. A comprehensive study of near-threshold resonances in various processes can provide new information about the interaction between hadrons at large distances.

</details>


### [135] [The Challenge of Detecting Quantum Nature of Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08803)
*Yu Miyauchi,Hidetoshi Omiya,Atsuhisa Ota,Hiroki Takeda,Takahiro Tanaka*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子引力波的压缩（squeezing）能否作为可观测信号。作者证明，由于真实探测器只耦合特定波包模式，源的压缩在单模可及扇区中退化为未压缩的热态；随机背景中的相位非相干性和立体角覆盖受限也会强烈抑制压缩。但作者发现，若初始探测器态制备为压缩态，量子化的引力波可产生正的压缩见证（witness），而经典外部引力场只能产生位移。然而信号受极小的引力子-探测器耦合限制，因此探测器压缩虽可消除对压缩入射波的需求，但无法克服弱引力相互作用的抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子引力波的压缩效应能否作为可行的观测签名，明确压缩在何种条件下可观测、何种条件下被抑制，以及是否可以用探测器压缩来替代源压缩。

Method: 通过对多种场景的量子光学分析：（1）膨胀时期的双模压缩在单模可及扇区退化为热态；（2）随机背景中相位非相干破坏压缩；（3）探测器立体角覆盖有限抑制来自孤立源的压缩；（4）对比量子引力波与经典引力场分别作用于预压缩探测器态时的响应（压缩见证 vs 位移）。

Result: 源压缩在现实探测条件下不可观测或强烈抑制；但预压缩的探测器态可被量子引力波触发产生正的压缩见证，经典引力场则无法产生，只能产生位移。然而该信号受极端微小的引力子-探测器耦合所约束。

Conclusion: 探测器压缩可以消除对压缩入射波的需求，但无法克服弱引力相互作用带来的信号抑制，因此从引力子-探测器耦合的角度来看，可观测的量子引力波压缩签名仍受到根本性限制。

Abstract: We investigate whether squeezing can provide an observable signature of quantum gravitational waves. Because a realistic detector couples only to a particular wave-packet mode, squeezing in global source modes need not remain observable. We show that inflationary two-mode squeezing reduces to an unsqueezed thermal state in the accessible one-mode sector, phase incoherence washes out squeezing in stochastic backgrounds, and the limited coverage of the solid angle of detectors strongly suppresses squeezing from isolated sources. We then show that source squeezing is not essential, {\it i.e.}, a quantized gravitational wave can generate a positive squeezing witness if the detector state is initially prepared in a squeezed state, whereas a classical external gravitational field cannot, producing only a displacement. However, the resulting signal is bounded by the extremely small graviton--detector coupling. Thus, detector squeezing can remove the need for squeezed incident waves, but not the suppression caused by weak gravitational interaction.

</details>


### [136] [Improved Regge Kinematics and All-Path-Length Corrections to Momentum Broadening in the Quark-Gluon Plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08894)
*Dario van den Berg,Isobel Kolbe*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在GLV（Gyulassy-Levai-Vitev）形式主义框架内研究了高能部分子在夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）中传播时的横向动量增宽，扩展了该框架以包含全路径长度（APL）修正和改善的次Regge运动学修正。到一阶不透明度展开，作者推导出横向动量增宽分布和动量输运系数q̂的解析表达式，发现这些修正尤其在短路径长度和高动量转移情况下能显著改变结果，提供了对QGP中动量增宽更完整的理论描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GLV框架依赖于大分离距离和大形成时间近似，这些近似在较大原子核中成立，但在长度和相干尺度相当的小碰撞系统中可能失效。因此需要系统地放宽这些近似，以提供适用于小碰撞系统（如p+A和pp碰撞）的动量增宽描述。

Method: 在GLV形式主义的一阶不透明度展开下，分别通过放宽大分离距离近似得到全路径长度（APL）修正，通过改进的Regge运动学（超越严格Regge极限）得到次Regge修正，并结合两者得到组合的次Regge-APL修正，推导出横向动量增宽分布和动量输运系数q̂的解析表达式。

Result: 研究发现APL修正和次Regge修正显著改变了横向动量增宽分布和动量输运系数q̂，尤其在短路径长度和高交换动量区域。这些修正提供了超越标准GLV框架的更完整描述。

Conclusion: 通过系统放宽GLV框架中的标准近似条件，本文建立了适用于小碰撞系统以及短路径、高动量转移情形的动量增宽理论，为更准确地理解QGP中部分子的动量输运性质提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We present a study of transverse momentum broadening for high-energy partons propagating through the quark-gluon plasma (QGP), extending the Gyulassy-Levai-Vitev (GLV) formalism to include both all-path-length (APL) corrections and improved sub-Regge kinematics. The standard GLV framework relies on the large separation distance and large formation time approximations, which are well justified in large nuclei but may become unreliable in small collision systems, where the relevant length and coherence scales are comparable. Working to first order in the opacity expansion, we derive analytic expressions for the transverse momentum broadening distribution and the corresponding momentum transport coefficient, $\hat{q}$, while systematically relaxing these approximations. The APL correction arises from relaxing the large separation distance approximation, whereas the sub-Regge and combined sub-Regge-APL corrections are obtained using an improved set of Regge kinematics beyond the strict Regge limit. We find that these corrections can substantially modify both the transverse momentum broadening distribution and the momentum transport coefficient, particularly for short path lengths and high exchanged momentum, providing a more complete theoretical description of momentum broadening in the QGP.

</details>


### [137] [Quark spin-orbit correlations in spin-1 targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09012)
*Hyunwoo Kim,June-Young Kim*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了强子内夸克自旋-轨道角动量的关联，通过QCD算符（宇称奇性能量-动量张量的位置矩）定义该关联，利用QCD运动方程推导出自旋-1强子的两个规范不变求和规则，并分别基于格点QCD和唯象输入估算了ρ介子和氘核的未极化自旋-轨道关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夸克自旋-轨道关联是理解强子内部结构的重要观测量，它反映了夸克螺旋度与纵向动力学轨道角动量的对齐程度。此前对自旋-0和自旋-1/2目标的研究已较充分，但自旋-1强子的自旋-轨道关联及其张量极化依赖尚未被系统研究，因此需要发展相应的理论框架和求和规则。

Method: 作者将自旋-轨道关联定义为QCD算符——宇称奇性（parity-odd）夸克能量-动量张量的位置矩。该二阶张量的矩阵元分解为对称无迹、反对称和迹三个部分。对称无迹部分匹配到轴向广义部分子分布（GPDs）的矩；利用QCD运动方程将反对称部分关联到矢量和张量形状因子，并令迹部分为零。基于这些关系推导出两个规范不变的求和规则，分别给出未极化靶中及张量极化依赖的自旋-轨道关联。

Result: 推导出自旋-1强子的两个规范不变求和规则：一个描述未极化靶中的自旋-轨道关联，另一个描述其张量极化依赖性（该依赖性在自旋-0和自旋-1/2靶中不存在）。利用现有格点QCD数据估算ρ介子的未极化自旋-轨道关联，并利用唯象输入估算氘核的相应关联。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统地建立了自旋-1强子中夸克自旋-轨道关联的理论框架，揭示了张量极化依赖这一自旋-1靶特有的新效应，并为ρ介子和氘核提供了数值估算，为未来实验和格点QCD研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The quark spin-orbit correlation probes the alignment of quark helicity with longitudinal kinetic orbital angular momentum inside a hadron. This correlation is defined by a QCD operator: the position moment of the asymmetric parity-odd quark energy-momentum tensor. The matrix element of this rank-two tensor decomposes into symmetric-traceless, antisymmetric, and trace parts. The symmetric-traceless part is matched to moments of axial generalized parton distributions. The QCD equations of motion relate the antisymmetric part to vector and tensor form factors and set the trace to zero. Using these relations, we derive two gauge-invariant sum rules for the spin-orbit correlation in a spin-$1$ hadron. One gives the correlation in an unpolarized target. The other gives its tensor-polarization dependence, which is absent for spin-$0$ and spin-$1/2$ targets. We estimate the unpolarized spin-orbit correlations for the $ρ$ meson and deuteron using existing lattice and phenomenological inputs, respectively.

</details>


### [138] [Transverse distributions of the energy-momentum tensor for a spin-$3/2$ baryon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09242)
*Hui-Jae Lee,Ki-Hoon Hong,June-Young Kim,Hyun-Chul Kim*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了自旋3/2重子的能量-动量张量横向分布的多极描述，在纵向boost连接的参考系中，通过多极形状因子分解能量、角动量和应力分布，并与光前Rarita-Schwinger旋子计算对照。弹性框架从横断Breit框架连续插值到无穷动量框架（Wigner旋转变为Melosh旋转），以Δ重子Skyrme模型形状因子为输入，发现能量分布由能量单极主导且随纵向boost变化微弱，但在有限纵向动量下通过boost混合为纵向动量及其通量提供主导贡献。纵向极化目标只有单极贡献，横向极化目标则呈现自旋相关的四极、八极形变和偶极位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重子内部分布的能量-动量张量结构是核子结构研究的重要课题。对于自旋3/2的Δ重子，其横向分布在不同参考系（尤其是纵向boost下）如何变换、以及如何与光前形式结果匹配，此前尚未有系统的多极分解描述。该工作旨在填补这一空白，建立统一的弹性框架理论。

Method: 论文采用多极展开方法描述横向能量-动量张量分布。在横断Breit框架中，T^00、T^03、T^33矩阵元通过七个多极形状因子表达。在有限纵向动量下，将Lorentz混合效应与自旋3/2 Wigner旋转对外部态的效应因子化分解，得到包含六个横向多极的弹性框架矩阵元。同时用光前Rarita-Schwinger旋子直接计算T^++、T^+-、T^--作为交叉验证。以Skyrme模型给出的Δ重子引力形状因子作为数值输入进行具体分析。

Result: 弹性框架构造提供了从横断Breit框架到无穷动量框架的连续插值。在无穷动量极限下Wigner旋转退化为Melosh旋转，弹性框架矩阵元复现光前结果。数值分析表明：能量分布由横断Breit框架中定义的能量单极主导，随纵向boost变化微弱；通过boost混合，该单极为有限P_z下的纵向动量分布及其通量提供主导贡献。纵向极化目标的分布仅含单极贡献，而横向极化目标则出现自旋相关的四极、八极形变以及使最大值位移的偶极项。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了自旋3/2重子能量-动量张量横向分布的完整多极框架，统一了横断Breit框架、有限动量弹性框架和光前形式三种描述。揭示了能量单极在跨参考系分布中的核心地位，以及自旋极化对横向分布形态（偶极位移、四极和八极形变）的调制作用。该框架为未来从格点QCD或实验数据提取自旋3/2重子内部结构提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We develop a multipole description of transverse distributions of the energy-momentum tensor for a spin-$3/2$ baryon in frames connected by a longitudinal boost. In the transverse Breit frame, the $T^{00}$, $T^{03}$, and $T^{33}$ matrix elements are expressed through seven multipole form factors for energy, angular momentum, and stress. At finite longitudinal momentum, we factorize the Lorentz mixing of these three components from the spin-$3/2$ Wigner rotations of the external states. The resulting elastic-frame matrix elements contain six transverse multipoles, whose Fourier transforms define the distributions of energy, longitudinal momentum, and longitudinal momentum flux. We also calculate $T^{++}$, $T^{+-}$, and $T^{--}$ directly with light-front Rarita-Schwinger spinors. The elastic-frame construction provides a continuous interpolation from the transverse Breit frame to the infinite-momentum frame. In this limit, the Wigner rotation becomes the Melosh rotation, and the leading elastic-frame matrix elements reproduce the corresponding light-front results. Using the $Δ$-baryon gravitational form factors obtained in the Skyrme model as a representative numerical input, we find that the energy distribution is dominated by the energy monopole defined in the transverse Breit frame and changes only weakly under longitudinal boosts. Through boost mixing, this monopole provides the dominant contribution to the longitudinal momentum distribution and its flux at finite $P_z$. For a longitudinally polarized spin-$3/2$ target, the distributions contain only the monopole contributions, whereas those of a transversely polarized target exhibit spin-dependent quadrupole and octupole deformations and a dipole that shifts their maxima.

</details>


### [139] [Effects of Born-Infeld Electrodynamics on Chiral Symmetry Restoration and Meson Susceptibilities in Holographic QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09489)
*Hiwa A. Ahmed,Peshwaz A. Abdoul*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在全息QCD框架下，基于非线性带电Born-Infeld黑洞背景，数值研究了有限温度和化学势下的手征对称性破缺及相变。发现物理夸克质量下为手征交叉转变（$T_{pc}=0.1477$ GeV），手征极限下变为一级相变（$T_c=0.1337$ GeV）。介子磁化率$(χ_π-χ_σ)$的行为进一步支持了这些结果。Born-Infeld参数$β$仅改变相边界位置而不改变相变级次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用物质在有限温度和化学势下的手征相图是QCD的核心问题之一。传统格点QCD在有限化学势下存在符号问题，而全息QCD提供了一种互补的强耦合计算方法。本文旨在利用非线性Born-Infeld电动力学构建更丰富的全息模型，研究手征相变的结构及其对模型参数的依赖。

Method: 在非线性带电Born-Infeld黑洞背景上构建全息QCD模型，通过在体标量场的渐近行为中提取手征凝聚作为序参量。数值计算了：（1）零化学势下物理夸克质量和手征极限两种情形下的相变行为；（2）不同夸克质量问题下的相变级次分类；（3）有限化学势下第二阶相变边界；（4）介子磁化率$(χ_π-χ_σ)$的响应；（5）Born-Infeld参数$β$对相图的影响。

Result: 零化学势下：物理夸克质量对应手征交叉，$T_{pc}=0.1477$ GeV；手征极限下为一级相变，$T_c=0.1337$ GeV；零光夸克质量下临界奇异夸克质量为$m_s=37$ MeV，区分一阶与二阶相变区域。有限化学势下物理奇异质量（95 MeV）且光夸克无质量时保持二阶相变，$T_c$随$μ$增大而下降。介子磁化率差$(χ_π-χ_σ)$在相边界处快速热衰减并收敛。较小的$β$将二阶相变边界移至更高温度（稳定手征破缺相），但不改变相变级次，研究范围内未发现临界端点。

Conclusion: 非线性Born-Infeld电动力学在全息QCD中显著改变了手征相图，但仍与先前软墙模型研究结果定性一致。该模型展示了从手征交叉到一阶相变的完整转变路径，并揭示了非线性参数$β$对手征破缺相稳定性的调控作用，为深入理解QCD相图提供了有价值的新视角。

Abstract: Within a holographic QCD framework, we numerically investigate chiral symmetry breaking and the associated phase transition at finite temperature and chemical potential. The model is constructed on a nonlinear charged Born-Infeld black hole background. The chiral condensate, extracted from the asymptotic behavior of the bulk scalar field, serves as the primary order parameter. At zero chemical potential, we find a chiral crossover transition for physical quark masses with a pseudocritical temperature of $T_{pc}=0.1477$ GeV. In the chiral limit, the transition becomes first-order with a critical temperature of $T_{c}=0.1337$ GeV. A critical strange quark mass of $m_s=37$ MeV, at zero light quark mass, separates first- and second-order transition regions. For finite chemical potential ($μ$) and a physical strange mass ($m_s=95$ MeV) with massless light quarks, the transition remains second-order, with $T_c$ decreasing as $μ$ increases. These results are further supported by the behavior of meson susceptibilities $(χ_π-χ_σ)$, which exhibit a rapid thermal decay and convergence across the phase boundary. Introducing the Born-Infeld parameter $β$ shifts the second-order phase boundary to higher temperatures for smaller $β$ (stabilizing the chirally broken phase) but does not alter the transition order or introduce a critical endpoint within the studied range. Our findings are consistent with previous soft-wall model studies and highlight the significant role of nonlinear bulk electrodynamics in modifying the chiral phase diagram.

</details>


### [140] [The Living Guide of Machine Learning for Particle Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09531)
*Claudius Krause,Ramon Winterhalder,Matthew Feickert,Benjamin Nachman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文宣布将HEP-ML Living Review（粒子物理机器学习综述）冻结为截止2026年6月1日的档案参考，并用新的HEP-ML Living Guide替代。新指南不再追求全面收录，而是采用策展式、带注释的方式，引导读者找到基础性和代表性工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2020年创建Living Review时，该领域论文增长速度超过任何个人能够跟上的程度，全面且持续更新的参考书目具有即时价值。但此后文献量增长了一个数量级以上，方法远超早期的分类与生成任务，社区也构建了专题综述、基准论文和软件框架等自有生态，原有模式已无法支撑且不再适合该领域。

Method: 将原Living Review冻结，作为覆盖截至2026年6月1日文献的档案性参考，保留为HEP-ML第一阶段研究的稳定记录。同时推出HEP-ML Living Guide作为替代资源，它不再列举所有论文，而是通过策展、注释和引导，为研究者指向基础性与代表性工作。

Result: Living Review被转为档案资源；新的HEP-ML Living Guide取代其持续更新的角色，提供策展式的导读服务而非穷举式文献列表。

Conclusion: 该领域已从快速增长的早期阶段进入成熟且快速多样化的阶段，穷举式综述模式不再适用。转变方向是必要的，新指南以策展和注释的方式帮助研究者高效入门。

Abstract: We started the Living Review of Machine Learning for Particle Physics (HEP-ML Living Review) in 2020 as a community-maintained, near-comprehensive bibliography of machine learning in particle physics. The field was then growing faster than any single researcher could follow, finding the relevant papers was hard, and a structured, continuously updated reference paid off immediately. Since then the literature has grown by more than an order of magnitude, the methods reach far beyond the classification and generation tasks of the early years, and the community has built its own ecosystem of topic-specific reviews, benchmark papers, and software frameworks. The original model no longer serves this field well, and we can no longer sustain it. We therefore change direction. We freeze the Living Review as an archival reference covering the literature up to 1 June 2026, where it remains a stable record of the first phase of HEP-ML. A new resource, the HEP-ML Living Guide, replaces it. It does not list everything. It curates, it annotates, and it points readers to foundational and representative work, so that researchers can find their way into a mature and rapidly diversifying field. In this article we explain why we make this change and how the new resource works.

</details>


### [141] [Prospects for early heavy-quark measurements at the EIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09608)
*Allencris John Rubesh Rajan,Laboni Manna*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估了电子-离子对撞机（EIC）早期运行阶段，在ep和eA碰撞中开展包含重夸克对电产生以及双重味介子对电产生的可行性，给出了总截面、微分分布和预期事件产额，并计算了核修正比R^eA，为EIC的物理可行性研究和后续高精度理论计算提供参考基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EIC即将投入运行，需要提前评估其在重夸克物理方向的探测能力，特别是包含重夸克对电产生和双重味介子对电产生过程在早期科学计划中的统计精度、运动学覆盖范围及核物质效应探测潜力，为实验设计和理论建模提供基线预测。

Method: 基于EIC预期的束流能量和亮度，计算总截面及光子虚度Q^2、重夸克横动量和快度等微分分布；在不同Bjorken-x区间和Q^2范围内评估事件产额；对eA碰撞，利用核部分子分布函数（nPDF）在共线因子化框架下计算核修正比R^eA。

Result: 在中等和大x_Bj区域，EIC早期数据相较于此前HERA测量可实现显著的统计精度提升；在eA碰撞中，核修正比R^eA能够覆盖影遮蔽、反遮蔽和EMC类压低等不同核效应运动学区域，表明早期EIC数据即可对这些效应进行测量。

Conclusion: 该分析作为部分子级基线，支持EIC可行性研究，并为未来的高阶理论计算和实验测量提供参考基准。

Abstract: We assess the prospects for inclusive heavy-quark-pair electroproduction in electron-proton ($ep$) and electron-nucleus ($e$A) collisions, alongside double-charm-pair ($D^0 \bar{D}^0$ and $D^0D^0$) electroproduction in $ep$ collisions, during the early operational phase of the Electron-Ion Collider (EIC). Based on the anticipated beam energies and luminosities of the EIC, we estimate the total cross sections and differential distributions for photon virtuality $Q^2$, heavy-quark transverse momentum, and rapidity. This analysis evaluates different Bjorken-$x$ ($x_{\rm Bj}$) intervals across a range of $Q^2$ values. Expected event yields are estimated from the projected integrated luminosities of the early science programme, demonstrating the substantial improvement in statistical precision over previous HERA measurements at moderate and large $x_{\rm Bj}$. For electron--nucleus collisions, we compute the nuclear modification ratio $R^{e\text{A}}$ within collinear factorisation using nuclear parton distribution functions, and identify the kinematic regimes in which shadowing, anti-shadowing, and EMC-like suppression are accessible with early EIC data. These results are intended to serve as a parton-level baseline to support feasibility studies and to provide a reference for future higher-order theoretical and experimental investigations at the EIC.

</details>


### [142] [NNLO soft functions for heavy-quark pair production at hadron colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09621)
*Guido Bell,Alessandro Broggio,Sebastian Edelmann,Matthew A. Lim,Rudi Rahn*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推广了SoftSERVE框架，用于在背对背运动学中自动计算含末态重夸克的软函数，达到NNLO精度。算法采用适合大质量及混合质量-无质量贡献的相空间参数化，实现高效数值计算。针对满足非阿贝尔指数化的SCET_I可观测量，给出强子顶夸克对产生中0-jettiness软函数的二维网格显式结果，并详细描述重整化程序及阈值极限下三极颜色关联的奇异行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算含末态重夸克的软函数是强子对撞机中顶夸克对产生等过程高精度预测的关键。现有的SoftSERVE框架主要针对无质量粒子，需要将其推广到末态含重夸克的情形，以支持重夸克相关物理过程在NNLO阶的精确理论预言。

Method: 推广SoftSERVE框架，采用适合大质量粒子和混合质量-无质量粒子贡献的相空间参数化，实现软函数的自动计算。算法针对满足非阿贝尔指数化的SCET_I可观测量，输出以二维网格形式给出的数值结果。

Result: 给出了强子顶夸克对产生过程中0-jettiness软函数在NNLO阶的显式数值结果（以二维网格形式呈现），并描述了重整化程序以及阈值极限下三极颜色相关项的奇异行为。

Conclusion: 成功将SoftSERVE推广至末态含重夸克的软函数计算，为顶夸克对产生等重味物理过程提供了NNLO阶的精确理论输入，同时阐明了相关颜色关联结构的阈值性质。

Abstract: We generalise the SoftSERVE framework for the automated computation of soft functions involving final-state heavy quarks in back-to-back kinematics up to next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) in the strong-coupling expansion. Our algorithm employs suitable phase-space parameterisations for both massive and mixed massless-massive contributions, allowing for an efficient numerical evaluation. Focusing on SCET$_\mathrm{I}$ observables that satisfy non-Abelian exponentiation, we present explicit results for the 0-jettiness soft function in hadronic top-quark pair production in the form of two-dimensional grids. We describe the renormalisation procedure in detail and discuss the singular behaviour of the tripole colour correlations in the threshold limit.

</details>


### [143] [Leptogenesis in Brane-modified cosmology: Signatures in primordial gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09814)
*Amit Dutta Banik,Arnab Paul*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了膜启发（brane-inspired）的早期宇宙演化修正对通过轻子生成（leptogenesis）实现重子生成（baryogenesis）的影响。修正的宇宙历史改变了轻子不对称性所满足的玻尔兹曼方程演化，使得在标准宇宙学场景中不可达的参数空间中也能实现成功的轻子生成。同时，修正的宇宙历史也改变了暴胀期间产生的原初引力波（PGWs）的谱能量密度（SED），从而建立了一种通过未来引力波观测间接探测高能标轻子生成场景的新途径。论文考虑了暴胀后、辐射主导期之前的单阶段和多阶段刚性状态方程（stiff equation of state）两种场景，并确定了可实现成功轻子生成及PGW可观测性的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准宇宙学场景下的轻子生成机制在某些参数空间中无法实现成功的重子生成，而膜启发模型对早期宇宙演化历史的修正可能改变这一局面，并为高能物理过程提供新的探测窗口。

Method: 通过修改早期宇宙的演化历史，重新求解轻子不对称性满足的玻尔兹曼方程，分析单阶段和多阶段刚性状态方程场景下的轻子生成条件；同时计算修正宇宙历史对暴胀产生的原初引力波谱能量密度的影响，以确定引力波可观测性与轻子生成参数空间的关联。

Result: 在单阶段和多阶段刚性状态方程场景中，均识别出了可实现成功轻子生成的参数空间；同时确定了该参数空间下原初引力波谱能量密度发生形状改变的条件，从而为轻子生成场景提供了可被未来观测检验的间接窗口。

Conclusion: 膜启发的修正宇宙历史不仅扩大了成功轻子生成的参数空间，还在原初引力波谱中留下了独特印记，未来引力波观测有望成为间接探测高能标轻子生成场景的有效手段。

Abstract: We investigate the implications of brane-inspired modifications of the evolutionary history of the early universe on the process of baryogenesis via leptogenesis. A modified cosmic history alters the evolution of the Boltzmann equations governing lepton asymmetry, providing the possibility of successful leptogenesis in regions of the parameter space inaccessible in the standard cosmological scenario. Furthermore, a modified cosmic history also alters the shape of an otherwise scale-invariant spectral energy density (SED) of primary gravitational waves (PGWs) produced during inflation. This establishes a novel yet indirect probe of high scale leptogenesis scenarios through the observation of the SED of PGWs via future observations. We consider two scenarios in this work with single and multiple epochs of stiff equation of state(s) after inflation and before the epoch of radiation domination. We identify the parameter space where successful leptogenesis is possible, along with the possible observability of the PGWs, hence providing an indirect window into this leptogenesis scenario through PGWs.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

### [144] [Analytic Integration of the Lambert W Cosmic Fluid Model H(z) Formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07878)
*Ken Roberts,S. R. Valluri*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对Lambert W (LW)宇宙流体状态方程模型中的数值积分进行了解析推导，给出了ln(H(z)/H_0)的闭式解析表达式，取代了Dubey等人2026年工作中使用的数值积分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LW模型由Saha和Bamba在2019-2020年提出，近期Dubey等人的工作对该模型进行了观测数据拟合以估计参数，但其公式依赖于数值积分。作者希望将该积分解析化，以便更方便地探索该模型。

Method: 将Dubey等人论文中对数相对哈勃因子ln(H(z)/H_0)的数值积分表达式进行解析求解，推导出H(z)/H_0的闭式解析表达式。

Result: 成功将该数值积分解析化为封闭解析形式，得到了H(z)/H_0的解析表达式。

Conclusion: 得到的H(z)/H_0解析表达式可能为未来探索Lambert W宇宙流体模型提供便利。

Abstract: The Lambert $W$ (LW) model for the cosmic fluid equation of state was proposed by S. Saha and K. Bamba in 2019-2020. A recent (early 2026) paper by Dubey, et al, carries out a new fit of the LW model to observational data, in order to estimate the model's parameters. That paper exhibits a formula for the logarithm of the relative Hubble factor at redshift $z$, $\ln(H(z)/H_0)$, which is based upon a numerical integration. That integral can be evaluated analytically, and we present the details in this working paper. The resulting analytic expression for $H(z)/H_0$ may be convenient for exploration of the LW model.

</details>


### [145] [Perturbation Theory for Time-dependent Singular Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08108)
*Kaya Güven Akbaş,Fatih Erman,O. Teoman Turgut*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个用于量子系统中含时点相互作用的微扰框架，覆盖非自治哈密顿量在静态点相互作用谱基下的展开，推导了第一和第二阶极点偏移、投影修正和跃迁振幅，并通过多个实例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间依赖的点相互作用在量子力学中具有重要应用，但现有的微扰理论主要针对非奇异势。点相互作用（尤其在高维中）涉及重整化过程，如何系统地处理含时点相互作用中的微扰展开是一个尚未完善的问题。

Method: 对于具有纯离散非微扰谱的量子系统，在二维和三维中通过热核正则化获得重整化解式矩阵来描述点相互作用；在一维中由于对角Green函数有限无需重整化。时间依赖可通过相互作用参数或支持点运动引入。方法将非自治哈密顿量在当前对应的静态点相互作用问题的谱基中进行展开，推导微扰级数中的各阶修正项。

Result: 推导了第一和第二阶极点偏移、投影修正和跃迁振幅；通过含时耦合的点相互作用、移动点相互作用的谐振子、球面上移动相互作用中心的粒子等实例进行了说明；并证明在一维非重整化情形下，该方法可约化为标准的含时微扰理论。

Conclusion: 该框架为含时点相互作用的量子系统提供了一套统一的微扰处理方案，特别适用于离散谱系统，并可在一维极限下与标准含时微扰理论自然衔接。

Abstract: We develop a perturbative framework for time-dependent point interactions in quantum systems with purely discrete unperturbed spectrum. In two and three dimensions the point interaction is described through the renormalized resolvent obtained from heat-kernel regularization, while in one dimension the diagonal Green function is finite and no renormalization is required. Time dependence is introduced either through the interaction parameter or through the motion of the support point. In both cases we expand the non-autonomous Hamiltonian in the spectral basis of the corresponding static point-interaction problem and derive the first- and second-order pole shifts, projection corrections, and transition amplitudes. The method is illustrated by explicit examples: point interactions with time-dependent coupling, harmonic oscillators perturbed by moving point interactions, and a particle on a sphere with a moving interaction center. We also discuss the one-dimensional harmonic oscillator with a moving delta potential, showing that in the non-renormalized case the present formulation reduces to the standard time-dependent perturbation theory.

</details>


### [146] [Moving Frames for Lie Pseudo-groups: A Recursive Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08249)
*Peter J. Olver,Francis Valiquette*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的递归实现方法，用于无限维Lie伪群作用的等变移动框架方法。该方法允许在计算延拓作用之前执行伪群归一化，从而避免引入不必要复杂的中间表达式，并通过多个示例验证了算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的等变移动框架方法在计算无限维Lie伪群作用时，需要先计算延拓作用再进行归一化，这会产生大量不必要的复杂中间表达式，导致计算效率低下且表达式繁琐。

Method: 提出一种递归实现的等变移动框架方法，核心创新在于可以先执行伪群归一化，再计算延拓作用，从而避免中间表达式的过度膨胀。

Result: 通过多个示例验证了新算法的正确性和高效性，证明了该递归实现方法能够有效简化计算过程。

Conclusion: 该递归方法为无限维Lie伪群等变移动框架的计算提供了一种更高效的实现方式，能够显著减少不必要的中间计算复杂度。

Abstract: A new recursive implementation of the equivariant moving frame method for infinite-dimensional Lie pseudo-group actions is presented. It allows pseudo-group normalizations to be performed before the prolonged action is computed, thereby avoiding the introduction of unnecessarily complicated intermediate expressions. Several examples are provided to illustrate the algorithm.

</details>


### [147] [New results on the domain of analyticity of the free energy for the Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08396)
*Nguyen Tong Xuan,Nguyen Dang Thien Thu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了伊辛模型在外加非零磁场下、高温及低温三个区域中自由能的解析性，利用Fernandez-Procacci收敛准则、生成函数技术和图论方法，在三个区域均获得了改进的收敛条件，尤其在高温区域通过基于Veblen定理的新展开得到了比经典结果大得多的解析性区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊辛模型自由能的解析性直接关系到相变现象的理解，特别是在非零外磁场条件下对解析性区域进行精确刻画是统计力学中的核心问题。现有方法在高温、低温及强磁场区域的收敛条件仍有改进空间。

Method: 结合三类技术工具：Fernandez-Procacci收敛准则（用于簇展开收敛性判定）、生成函数技术（在强场和低温区域对聚合物与轮廓估计给出更锐利的界）、以及基于Veblen定理的图论方法（构造新的高温展开以扩大解析性区域）。

Result: 在三个区域（高温、低温、非零磁场）均获得了比经典结果更优的收敛条件：强场和低温区域通过生成函数方法得到更精确的聚合物/轮廓估计；高温区域通过Veblen定理驱动的展开使解析性区域显著增大。

Conclusion: 该工作通过整合多种数学工具，系统性地改进了伊辛模型自由能解析性区域的已知结果，其中基于Veblen定理的高温展开尤为突出，为后续相关模型的解析性研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: We investigate the analyticity of the free energy of the Ising model in the presence of a non-zero external magnetic field, at high temperature, and at low temperature. Using the Fernandez--Procacci convergence criterion for cluster expansions, together with generating-function techniques and graph-theoretical methods, we derive improved convergence conditions in all three regimes. In particular, the generating-function approach yields sharper estimates for polymers and contours in the strong-field and low-temperature regimes, while a new high-temperature expansion based on Veblen's theorem provides a substantially larger analyticity region than the classical results in the literature.

</details>


### [148] [Notes on the Optical-Lattice Hard-Core Bose Gas of Aizenman--Lieb--Seiringer--Solovej--Yngvason](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08409)
*Yoshitsugu Sekine*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文是对Aizenman-Lieb-Seiringer-Solovej-Yngvason关于交错光学晶格硬核玻色气体的详细说明性笔记，用统一记号重述并扩展了其论证和主要输入，描述了热力学极限平衡态的中央分解和轨道分解，旨在使压缩或委托的步骤显式化，而非引入新方法或改进已有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原论文中的许多论证步骤被压缩或委托给其他文献，给读者理解带来困难。本文旨在通过详细的说明性笔记，用统一的数学记号重述和扩展原始论证，使这些隐式步骤显式化，帮助读者更好地理解交错光学晶格硬核玻色气体的核心结论。

Method: 采用说明性笔记的形式，在统一记号的框架下重述并扩展原始论文的论证过程，详细阐述其核心输入（principle inputs），并系统描述热力学极限下平衡态的中心分解（central decomposition）和轨道分解（orbitwise decomposition）结构。

Result: 本文不对原始结果做任何方法上的改进或估计上的增强，其"结果"在于提供了一套完整、显式的论证展开，使原论文中压缩或委托的步骤变得清晰可循，从而提高了该领域结果的可用性和可读性。

Conclusion: 本文定位为教学性和说明性的补充材料，价值在于教育性和文献可读性的提升，而非科学结论的创新。它使交错光学晶格硬核玻色气体的核心论证对更广泛的读者群体可访问。

Abstract: These are detailed expository notes on the staggered optical-lattice hard-core Bose gas of Aizenman, Lieb, Seiringer, Solovej and Yngvason. They restate and expand the arguments and their principal inputs in a common notation, and describe the central and orbitwise decompositions of thermodynamic-limit equilibrium states. The purpose is to make compressed or delegated steps explicit, not to introduce a new method, strengthen the estimates, or otherwise improve the cited results.

</details>


### [149] [The $\mathcal{L}$-Calculus for Causal Variational Principles: An Exterior Differential Calculus on Non-Smooth Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08811)
*Felix Finster,Niky Kamran,Frank van der Top*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了因果变分原理的微分演算，将微分形式的外微分演算及相关微分拓扑结构推广到非光滑空间，包含外导数、de Rham上同调、粘合构造、Künneth公式、Poincaré引理、Stokes定理和Gauß散度定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果变分原理作为量子物理和广义相对论中一种新兴的数学框架，其定义于非光滑空间之上，缺乏传统微分几何中成熟的微分演算工具。为了在非光滑环境中开展几何与分析研究，需要建立一套推广的外微分演算体系。

Method: 发展一套针对因果变分原理的微分演算框架，包括：外导数算子、de Rham上同调理论、微分形式的限制与延拓（粘合构造）、Mayer-Vietoris序列、Künneth公式和Poincaré引理；同时证明Stokes定理和Gauß散度定理在该框架下的推广版本，并通过多个例子对构造和结果进行说明。

Result: 成功建立了因果变分原理上的推广微分演算，将经典微分形式的全部核心运算和相应的微分拓扑结构（上同调、粘合、Künneth公式、Poincaré引理）以及积分定理（Stokes、Gauß）推广至非光滑空间，并用实例验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为因果变分原理提供了完整的微分演算工具包，填补了其在非光滑环境下缺乏外微分和微分拓扑结构的空白，为后续在量子场论和引力理论中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: A differential calculus for causal variational principles is developed, which generalizes the exterior calculus of differential forms and some of the associated differential topological structures to non-smooth spaces. Our calculus includes the exterior derivative, de Rham cohomology, glueing constructions (restrictions and extensions of differential forms, Mayer-Vietoris sequence), a Künneth formula and Poincar{é}'s lemma. Moreover, we prove versions of Stokes' theorem and the Gauß divergence theorem. The constructions and results are illustrated by several examples.

</details>


### [150] [Theoretical analysis of the maximum range of a projectile released from a pendulum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09157)
*Ken Yamamoto*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了从简单摆释放的抛射体运动，重点研究使水平射程最大的最优释放角及对应的最大射程。该问题作为Tarzan跳跃问题的简化模型，利用解析方法证明最优释放角由三次方程刻画，并随初速度增大而增大，同时推导了低、高初速度极限下的渐近表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简化实际中类似Tarzan跳跃的物理问题，通过简单摆模型研究抛射体最优释放角与最大射程，为这类具有初始速度与重力共同作用的抛射问题提供理论解析基础。

Method: 采用简单解析方法，建立摆上抛射体的运动方程，将水平射程表示为释放角的函数，通过求极值推导最优释放角所满足的三次方程，并在低、高初速度极限下进行渐近展开。

Result: 最优释放角由三次方程刻画，且随初始速度单调增大；在低初速度和高初速度两个极限下分别获得了最优角和最大射程的渐近表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作为变初始位置和初速度方向的抛射体最优释放问题提供了完整的解析框架，深化了对Tarzan跳跃类问题的理解。

Abstract: The motion of a projectile released from a simple pendulum is analyzed, with particular emphasis on investigating the optimal release angle that maximizes the horizontal range and the corresponding maximum range. This system serves as a simplified model of the Tarzan jump problem. Using simple analytical methods, the optimal release angle is shown to be characterized by a cubic equation and to increase with the initial velocity. In addition, asymptotic expressions for both the optimal angle and maximum range are derived in the limits of low and high initial velocity.

</details>


### [151] [Eigenvalue asymptotics for the one-particle density matrix and one-particle kinetic energy density operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09570)
*Søren Fournais,Alexander V. Sobolev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了N粒子原子薛定谔算符本征函数的一粒子密度矩阵γ和动能密度矩阵κ的特征值渐近公式。对于一般本征函数，特征值λ_k(Γ)和λ_k(K)分别以k^{-8/3}和k^{-2}的速率衰减；当本征函数完全反对称时，由于正则性增强，衰减速率分别加速为k^{-10/3}和k^{-8/3}，且所有渐近常数均由本征函数及其梯度显式给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子化学计算中，一粒子密度矩阵γ和动能密度矩阵κ的积分算子特征值行为对于估计有限维逼近的误差至关重要。然而，这两个矩阵在粒子的对合点（pair coalescence）处具有奇异性，导致其特征值精确渐近行为此前未知，从而限制了计算误差的量化分析。

Method: 作者利用近期获得的关于原子本征函数在粒子对合点奇异性的正则性结果来识别和分离这些奇异性，然后应用具有齐次符号的拟微分算子的Birman-Solomyak谱渐近理论，分别对一般本征函数和完全反对称本征函数两种情形推导特征值渐近公式。

Result: 对于一般本征函数，得到lim_{k→∞} k^{8/3}λ_k(Γ)=A^{8/3}和lim_{k→∞} k^2λ_k(K)=B^2，其中A、B是显式给出的非负常数。对于完全反对称本征函数，由于正则性增强，渐近公式变为lim_{k→∞} k^{10/3}λ_k(Γ)=A_{asym}^{10/3}和lim_{k→∞} k^{8/3}λ_k(K)=B_{asym}^{8/3}，常数依赖于ψ的梯度。

Conclusion: 一粒子密度矩阵和动能密度矩阵的特征值渐近由波函数在粒子对合点处的奇异行为完全决定；反对称性（泡利不相容原理）带来的正则性增强会显著加快特征值衰减速率，这对量子化学计算中基组逼近的误差估计具有重要理论指导意义。

Abstract: Let $ψ({\mathbf x})$, ${\mathbf x} \in{\mathbb R}^{3N}$, be an eigenfunction of the $N$-particle atomic Schrödinger operator. We consider the one-particle density matrix $γ(x, y)$ and one-particle kinetic energy density $\varkappa(x, y)$, $x, y\in {\mathbb R}^3$, associated with the eigenfunction $ψ$. Both functions play a central role in quantum chemistry computations of atomic and molecular bound states: the knowledge of the eigenvalue behaviour of the integral operators ${\sfΓ}$ and ${\sf{K}}$ with kernels $γ(x, y)$ and $\varkappa(x, y)$ serves to estimate the errors due to finite-dimensional approximations. We find the following asymptotic formulas for their eigenvalues $λ_k({\sfΓ})>0$ and $λ_k({\sf{K}})>0$: \[
  \lim_{k\to \infty} k^{\frac{8}{3}} \,λ_k({\sfΓ}) = A^{\frac{8}{3}},\quad \lim_{k\to \infty} k^2\,λ_k({\sf{K}}) = B^2, \] where $A$ and $B$ are non-negative constants given explicitly in terms of the eigenfunction $ψ$. These asymptotics are determined by the singularities of the function $ψ$ at pair coalescence points of the particles. To identify and isolate these singularities we use some recent regularity results for $ψ$. At the last step we apply Birman-Solomyak spectral asymptotics results for pseudodifferential operators with homogeneous symbols.
  In the special case where the eigenfunction $ψ$ is totally antisymmetric, it exhibits enhanced regularity, which leads to a faster decay of the eigenvalues $λ_k(\sfΓ)$ and $λ_k(\sf{K})$. The asymptotic formulas take the form \[ \lim_{k\to \infty} k^{\frac{10}{3}} \,λ_k({\sfΓ}) = \big(A_{asym}\big)^{\frac{10}{3}},\quad \lim_{k\to \infty} k^{\frac{8}{3}} \,λ_k({\sf K}) = \big(B_{asym}\big)^{\frac{8}{3}}, \] where $A_{asym}$ and $B_{asym}$ are non-negative constants given explicitly in terms of the gradient of $ψ$.

</details>


### [152] [The determinant of the Dirichlet-to-Neumann map for a surface with boundary and periods of holomorphic differentials on its double](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09737)
*Dmitrii Korikov,Alexey Kokotov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射的zeta正则化行列式这一共形不变量在亏格g>0情形的初等计算公式，仅通过M的double 2M上全纯微分的周期表示。该公式是Guillarmou和Guillopé结果的初等对应物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Edward和Wu证明了g=0时该共形不变量等于1；对于g>0，Guillarmou和Guillopé通过Ruelle和Selberg zeta函数给出了两个显式表达式，但这些公式依赖于负常曲率曲面上的谱zeta函数，计算较为复杂。作者希望找到更简单、仅依赖双曲面M上全纯微分周期的初等公式。

Method: 作者利用M的Hilbert变换的性质以及Kontsevich-Vishik-Friedlander-Guillemin对伪微分算子行列式的正则化方法，通过2M上全纯微分的周期来建立该共形不变量的显式表示。具体地，建立了（均匀化的）M、2M的长度谱与2M上全纯微分周期之间的联系。

Result: 得到了Guillarmou和Guillopé公式的一个初等版本，该公式仅用double 2M上全纯微分的周期表示Dirichlet-to-Neumann算子行列式所定义的共形不变量。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了计算该共形不变的更简单途径，避免了负常曲率曲面zeta函数的复杂工具，同时揭示了M、其double 2M的长度谱与全纯微分周期之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Let $(M,g)$ be a smooth orientable $2d$ Riemannian manifold of genus $\mathfrak{g}$ with Riemannian metric $g$ and connected boundary $Γ$. Let $Λ$ be the Dirichlet-to-Neumann map on $Γ$ and let ${\rm det}_ζ(Λ)$ be its (modified, i. e. with zero mode excluded) $ζ$-regularized determinant. It is well-known that the quantity ${\rm det}_ζ(Λ)/|Γ|$ (where $|Γ|$ is the length of $Γ$) is a conformal invariant. It was shown by Edward and Wu (\cite{EV}) that this invariant equals one for $\mathfrak{g}=0$; in the case $\mathfrak{g}>0$ Guillarmou and Guillopé \cite{Guillarmou} found two explicit expressions for this invariant through the Rouelle and (respectively) the Selberg zeta-functions of the two surfaces of negative constant curvature from the conformal class of $(M,g)$: one is of infinite volume and complete whereas another has geodesic boundary.
  We present an elementary counterpart of the formulae of Guillarmou and Guillopé using the periods of holomorphic differentials on the double $2M$ of $M$ only. Our approach is based on the properties of the Hilbert transform of $M$ \cite{B,HilbKor} and the Kontsevich-Vishik-Friedlander-Guillemin regularization of the determinants of pseudodifferenial operators \cite{KV,Ww,F}. In particular, a connection between the length spectra of (uniformized) $M$, $2M$ and the periods of holomorphic differentials on $2M$ is established.

</details>

### [153] [Disconnected multigraded Hilbert schemes on $\mathbb{P}^2\times\mathbb{P}^1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07704)
*Yairon Cid-Ruiz*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在双射影空间$\mathbb{P}^2 \times \mathbb{P}^1$上构造了一个无限族的非连通多重分次Hilbert概形。具体地，对任意整数$a \ge 2$，具有特定Hilbert多项式$p_a(z_1,z_2)=2az_1+az_2+3a-2a^2$的多重分次Hilbert概形${\rm Hilb}_{p_a}(\mathbb{P}^2 \times \mathbb{P}^1)$是不连通的。这导致即使在仅含五个变量的多项式环的标准双分次情形下，也存在无穷多个非连通的Haiman-Sturmfels多重分次Hilbert概形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重分次Hilbert概形的连通性问题一直是代数几何中的核心问题之一。已有的非连通性结果通常需要较为复杂的设定，本文旨在构造一个更简单、更基础的无限族非连通例子，即在标准的双射影空间$\mathbb{P}^2 \times \mathbb{P}^1$上，在五变量多项式环的标准双分次情形下，寻找无限多个非连通的多重分次Hilbert概形。

Method: 作者构造了一族以整数$a \ge 2$为参数的多项式$p_a(z_1,z_2)=2az_1+az_2+3a-2a^2$，并证明对应的多重分次Hilbert概形${\rm Hilb}_{p_a}(\mathbb{P}^2 \times \mathbb{P}^1)$不连通。通过系统地构造该参数族的无限多个不连通实例，将非连通性推广到了更基本的五变量多项式环标准双分次情形。

Result: 证明了对于任意$a \ge 2$，多重分次Hilbert概形${\rm Hilb}_{p_a}(\mathbb{P}^2 \times \mathbb{P}^1)$都是不连通的，从而得到了一个无限族的非连通多重分次Hilbert概形。由此推论出存在无穷多个非连通的Haiman-Sturmfels多重分次Hilbert概形，且其底空间仅为五变量多项式环上的标准双分次。

Conclusion: 该结果显著降低了已知非连通多重分次Hilbert概形例子的复杂度（变量数从更多降低到五个），表明即使在最基础的双分次设定下，多重分次Hilbert概形的连通性仍然可以被破坏，且破坏方式具有无穷多参数化族。

Abstract: We exhibit an infinite family of disconnected multigraded Hilbert schemes on the biprojective space $\mathbb{P}^2_{\mathbb{k}} \times_\mathbb{k} \mathbb{P}^1_{\mathbb{k}}$. More precisely, for any integer $a \ge 2$ and $p_a(z_1,z_2) = 2az_1+az_2+3a-2a^2 \in \mathbb{Q}[z_1,z_2]$, the multigraded Hilbert scheme ${\rm Hilb}_{p_a}(\mathbb{P}^2_{\mathbb{k}}\times_\mathbb{k} \mathbb{P}^1_{\mathbb{k}})$ is disconnected. As a consequence, there exist infinitely many disconnected Haiman-Sturmfels multigraded Hilbert schemes even for a standard bigrading on a polynomial ring in only five variables.

</details>


### [154] [The dimension threshold for Batyrev's non-negativity conjecture on stringy Hodge numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07836)
*Jiahui Huang,Matthew Satriano*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Batyrev非负性猜想在维数≤4时成立，并在维数≥5时构造了反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Batyrev非负性猜想是镜像对称、动机积分和McKay对应中的核心指导性猜想，其真伪直接影响这些领域的基础理论发展，因此验证或否定该猜想具有重要意义。

Method: 通过分维数讨论：在低维（≤4）情形下利用代数几何与奇点理论的工具给出证明；在高维（≥5）情形下构造具体的反例来否定猜想的一般性。

Result: 证明了Batyrev非负性猜想在维数不超过4时成立，同时构造出维数至少为5的反例，表明该猜想在高维情形下不再成立。

Conclusion: Batyrev非负性猜想的成立具有维度界限（≤4成立，≥5不成立），这一结果精确刻画了该猜想的适用范围，对镜像对称、动机积分和McKay对应等领域的理论边界给出了重要修正。

Abstract: Batyrev's non-negativity conjecture has been a major guiding conjecture in mirror symmetry, motivic integration, and the McKay correspondence. We show the conjecture is true in dimension at most 4 and give counter-examples in dimension at least 5.

</details>


### [155] [A remark on Banecki's theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07843)
*Shulim Kaliman*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了所有光滑完备有理簇在Gromov意义下都是代数椭圆的，将Banecki关于光滑射影有理簇的结论推广到了更一般的光滑完备情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Banecki已证明每个光滑射影有理簇在Gromov意义下是代数椭圆的。本文的动机是将这一结果从射影情形推广到更一般的完备情形，因为完备簇比射影簇更广泛，需要修正原证明以覆盖这一推广。

Method: 修改Banecki的证明方法，将原有的针对光滑射影有理簇的论证进行调整和推广，使其适用于光滑完备有理簇。

Result: 证明了每个光滑完备有理簇在Gromov意义下都是代数椭圆的。

Conclusion: 本文成功将Banecki的结果从光滑射影有理簇推广到光滑完备有理簇，证明了完备情形下代数椭圆性仍然成立。

Abstract: J. Banecki proved that every smooth projective rational variety is algebraically elliptic in the sense of Gromov. Modifying his proof we show that the same is true for every smooth complete rational variety.

</details>


### [156] [Deformations, local freeness, and base change for higher Du Bois singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07849)
*Haoming Ning*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了严格高阶Du Bois奇异性在小形变下不变，并基于此证明了具有严格高阶Du Bois纤维的相对Du Bois复形的基变换定理，回答了Kovács–Taji的问题。同时展示了1-Du Bois纤维在形变不变性和基变换方面的反例，表明严格性条件本质上是尖锐的。作为基变换的应用，作者将Friedman–Laza的局部自由定理推广到光滑曲线上的一般族（超越局部完全交情形），并证明了任意基上的Hodge数常性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kovács–Taji提出关于相对Du Bois复形基变换定理的问题，此前对于Du Bois奇异性的形变不变性仅有低阶情形的部分结果。作者旨在确立严格高阶Du Bois奇异性在形变下的稳定性，并证明相应的基变换定理，填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者首先证明严格高阶Du Bois奇异性（严格k-Du Bois）在小形变下保持不变；随后利用这一定理建立相对Du Bois复形在严格高阶Du Bois纤维条件下的基变换定理。同时构造反例说明1-Du Bois（非严格）情形下形变不变性和基变换均可能失效，进而说明严格条件的必要性。最后，将基变换定理应用于两个方向：推广Friedman–Laza的局部自由定理（从局部完全交族推广到光滑曲线上的任意族），以及证明任意基上Hodge数的常性。

Result: 严格高阶Du Bois奇异性在小形变下不变；证明了相对Du Bois复形的基变换定理（解决了Kovács–Taji的问题）；给出了1-Du Bois纤维不满足形变不变性和基变换的反例，证明严格性条件不可去除；推广了Friedman–Laza定理；建立了任意基上Hodge数常性定理。

Conclusion: 严格高阶Du Bois奇异性具有稳健的形变不变性，由此导出的基变换定理是强有力的工具，能够统一地推广已知结果并在更广泛的情境下（任意基）证明Hodge数常性。1-Du Bois情形的反例表明严格性条件对该理论是本质的。

Abstract: We prove that strict higher Du Bois singularities are invariant under small deformations. Using this, we prove a base change theorem for the relative Du Bois complex with strict higher Du Bois fibers, answering a question of Kovács--Taji. We exhibit failures of deformation invariance and base change for $1$-Du Bois fibers, showing that the strictness condition is essentially sharp. As applications of base change, we generalize the local-freeness theorem of Friedman--Laza beyond the local complete intersection setting for families over a smooth curve, and prove constancy of Hodge numbers for families over an arbitrary base.

</details>


### [157] [A note on geometry of Bridgeland Moduli spaces on Gushel-Mukai threefolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07853)
*Songtao Kenneth Ma*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Gushel-Mukai三流形X的Kuznetsov分量Ku(X)中的Bridgeland半稳定对象模空间是正规的；在特定数值条件下，若数值类是本原的且X是一般的，则相应的Bridgeland模空间是整的（integral）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gushel-Mukai三流形是代数几何中的重要研究对象，其Kuznetsov分量上的Bridgeland模空间的几何性质（如正规性、整性）对于理解其导出范畴结构及相关的模空间理论具有重要意义。

Method: 通过Bridgeland稳定性条件，研究Ku(X)中半稳定对象的模空间；利用数值条件、本原性假设以及X为一般情形的条件，结合奇点理论和模空间的正规性判据来证明相应结论。

Result: 得到了Bridgeland模空间在Ku(X)上的正规性结果；在附加的数值本原性和X一般性条件下，进一步证明了模空间的整性。

Conclusion: 该工作深化了对Gushel-Mukai三流形Kuznetsov分量模空间几何性质的理解，为后续研究其在双有理几何和导出范畴中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a Gushel--Mukai threefold and let $\operatorname{Ku}(X)$ be its Kuznetsov component. We show that the Bridgeland moduli spaces of semistable objects in $\operatorname{Ku}(X)$ are normal. Moreover, under certain numerical conditions, if the numerical class is primitive and $X$ is general, we prove that the corresponding Bridgeland moduli space is integral.

</details>


### [158] [Automorphisms of very general blow up](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07983)
*Supravat Sarkar*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明：如果X是维数≥2且不是有理曲面的射影簇，那么在足够多的一般点上取爆炸（blow up）后，所得簇没有非平凡自同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于P^2和P^3的类似结果已被证明，本文旨在将此结论推广到更一般的射影簇X上，从而更广泛地理解射影簇爆炸后自同构群的平凡化现象。

Method: 利用射影簇X在足够多的一般点上的爆炸构造，并通过几何与双有理方法分析所得簇的自同构群，证明其不存在非平凡自同构。

Result: 对于维数≥2且非有理曲面的任意射影簇X，在足够多的一般点上爆炸后，所得簇的自同构群是平凡的。

Conclusion: 该结果将此前仅对P^2和P^3成立的结论推广至所有非有理曲面的高维射影簇，深化了对射影簇爆炸后自同构群结构的理解。

Abstract: We show that if $X$ is a projective variety of dimension $\geq 2$ that is not a rational surface, then the blow up of $X$ at sufficiently many very general points has no nontrivial automorphism. Similar results were known before for $\mathbb{P}^2$ and $\mathbb{P}^3.$

</details>


### [159] [Pseudo-hyperbolicity of Horikawa surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08276)
*Anibal Aravena,Jaime Negrete,Wern Yeong*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了非常一般的Horikawa曲面（当p_g≥5时，以及第一类且p_g∈{3,4}时）只包含有限多条有理曲线或椭圆曲线，并给出了这些曲线的显式刻画与计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Horikawa曲面是拥有极小Chern斜率的一般型极小复代数曲面，其几何结构特殊。研究其上有理曲线与椭圆曲线的数量有助于理解这类曲面的代数几何性质与模空间结构。

Method: 通过利用Horikawa曲面的具体分类结构与Chern数条件，结合一般型曲面的模理论，对曲线上有理/椭圆曲线的存在性进行系统分析，并建立显式计数。

Result: 对于非常一般的Horikawa曲面，p_g≥5时仅有有限多条有理或椭圆曲线；第一类Horikawa曲面在p_g∈{3,4}时结论同样成立。文中给出了这些曲线的完整刻画与计数公式。

Conclusion: Horikawa曲面上的有理曲线与椭圆曲线在非常一般情形下是有限的，且可以精确计数，这推广了先前关于这类曲面曲线分布的结果。

Abstract: Horikawa surfaces are minimal complex algebraic surfaces of general type with minimal Chern slope, satisfying either $c_2=5c^2_1+36$ if $c_1^2$ is even, or $c_2=5c^2_1+30$ if $c_1^2$ is odd. We prove that very general Horikawa surfaces with $p_g\ge 5$ contain only finitely many rational or elliptic curves. Moreover, we provide an explicit characterization and count of these curves. Our results also apply to very general Horikawa surfaces of the first kind with $p_g\in \{3,4\}.$

</details>


### [160] [Fundamental forms and infinitesimal symmetries of projective varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08442)
*Jun-Muk Hwang,Qifeng Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文给出了射影簇Z⊂PV在一般点的基本形式维数对其线性自同构群维数的上界估计，并证明该上界恰好由Euler对称簇达到。作为副产品，确定了Euler对称簇的无穷小自同构李代数，并获得了关于保持基本形式同构类型的Euler对称簇特化的刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解射影簇的线性自同构群维数与其局部微分几何性质（基本形式）之间的关系，是射影几何中的一个基本问题。本文旨在建立这一联系，并刻画达到维数上界的簇的结构。

Method: 利用一般点处的基本形式（fundamental forms）来界定线性自同构群的维数上界，并通过分析达到该上界的条件，证明其充要条件是该簇射影等价于Euler对称簇。进一步计算Euler对称簇的无穷小自同构李代数，并研究其特化过程中的刚性性质。

Result: 得到了射影簇线性自同构群维数在基本形式维数下的上界估计；证明上界恰由Euler对称簇达到；计算出Euler对称簇的无穷小自同构李代数；证明了保持基本形式同构类型的Euler对称簇特化具有刚性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了射影簇线性自同构群维数与基本形式之间的联系，给出了精确的上界估计并完整刻画了等号成立的情形（Euler对称簇）。这一结果丰富了射影几何中关于自同构群与几何不变量关系的理论，也为后续研究射影簇的刚性提供了新工具。

Abstract: We give a bound on the dimension of the linear automorphism group of a projective variety $Z \subset \mathbb{P} V$ in terms of its fundamental forms at a general point. Moreover, we show that the bound is achieved precisely when $Z \subset \mathbb{P} V$ is projectively equivalent to an Euler-symmetric variety. As a by-product, we determine the Lie algebra of infinitesimal automorphisms of an Euler-symmetric variety and also obtain a rigidity result on the specialization of an Euler-symmetric variety preserving the isomorphism type of the fundamental forms.

</details>


### [161] [Tangent discontinuity in the oper stratification of de Rham moduli spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08450)
*Pengfei Huang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在秩2、亏格g≥4的任意光滑复射影曲线上，构造了Simpson微分叶状结构猜想的一个反例，证明了切平面沿横跨两个相邻层的全纯曲线不连续，从而叶状结构断言不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Simpson在稳定轨迹上建立了oper分层，其纤维为拉格朗日纤维，并猜想这些纤维是闭的且能拼成一个光滑叶状结构（即"叶状结构猜想"）。该猜想的验证对理解模空间M_dR(X,r)的几何结构具有重要意义，但长期悬而未决。

Method: 作者在秩2情形下进行构造和分析。关键策略是证明切平面在一条横跨两个相邻oper层的全纯曲线上不连续，从而直接否定叶状结构（可积性/光滑纤维丛结构）的存在性。该不连续性意味着oper分层的纤维不能光滑地拟合成叶状结构。

Result: 在任意亏格g≥4的曲线上，秩2的Simpson叶状结构猜想被否定，给出了明确的反例。不连续性源于oper分层相邻层之间的切平面跳跃。

Conclusion: Simpson的叶状结构猜想在秩2、亏格g≥4的情形下不成立。这一反例揭示了oper分层在稳定轨迹上的纤维结构比预期更为复杂，其几何特性和可积性问题需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth complex projective curve of genus $g$, and let $\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{dR}}(X,r)$ be the moduli space of flat bundles of rank $r$. Over the stable locus, Simpson showed the oper stratification with Lagrangian fibers and asked whether these fibers are closed and fit together into a smooth foliation. This question is often referred to as the foliation conjecture. In this paper, we give a counterexample to this conjecture in rank two on every curve of genus $g\geq4$. The main idea is to show that the tangent planes are discontinuous along a holomorphic curve crossing two adjacent strata, hence the foliation assertion fails.

</details>


### [162] [$E_1$-Degeneration for Irregular Hodge Filtrations on Deligne--Mumford Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08539)
*Haoxu Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了光滑分离的Deligne-Mumford栈在有理指数处的不规则Hodge滤过的E1退化性质，条件是其粗空间为拟射影。结论在任意良好栈紧化和NC有理栈紧化上都成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Esnault-Sabbah-Yu关于光滑射影簇的不规则Hodge滤过E1退化定理推广到更一般的Deligne-Mumford栈情形，从而使得滤过及其分次维度可以在任意紧化上计算。

Method: 利用Kontsevich复形的有限平坦下降（finite flat descent），将射影栈情形约化到Esnault-Sabbah-Yu关于光滑射影簇的已知定理。

Result: 证明了在光滑分离的DM栈（粗空间拟射影）上，不规则Hodge滤过在每个有理指数处都满足E1退化，且该结果在所有良好栈紧化和NC有理栈紧化上成立。

Conclusion: 不规则Hodge滤过及其分次维度可在任意良好栈紧化或NC有理栈紧化上计算，推广了射影簇情形的经典结果。

Abstract: Let $(\mathscr U,w)$ be a smooth separated Deligne--Mumford stack over $\mathbb{C}$. Assume that its coarse space is quasi-projective. We prove $E_1$-degeneration at every rational index for its irregular Hodge filtration. The result holds on every good stack compactification and every NC rational stack compactification. In particular, the filtration and its graded dimensions can be computed on any such compactification. The proof uses finite flat descent for Kontsevich complexes. It reduces the projective stack case to the theorem of Esnault--Sabbah--Yu for smooth projective varieties.

</details>


### [163] [A Torelli-type theorem for hyperkähler quotients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08680)
*Ryota Kotani*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了由四元数向量空间\mathbb{H}^n通过超恺勒商构造的代数渐近超恺勒结构的模空间，证明了在Kirwan映射满射假设下，周期映射是双射（Torelli型定理），从而将Kronheimer关于ALE引力瞬子的Torelli定理推广到了代数几何情形，适用于环面超恺勒簇和Nakajima箭图簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kronheimer于1989年证明了ALE引力瞬子的Torelli型定理，即周期映射的双射性。本文的动机是将这一经典微分几何结果推广到代数几何框架中，研究更一般的超恺勒商构造的代数渐近超恺勒结构，并建立相应的Torelli型定理。

Method: 本文研究了由四元数向量空间经超恺勒商得到的代数渐近超恺勒结构的模空间，在该模空间上考虑周期映射，并在假设Kirwan映射满射的条件下证明周期映射的双射性（Torelli型定理）。

Result: 证明了在Kirwan映射满射假设下周期映射的双射性，即Torelli型定理成立，并将该结果推广到环面超恺勒簇和Nakajima箭图簇上。

Conclusion: 该工作为超恺勒商的代数渐近结构的模空间提供了Torelli型定理，作为Kronheimer经典结果在代数几何层面的推广，在环面超恺勒簇和Nakajima箭图簇中均有应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the moduli space of algebraic asymptotic hyperkähler structures on hyperkähler quotients arising from a quaternionic vector space $\mathbb{H}^n$. We consider the period map on this moduli space, and prove a Torelli-type theorem (i.e., the bijectivity of the period map) assuming the surjectivity of the Kirwan map. This work provides an algebro-geometric generalization of the Torelli-type theorem for ALE gravitational instantons by Kronheimer (1989). In particular, this theorem applies to toric hyperkähler varieties and Nakajima quiver varieties.

</details>


### [164] [Essential p-dimension and Chern numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08877)
*Olivier Haution*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明：若光滑射影簇X有一个与p互素的陈类数，则有限p-群在X上的作用都通过GL_n(k)的一个子群因子，其中n=dim X。由此推导出对基域算术敏感的不动点定理，并给出Cremona群中循环p-子群阶数的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于有限p-群作用的不动点定理不依赖于基域的算术性质。本文希望建立一个对基域算术敏感的理论，并利用p-本质维数来转移有限p-群表示的性质到其在代数簇上的作用。

Method: 核心方法基于一个独立有趣的观察：对于特征零域上的仿射代数群G，ed_p(G)+dim G等于具有一般自由G-作用的光滑射影簇Y的最小维数，使得度映射CH_G(Y)→F_p非零。关键输入是Karpenko和Merkurjev关于p-群p-本质维数的结果。

Result: 证明了有限p-群在满足陈类数条件的光滑射影簇上的作用可分解通过GL_n(k)的子群；得到了对基域算术敏感的不动点定理；证明了Cr_n(Q)在p≥n+2时不包含阶为p²的元素。

Conclusion: 这项工作建立了本质p-维数与代数簇上p-群作用之间的新联系，使得可以将有限p-群表示论的性质系统地转移到其作用上，并推广了已知的不动点定理和Cremona群中元素阶数的界。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth, projective, geometrically connected variety over a field $k$ containing a root of unity of order $p$. If $X$ has a Chern number prime to $p$, we show that every action of a finite $p$-group on $X$ factors through a subgroup of $\operatorname{GL}_n(k)$, where $n=\dim X$. This allows one to transfer properties of representations of finite $p$-groups to their actions on $X$. We deduce a fixed-point theorem which, unlike previously known results of this kind, is sensitive to the arithmetic of the base field. We also obtain a bound on the orders of cyclic $p$-subgroups of the Cremona groups: for instance $\operatorname{Cr}_n(\mathbb{Q})$ contains no element of order $p^2$ when $p \ge n+2$.
  The method is based on the following observation, of independent interest. For an affine algebraic group $G$ over a field of characteristic zero, $\operatorname{ed}_p(G) + \dim G$ is the least dimension of a smooth projective variety $Y$ with a generically free $G$-action such that the degree map $\operatorname{CH}_G(Y) \to \mathbb{F}_p$ is nonzero.
  A key input for our result is Karpenko and Merkurjev's computation of the essential $p$-dimension of $p$-groups.

</details>


### [165] [The universal cover of the second-type locus of a cubic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08909)
*Frank Gounelas*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一般四次三维簇（cubic fourfold）上第二类直线的曲面（surface of second-type lines）的基本群阶为2，并指出其由Huybrechts构造的万有覆盖可通过沿每条第二类直线的Gauss映射的两个分歧点来获得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一般四次三维簇上第二类直线所构成的曲面的拓扑结构，特别是其基本群的性质，以期深入理解这一几何对象的代数拓扑本质。

Method: 作者通过分析Gauss映射在每条第二类直线上的两个分歧点，利用Huybrechts构造的万有覆盖来证明该曲面的基本群阶为2。

Result: 证明了该曲面的基本群为二阶群（即Z/2Z），其万有覆盖由两条分歧点构造给出。

Conclusion: 该结果为理解四次三维簇上第二类直线曲面的拓扑结构提供了精确的刻画，基本群阶为2意味着该曲面具有非平凡的有限覆盖。

Abstract: We prove that the surface of second-type lines on a general cubic fourfold has fundamental group of order two. Its universal cover, constructed by Huybrechts, is obtained by considering the two ramification points of the Gauss map along each second-type line.

</details>


### [166] [Geometric characterization of $p$-exceptional monomial GAPN functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08969)
*Masamichi Kuroda,Kentaro Mitsui*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文给出了p-例外单项式GAPN函数的几何刻画，通过建立有限域F_q[x,y]中无绝对不可约因子（定义在F_q上）的非零多项式的几何特征来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PN（奇特征）和APN（偶特征）函数是经典的高非线性函数类，GAPN函数将它们推广到所有特征p。此类函数分类是核心研究目标，而p-例外单项式PN和2-例外单项式APN函数已被完全分类，但对应的GAPN函数分类问题仍开放。

Method: 本文通过建立F_q[x,y]中非零多项式不含定义在F_q上的绝对不可约因子的几何刻画，以此作为工具，对p-例外单项式GAPN函数给出几何表征。

Result: 给出了p-例外单项式GAPN函数的几何特征刻画。

Conclusion: 该几何表征为解决p-例外GAPN函数分类这一开放问题提供了新的途径和工具。

Abstract: On finite fields of characteristic $p$, PN (perfect nonlinear) functions for odd $p$ and APN (almost perfect nonlinear) functions for even $p$ are well-known classes of highly nonlinear functions. GAPN (generalized almost perfect nonlinear) functions were introduced as a generalization of APN functions for even $p$ to all $p$. One of the main targets of studies on such highly nonlinear functions is their classification. While $p$-exceptional monomial PN and $2$-exceptional monomial APN functions have been classified, the corresponding problem for GAPN functions remains open. Here, a polynomial over $\mathbb{F}_p$ is called a $p$-exceptional PN (resp. APN, resp. GAPN) function if it is a PN (resp. APN, resp. GAPN) function on $\mathbb{F}_{p^n}$ for infinitely many positive integers $n$.
  In this paper, we give a geometric characterization of $p$-exceptional monomial GAPN functions. To this end, for a prime power $q$, we establish a geometric characterization of non-zero polynomials in $\mathbb{F}_{q}[x,y]$ having no absolutely irreducible factor defined over $\mathbb{F}_{q}$.

</details>


### [167] [Mori dream Jacobian elliptic surfaces of Kodaira dimension one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09002)
*Antonio Laface,Sichen Li,Jihao Liu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究复域上的Jacobian椭圆曲面X（Kodaira维数为1），在Mordell-Weil群有限且恰有一个I_n型可约纤维的条件下，证明了当n≤4χ时闭Mori锥是有理多面体，当n≤2χ+3时X是Mori dream曲面。同时构造了反例说明Kodaira维数1时MW群与自同构群有限不蕴含Mori锥的多面体性，并构造了各种Picard数的Mori dream曲面的例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Jacobian椭圆曲面在Kodaira维数为1时的Mori锥几何结构，特别是确定Mori dream曲面性质的精确临界条件，并厘清有限性条件（Mordell-Weil群、自同构群）与Mori锥多面体性之间的逻辑关系。

Method: 结合Mori锥的显式描述与Artin判别准则（应用于对偶nef射线）来证明Mori锥的多面体性；通过构造具体例子（如(χ,n)=(3,11)的曲面）来说明big且nef但不semiample的除子存在，从而否定Mori dream性质；利用显式构造证明任意Picard数下的Mori dream曲面存在性。

Result: 证明了当n≤4χ时闭Mori锥为有理多面体；当n≤2χ+3时X是Mori dream曲面。构造了(χ,n)=(3,11)、Picard数12的曲面，其中存在big且nef但不semiample的除子（故非Mori dream）。证明了Kodaira维数1时MW群与自同构群有限不蕴含Mori锥多面体性。对每个ρ≥2构造了Kodaira维数1、Picard数ρ的Mori dream曲面。

Conclusion: 对于具有有限Mordell-Weil群和单个I_n型可约纤维的Jacobian椭圆曲面，Mori锥的多面体性与Mori dream性质可由n与χ的比例关系精确刻画；有限性条件不足以确保Mori锥的多面体性，且任意Picard数的Mori dream曲面均可在该框架下构造。

Abstract: Let $π\colon X\to\mathbb P^1$ be a Jacobian elliptic surface over $\mathbb C$, and set $χ=χ(\mathcal O_X)\ge3$, so that $κ(X)=1$. Assume that the Mordell-Weil group of $π$ is finite and that $π$ has exactly one reducible fiber, of type $I_n$. We prove that the closed Mori cone of $X$ is rational polyhedral when $n\le4χ$, and that $X$ is a Mori dream surface when $n\le2χ+3$. The proof combines an explicit description of the Mori cone with Artin's criterion applied to the null loci of its dual nef rays. We also show that, in Kodaira dimension one, finiteness of both the Mordell-Weil group and the automorphism group does not imply polyhedrality of the Mori cone. In the polyhedral range, we construct a Jacobian elliptic surface with $(χ,n)=(3,11)$, Picard number $12$, and a big and nef divisor which is not semiample; in particular, this surface is not a Mori dream surface. Finally, for every integer $ρ\ge2$, we construct a Jacobian elliptic surface of Kodaira dimension one and Picard number $ρ$ that is a Mori dream surface.

</details>


### [168] [Line-filtered rank-two ACM bundles on abelian varieties of Picard rank one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09163)
*Soham Mondal*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文对复数阿贝尔簇A（维数g≥2，NS(A)≅Z，由ample类L生成）上的rank-2算术Cohen-Macaulay（ACM）丛进行了完全分类，这些丛具有空亏格（empty defect）。分类结果（模去L的幂次扭转）为：要么是两个ACM线丛的直和，要么是一个非平凡零度线丛的非分裂自扩张。此外，文中证明此类丛均不是Ulrich丛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACM丛在代数几何中具有重要地位，它们与代数簇的Grothendieck群、向量丛的稳定性以及Ulrich丛等问题密切相关。在阿贝尔簇这类特殊且重要的簇上，分类rank-2 ACM丛是理解其几何结构的关键步骤。此前关于ACM丛的分类工作主要集中在射影空间和若干其他簇上，而阿贝尔簇上的情形尚未被系统处理，特别是空亏格情形下的rank-2 ACM丛的分类仍是开放问题。

Method: 论文采用代数几何中的标准技术，包括对ACM丛的亏格（defect）概念进行分析，利用阿贝尔簇上NS(A)≅Z的特殊结构，结合线丛的扭转和自扩张理论进行分类。通过分析rank-2 ACM丛与线丛之间的关系，利用extension和self-extension的代数结构，以及ULrich丛的判别条件来得出分类结论。

Result: 主要结果为：(1) 在扭转为L的幂次的等价意义下，rank-2且空亏格（empty defect）的ACM丛只有两类：两个ACM线丛的直和，或者一个非平凡零度线丛的非分裂自扩张。(2) 不存在rank-2、空亏格且为Ulrich的ACM丛。

Conclusion: 该文给出了复数阿贝尔簇上rank-2空亏格ACM丛的完整分类，否定了此类丛为Ulrich丛的可能性，从而为阿贝尔簇上向量丛的研究提供了新的结构性认识。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a complex abelian variety of dimension $g\ge 2$ with $\NS(A)\cong\ZZ$ generated by an ample class $L$. We classify, up to twist by powers of $L$, the rank-two arithmetically Cohen--Macaulay bundles on $A$ with empty defect: they are sums of two ACM line bundles or nonsplit self-extensions of a nontrivial degree-zero line bundle. We also show none is Ulrich.

</details>


### [169] [Dynamical degrees of twisted rational maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09275)
*Marc Abboud,Junyi Xie*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了扭有理映射（twisted rational maps）这一相对代数动力学中的自然框架，将动力次数理论（dynamical degrees）推广到该框架下，证明了定义极限的存在性、与极化的无关性及双有理不变性，并识别了半共轭的相对动力次数与诱导扭有理映射动力次数之间的联系，证明了混合次数公式，以及扭仿射簇自同态第一动力次数的代数性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在相对代数动力学中，若一个有理自映射保持一个纤维化结构，则其在一般纤维上诱导的映射通常不是函数域上的普通有理自映射，而是扭有理映射。因此需要建立扭有理映射的理论框架来系统研究相对动力学，并推广动力次数这一衡量动力系统渐近复杂性的数值不变量。

Method: 本文采用多管齐下的方法：(1) 在扭有理映射框架下推广动力次数理论，证明定义极限存在且不依赖极化选择，并验证其双有理不变性；(2) 利用谱解释（spectral interpretation）将半共轭的相对动力次数与诱导扭有理映射的动力次数相关联，证明混合次数公式；(3) 结合动力次数的谱解释与无穷远赋值方法（valuative methods at infinity），研究扭仿射簇自同态的第一动力次数的代数性。

Result: (1) 成功在扭有理映射框架下建立了动力次数理论，证明其定义极限存在、独立于极化选择且为双有理不变量；(2) 将半共轭的相对动力次数识别为一般纤维上诱导扭有理映射的动力次数，并证明了相应的混合次数公式；(3) 利用谱解释和无穷远赋值方法，证明了扭仿射簇自同态第一动力次数的代数性结果。

Conclusion: 扭有理映射构成了研究相对动力学的自然且统一的框架。在该框架下，动力次数理论得以完整推广，并揭示了相对动力次数与扭映射动力次数之间的深层联系。混合次数公式和第一动力次数的代数性结果进一步深化了对相对动力学系统中渐近复杂性度量的理解。

Abstract: Twisted rational maps arise naturally in relative algebraic dynamics: if a rational self-map preserves a fibration, then the induced map on the generic fiber is usually not an ordinary rational self-map over the function field, but a twisted one. This suggests that twisted rational maps form a natural framework for studying relative dynamics. In this framework we extend the theory of dynamical degrees, the numerical invariants measuring the asymptotic complexity of a dynamical system: the defining limits exist, are independent of the choice of polarization, and are birational invariants. We also identify the relative dynamical degrees of a semi-conjugacy with the dynamical degrees of the induced twisted rational map on the generic fiber, and prove the corresponding mixed degree formula. Finally, using the spectral interpretation of dynamical degrees and valuative methods at infinity, we prove an algebraicity result for the first dynamical degree of twisted endomorphisms of affine varieties.

</details>


### [170] [K-stability of complete intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09293)
*Livia Campo,Kristin DeVleming,Tiago Duarte Guerreiro*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一般情况下Fano完全交点的K-多重稳定性，以及非射影空间或二次超曲面的一般Fano完全交点的K-稳定性，并推广到某些光滑加权完全交点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-稳定性是代数几何中与典则度量和模空间紧化密切相关的重要概念，证明Fano完全交点的K-稳定性（特别是任意多重度和维数的一般情况）是该领域长期未解决的核心问题。

Method: 通过建立一般Fano完全交点（包括任意多重度和维数）的K-稳定性理论，并利用类似的论证方法处理某些光滑加权完全交点的情况。

Result: 证明了：(1) 任意多重度和维数的一般Fano完全交点是K-多重稳定的；(2) 非同构于射影空间或二次超曲面的一般Fano完全交点是K-稳定的；(3) 类似结论对某些光滑加权完全交点也成立。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了此前关于Fano完全交点K-稳定性的已知结论，涵盖了更广泛的任意多重度和维数情形，并将结论扩展至加权完全交点，为Fano簇的K-稳定性理论提供了更完整的图景。

Abstract: We prove the K-polystability of the general Fano complete intersection of arbitrary multidegree and dimension, and the K-stability of the general Fano complete intersection that is not isomorphic to projective space or a quadric hypersurface. We prove analogous results for certain smooth weighted complete intersections.

</details>


### [171] [The hyperkähler period-index conjecture is false](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09436)
*Pieter Belmans,James Hotchkiss*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Huybrechts关于超Kähler簇上Brauer类的指数整除周期之幂的猜想在特定四维超Kähler簇（包括K3^{[2]}型和Kum^2型）上不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Huybrechts猜想是对通常period-index猜想的加强，断言对超Kähler簇X上的任意Brauer类α，指数ind(α)整除per(α)^{dim(X)/2}。验证或反驳这一强化的猜想对于理解超Kähler簇上的Brauer类算术性质具有重要意义。

Method: 本文通过在特定超Kähler四维簇上构造反例来证明该猜想失败。研究对象涵盖K3^{[2]}型和Kum^2型两类超Kähler四维簇，通过分析其Brauer类的指数与周期的关系来展示不整除性。

Result: 成功构造了反例，证明Huybrechts猜想在K3^{[2]}型和Kum^2型超Kähler四维簇上均不成立。

Conclusion: Huybrechts对period-index猜想的强化版本在超Kähler四维簇上不成立，表明该猜想的适用范围需要修正或限定。

Abstract: Huybrechts conjectured that for every Brauer class $α$ on a hyperkähler variety $X$ we have that $\mathop{\rm ind}(α)\mid\mathop{\rm per}(α)^{\dim(X)/2}$, strengthening the usual period-index conjecture. We show that this fails on certain hyperkähler fourfolds, both in type $\mathrm{K}3^{[2]}$ and $\mathrm{Kum}^2$.

</details>


### [172] [On the internal homotopy theory of motivic categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09592)
*Dipankar Maity*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个用于内部Eilenberg-MacLane对象、内部同伦群和内部覆盖空间的∞-范畴框架，并研究了它们在适当局部化下的行为。通过将该框架应用于普通motivic局部化，作者将内部n-Eilenberg-MacLane对象与强A¹-不变群层联系起来，证明了A¹-局部化诱导经典A¹-覆盖与内部motivic覆盖之间的等价，并证明了广义motivic Van Kampen定理。此外，文章建立了Nisnevich局部到整体的A¹-连通性判据，并展示了在双有理motivic同伦范畴中可恢复普通A¹-框架中缺失的essential topos性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在回答 motivic 同伦理论中的基础性问题：motivic 同伦范畴是否构成一个 ∞-topos（以及如果构成，满足哪些性质）。此前普通 A¹-局部化下 motivic 同伦范畴缺乏某些 topos 理论性质，本文试图通过建立内部（internal）版本的 Eilenberg-MacLane 对象和覆盖空间理论来形式化这一障碍，并通过 birational motivic 局部化恢复这些性质。

Method: 作者首先在一般 ∞-范畴中发展内部 Eilenberg-MacLane 对象、内部同伦群和内部覆盖空间的形式化框架，并研究其在局部化下的行为。然后将这些工具应用于两个局部化设置：(1) 普通 motivic 局部化——将内部 n-EM 对象等同于强 A¹-不变群层，并用其建立 A¹-局部化前后覆盖空间的等价以及广义 motivic Van Kampen 定理；(2) 双有理 motivic 局部化——证明其在有限个一般点情形下构成 Postnikov 完备、上同调维数为 0 的 ∞-topos。同时还建立了 Nisnevich 局部到整体的 A¹-连通性判据。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 将内部 n-Eilenberg-MacLane 对象与强 A¹-不变（n≥2 时为阿贝尔）群层等同，从而给出 motivic 同伦范畴非 ∞-topos 的形式障碍；(2) A¹-局部化诱导经典 A¹-覆盖与内部 motivic 覆盖之间的等价，推广了 motivic Van Kampen 定理；(3) Nisnevich 局部到整体 A¹-连通性判据；(4) 在一般 Qcqs 基上，birational motivic 同伦范畴恢复 ordinary A¹-设置中缺失的 essential topos 性质；(5) 对具有限个一般点的概形，birational motivic 同伦范畴是 Postnikov 完备、上同调维数 0 的 ∞-topos。

Conclusion: 本文通过建立内部无穷范畴框架，系统性地发展了 motivic 同伦理论中的内部结构理论，既揭示了普通 A¹-motivic 同伦范畴与 ∞-topos 之间的本质差距（通过形式障碍），又证明了通过双有理局部化可以恢复 topos 性质。这项工作为理解 motivic 同伦范畴的结构性质提供了新的理论工具体系，并为后续研究广义 motivic Van Kampen 定理和连通性判据提供了统一视角。

Abstract: We develop a general $\infty$-categorical framework for internal Eilenberg-MacLane objects, internal homotopy groups, and an internal notion of covering spaces, and study their behavior under suitable localizations. Applying this to the ordinary motivic localization, we identify internal $n$-Eilenberg-MacLane objects with strongly $\mathbb{A}^1$-invariant sheaves of (abelian when $n\geq 2$) groups, yielding a formal obstruction to the motivic homotopy category being an $\infty$-topos. We prove that taking $\mathbb{A}^1$-localizations induces an equivalence between classical $\mathbb{A}^1$-coverings of a Nisnevich local space and the internal motivic coverings of its motivic localization, providing a streamlined proof of a generalized motivic Van Kampen theorem. Along the way, we establish a Nisnevich-local-to-global $\mathbb{A}^1$-connectivity criterion: a $k$-scheme is $\mathbb{A}^1$-connected if and only if it admits a Nisnevich cover by $\mathbb{A}^1$-connected schemes such that each pairwise intersection has a ($k$-)point. Finally, we show that over a general Qcqs base, passing to the birational motivic homotopy category recovers certain essential topos-theoretic properties absent in the ordinary $\mathbb{A}^1$-setting. In fact, for a scheme with finitely many generic points, we show that the birational motivic homotopy category is a Postnikov-complete $\infty$-topos of cohomological dimension $0$.

</details>


### [173] [Foliated Minimal Models and Flops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09663)
*Paolo Cascini,Roktim Mascharak,Calum Spicer*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了叶状结构（foliations）的极小模型和flop，证明了正规射影Q-因子簇上具有典范奇点的秩一叶状结构的K_F-MMP输出彼此同构；对三维簇上余秩一叶状结构，在klt和F-dlt条件下证明了D-flop的存在性；同时构造反例展示秩一叶状结构在flop收缩和典范模型方面存在经典MMP所没有的病态行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MMP理论（极小模型纲领）已在代数簇上取得了系统性的成果，但叶状结构的MMP理论尚不完善，特别是关于flop的唯一性、存在性以及典范模型的性质等核心问题仍待解决。本文旨在系统研究叶状结构的极小模型与flop，揭示其与经典MMP的异同。

Method: 本文采用双有理几何与叶状结构理论的工具。具体而言：（1）对于秩一叶状结构，利用K_F的伪有效性和典范奇点的性质，通过分析flop收缩与典范模型的关系证明任意两个MMP输出同构。（2）对三维簇上的余秩一叶状结构，在klt与F-dlt条件下建立D-flop的存在性定理，可能依赖于奇点分类与局部环的分析。（3）通过构造具体反例展示秩一叶状结构可出现flop收缩无D-flop、nef且大（big）的典范层不产生典范模型等病态现象。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）秩一叶状结构在K_F伪有效且具典范奇点时，K_F-MMP的任意两个输出同构；（2）三维簇上余秩一叶状结构在klt及附加条件下的F-dlt情形中D-flop存在；（3）构造反例表明秩一叶状结构具有经典MMP所不具有的病态：flop收缩不必存在D-flop，nef且big的典范除子也不必给出典范模型（即使在代数空间的范畴中亦然）。

Conclusion: 本文推进了叶状结构MMP理论的发展：一方面在广泛条件下建立了flop唯一性（秩一情形）和D-flop存在性（余秩一、三维情形）；另一方面通过反例清楚地刻画了叶状结构MMP与经典MMP的本质差异，表明叶状结构的flop理论需要处理经典情形中不存在的额外障碍，这为后续研究指明了新的方向。

Abstract: We study minimal models and flops for foliations. We show that if $\mathcal F$ is a rank one foliation with canonical singularities on a normal projective $\mathbb Q$-factorial variety and $K_{\mathcal F}$ is pseudo-effective, then any two outputs of the $K_{\mathcal F}$-MMP are isomorphic. For co-rank one foliations on threefolds, we prove existence results for $D$-flops in the klt setting and, under additional hypotheses, in the F-dlt setting. By contrast, we construct examples showing that rank one foliations display pathologies absent from the classical MMP: flopping contractions need not admit $D$-flops, and nef and big canonical divisors need not give rise to canonical models, even in the category of algebraic spaces.

</details>


### [174] [The rank of the $5\times 5$ permanent tensor is sixteen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09708)
*Jong In Han,Jeyoung Song*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了5×5永久张量在复数域及其子域上的张量秩至少为16，与已知的上界匹配，从而完全确定了该张量的张量秩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5×5永久张量的张量秩此前仅知其介于15和16之间，本文旨在精确确定其张量秩，解决该开放问题。

Method: 利用高阶Koszul展平（higher-order Koszul flattening）和Glynn公式等技术，证明了5×5永久张量的张量秩下界为16，与已知上界吻合。

Result: 证明了5×5永久张量的张量秩在复数域及其子域上至少为16，与已知上界16匹配，从而精确确定了该张量的张量秩为16。

Conclusion: 该结果完全解决了5×5永久张量张量秩的确定问题，并表明该张量的张量秩恰等于其Waring秩（均为16）。

Abstract: In this paper, we show that the tensor rank of the $5\times 5$ permanent tensor is at least $16$ over $\mathbb{C}$ and its subfields, which matches the known upper bound. The $5\times 5$ permanent tensor is a symmetric tensor corresponding to the monomial $x_1x_2x_3x_4x_5$ whose Waring rank is known to be $16$. Previously, it was known that its tensor rank is either $15$ or $16$ by the higher-order Koszul flattening and Glynn's formula.

</details>


### [175] [Abstract Six-Functor Formalisms: Extension to Ind- and Pro- Categories and Functorial Cohomological Purity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09726)
*Chirantan Chowdhury*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了抽象六函子形式体系的两个推论：首先证明抽象六函子形式体系可以推广到特定Ind-和Pro-范畴的几何构造上，从而为ind-pro代数叠（如Hecke叠）定义动机稳定同伦理论；其次利用Liu-Zheng发展的多单纯语言证明上同调纯性（Cohomological Purity）具有函子性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抽象六函子形式体系是代数几何中处理对偶性、紧合基变换等基本工具的系统化框架。作者希望将其扩展到更广泛的几何对象（ind-pro代数叠），并进一步深化对Cohomological Purity这一重要性质的理解。

Method: 本文采用两条技术路线：(1) 通过构造将抽象六函子形式体系延拓到Ind-和Pro-范畴的方法，得到ind-pro代数叠上的动机稳定同伦理论；(2) 利用Liu-Zheng发展的多单纯（multisimplicial）语言来证明Cohomological Purity的函子性。

Result: 实现了抽象六函子形式体系在Ind/Pro范畴上的延拓，从而为Hecke叠等ind-pro代数叠定义了动机稳定同伦理论；并证明Cohomological Purity在函子性意义下成立。

Conclusion: 抽象六函子形式体系具有广泛的适用范围，可推广至更一般的几何构造；Cohomological Purity的函子性为相关理论提供了更深的结构性质。

Abstract: In this article, we study two consequences of abstract six-functor formalisms. Firstly, we show that an abstract six-functor formalism can be extended to specific Ind- and Pro- categories of geometric setups. As an application, we can define the motivic stable homotopy theory for ind-pro-algebraic stacks such as the Hecke stack. Secondly, we also show that Cohomological Purity is functorial using the multisimplicial language developed by Liu-Zheng.

</details>


### [176] [Varieties with prescribed fundamental group schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09757)
*Lingguang Li,Hao Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Tannakian基本群概形的实现问题：给定一个仿射k-群概形G，何时存在一个光滑投影连通带点k-簇(X,x)使其基本群概形同构于G。通过建立主丛相关的基本群概形精确序列，并结合Godeaux–Serre构造和Lefschetz型定理，证明了每个有限平展k-群概形可实现为光滑投影簇的S-、Nori和扩展Nori基本群概形，每个有限常数群概形可实现为其F-和平展变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tannakian基本群概形的实现问题是代数几何中的一个基本问题，即判断哪些仿射k-群概形可以作为光滑投影簇的基本群概形出现。这一问题将代数群理论、Tannakian形式主义与代数簇的拓扑性质联系起来，具有重要的理论意义。

Method: 本文的方法主要包括：(1) 建立主丛相关的S-、Nori和扩展Nori基本群概形的精确序列；(2) 利用Godeaux–Serre构造将给定的群概形实现为某个簇的基本群；(3) 应用Lefschetz型定理来保持或控制基本群概形在超平面截面下的行为。

Result: 主要结果为：每个有限平展k-群概形都可以实现为光滑投影簇的S-、Nori和扩展Nori基本群概形；每个有限常数群概形可以实现为F-和平展变体的基本群概形。这为基本群概形的实现问题提供了完整的有限情形答案。

Conclusion: 本文通过建立精确序列并综合运用Godeaux–Serre构造和Lefschetz型定理，系统解决了有限平展群概形和有限常数群概形作为各类基本群概形的实现问题，推广和深化了此前关于基本群概形实现问题的已知结果。

Abstract: We study the realization problem for Tannakian fundamental group schemes: given an affine $k$-group scheme $G$, when does there exist a smooth projective connected pointed $k$-variety $(X,x)$ whose fundamental group scheme is isomorphic to $G$? We establish exact sequences for fundamental group schemes associated with principal bundles, and combine them with a Godeaux--Serre construction and Lefschetz-type theorems. As applications, we show that every finite étale $k$-group scheme is realized as the $S$-, Nori, and extended Nori fundamental group scheme of a smooth projective variety, while every finite constant group scheme is realized as the $F$- and étale variants.11

</details>


### [177] [Grothendieck weights and K-theoretic positivity for matroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09844)
*Yiyu Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文通过热带几何空间的拓扑引入了研究排列体环面簇上K-理论正性的方法，核心工具是作者发展的Grothendieck权理论。证明了两个正性结果：一是任意拟阵关联的扭结线束切丛的欧拉示性数正性，二是将Berget-Fink的外部活动复形从拟阵对推广到任意无公共环的拟阵元组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望建立K-理论正性的新判据，通过热带几何中的拓扑工具来研究排列体环面簇上的正性性质，并提出猜想性消没定理的数值证据，同时推广已有的外部活动复形理论。

Method: 利用Grothendieck权理论与热带几何空间的拓扑性质相结合，计算K-理论意义上的正性。通过将外部活动复形推广到任意拟阵元组，结合Eur-Fink-Larson的组合几何消没定理证明Cohen-Macaulay性。

Result: 证明了任意拟阵的扭结线束切丛欧拉示性数的正性；给出了外部活动复形的分级K-多项式公式（以外幂对偶拓扑商类表示），验证了系数交替符号性，并证明了每个复形的Cohen-Macaulay性（即使对拟阵对也是新证明），还部分回答了Berget-Eur-Spink-Tseng关于Chern数作为面计数的问题。

Conclusion: 该方法为K-理论正性研究提供了新工具，同时推广了外部活动复形理论并得到其强结构性质，对组合几何与代数几何交叉领域有重要贡献。

Abstract: We introduce a method for studying $K$-theoretic positivity on permutohedral toric varieties through the topology of spaces arising in tropical geometry. The key ingredient is the theory of Grothendieck weights developed by the author.
  We prove two positivity results using this method. The first result is the positivity of the Euler characteristics of tautological bundles associated with an arbitrary matroid and twisted by a nef line bundle. This gives numerical evidence for a conjectural vanishing theorem.
  The second result generalizes the external activity complex of Berget--Fink, originally defined for a pair of matroids, to the case of any tuple of matroids with no common loop. We deduce a formula for its graded $K$-polynomial in terms of exterior powers of the dual tautological quotient classes of the matroids. After a change of variables, its coefficients alternate in sign. We also prove the Cohen--Macaulayness of each such complex using the vanishing theorems for combinatorial geometries developed by Eur--Fink--Larson. This proof is new even in the case of a pair of matroids. As an application, we interpret certain Chern numbers of tautological quotient classes as counts of facets, partially answering a question of Berget--Eur--Spink--Tseng.

</details>


### [178] [Effective Results about Foliations on Smooth Projective Complete Intersection Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09877)
*Jorge Olivares,Daniel Posada-Buriticá*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究射影空间上保持光滑完全交集曲面M不变的全纯叶状结构，确定了此类叶状结构存在的精确次数条件，并由此得到经典Poincaré问题的新界，证明了此前关于P³中光滑超曲面的界是最优的。此外，对于孤立奇点的叶状结构且次数超过显式界时，证明了其截面的奇异闭包关系仅由切丛的全局自同态决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Poincaré问题关注代数叶状结构的次数界，此前仅对光滑超曲面情形有已知结果。本文希望将研究推广到更一般的完全交集曲面，确定叶状结构存在的精确条件，并检验已有界的紧致性（最优性）。

Method: 通过对射影空间上保持光滑完全交集曲面不变的叶状结构进行系统分类，利用代数几何与叶状结构理论中的工具，精确刻画叶状结构存在的次数条件；进而将所得结果应用于Poincaré问题，建立新的次数界，并通过构造反例证明已有超曲面情形的界为最优。对于高阶次数情形，利用切丛的全局自同态来刻画截面奇点闭包的包含关系。

Result: 确定了光滑完全交集曲面上保持不变的叶状结构存在的精确度数集合；获得了Poincaré问题的新次数界；证明了P³中光滑超曲面的已知界是最优的；证明了对孤立奇点叶状结构且次数超过显式界时，奇异闭包包含关系s'当且仅当s'=φ(s)，其中φ为切丛的全局自同态。

Conclusion: 本文将Poincaré问题的研究从光滑超曲面推广到了光滑完全交集曲面，给出了精确的次数存在性条件，并同时证明了已有界的最优性和高阶情形下奇点结构的刚性刻画，为该领域提供了新的理论工具与边界结果。

Abstract: We study holomorphic foliations on projective spaces that leave smooth complete intersection surfaces $M$ invariant. We determine precisely for which degrees such foliations on $M$ exist. As a consequence, we obtain new bounds for the classical Poincaré problem for smooth projective complete intersection surfaces and prove that previously known bounds for smooth hypersurfaces in $\mathbb{P}^3$ are optimal. Furthermore, for a foliation $[s]$ on $M$ with isolated singularities and for degrees beyond an explicit bound, we show that a section $s'$ has singular scheme containing that of $s$ if and only if $s'=φ(s)$ for some global endomorphism $φ$ of the tangent bundle of $M$.

</details>


### [179] [A no-go theorem for special Ulrich bundles, with a complement on primary Burniat surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09901)
*Cristian Anghel,Filip Chindea*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在光滑射影曲面（p_g=0）上，对于满足特定条件的极化H，从扩展0→O_X(H+K_X)→E→O_X(2H)⊗I_Z→0出发构造的秩二丛E不可能存在（no-go定理）。随后研究了主Burniat曲面，证明其所有无基点的丰沛除子都是非特殊的，因此每个极化都承载稳定的特殊Ulrich秩二丛，曲面关于每个极化都是严格Ulrich wild的。还定位了沿K_X的三个数值射线的特殊丰沛类，并分析了扭核构造变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究秩二Ulrich丛在特殊极化下的存在性问题，澄清Cayley-Bacharach构造中极化的非特殊性是必要条件而非仅仅方便条件。同时系统研究主Burniat曲面上的Ulrich丛分类与构造。

Method: 采用Cayley-Bacharach扩展构造方法建立no-go定理，利用主Burniat曲面的几何性质（p_g=0、K_X的数值特性）证明所有无基点丰沛除子的非特殊性，通过数值射线定位特殊丰沛类，并构造显式族及扭核变体分析。

Result: 在一般光滑射影曲面上证明了特殊极化下Cayley-Bacharach构造的no-go定理；在主Burniat曲面上刻画了特殊丰沛类的完整结构，证明了每个极化承载稳定特殊Ulrich秩二丛且曲面关于每个极化严格Ulrich wild；沿三条数值射线给出了显式实例；无特殊性证明的度界与Y. Cho的近期工作有重叠，但全局结论与no-go定理是独立的。

Conclusion: 特殊极化的Cayley-Bacharach构造在一般条件下不成立，非特殊性是关键必要条件；主Burniat曲面在Ulrich丛理论中具有极其丰富的结构，每个极化下都是严格Ulrich wild的。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth projective surface with $p_g=0$, and let $H$ be an ample divisor with $h^0(\mathcal{O}_X(H))\neq0$, $χ(\mathcal{O}_X(H))\ge q$, and $h^1(\mathcal{O}_X(H))\neq0$. We prove that no rank two bundle $\mathcal{E}$ with $c_1(\mathcal{E})=3H+K_X$, with the Ulrich value of $c_2(\mathcal{E})$, and satisfying $h^0(\mathcal{E}(-H))=0$, can arise from an extension $0 \to \mathcal{O}_X(H+K_X) \to \mathcal{E} \to \mathcal{O}_X(2H)\otimes\mathcal{I}_Z \to 0$. Thus, for this natural Cayley-Bacharach construction, non-speciality of the polarization is necessary rather than merely convenient.
  We then study primary Burniat surfaces. We show that every ample and base point free divisor is non-special; consequently, every polarization carries a stable special Ulrich bundle of rank two, and the surface is strictly Ulrich wild with respect to every polarization. We also locate the special ample classes on three numerical rays through $K_X$, compute explicit families on these rays, and analyze a twisted-kernel variant of the construction. The degree bound underlying the non-speciality result overlaps with recent work of Y. Cho, while the global consequences and the no-go theorem are independent.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [Preferential Attachment as a Simpliciality-Enforcing Mechanism in Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09788)
*Jason LaRuez,Brendan Rooney*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个广义优先连接超图模型，其中超边大小和每步新增节点数均来自任意分布，并通过平均场近似主方程方法解析推导出超度分布服从幂律，其指数仅取决于这两个随机变量期望值之比。该模型可通过向后步进法直接从时间戳超图数据中拟合，无需参数假设。对八个真实数据集应用非线性扩展模型后，发现单纯性分数随优先连接强度单调增加直至凝胶化转变，确立了优先连接是促进单纯性结构形成的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图能够直接建模任意规模的多体交互，真实系统中普遍存在高"单纯性"（即超边子集也倾向作为超边出现）的现象，但产生这种结构的生成机制尚不清楚，现有模型缺乏对此的解释能力。

Method: 提出了广义优先连接超图模型，其中超边大小Y_t和每步新增节点数X_t均从任意分布中抽取；利用平均场近似主方程方法解析推导稳态超度分布的幂律指数仅取决于p=E[X_t]/E[Y_t]；提出了向后步进法直接从时间戳超图数据估计X_t和Y_t；进一步引入非线性扩展模型并应用于八个真实超图数据集。

Result: 解析推导表明稳态超度分布遵循幂律，其指数仅依赖于X_t和Y_t期望值之比p，与底层分布形状无关；对八个真实数据集的分析发现，单纯性分数随优先连接强度α单调增加，直至α>1处的凝胶化转变。

Conclusion: 优先连接是超图单纯性结构的促进机制，本文模型提供了无需参数假设的、可直接从数据拟合的超图生成框架，为理解真实高维网络中多体交互的涌现结构提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Higher-order networks, represented as hypergraphs, enable direct modeling of multi-body interactions of arbitrary size. Hypergraph representations of real-world systems have been observed to exhibit high \emph{simpliciality} --- the tendency for subsets of hyperedges to also appear as hyperedges --- yet the generative mechanisms responsible for this structure are poorly understood. We introduce a generalized preferential attachment hypergraph model in which both hyperedge size $Y_t$ and the number of new nodes per step $X_t$ are drawn from arbitrary distributions, and derive analytically, using a mean-field approximate master equation approach, that the stationary hyperdegree distribution follows a power law whose exponent depends only on the ratio $p = E[X_t]/E[Y_t]$, independent of the shapes of the underlying distributions. Crucially, both $X_t$ and $Y_t$ can be estimated directly from any timestamped hypergraph dataset via a backward-stepping procedure, enabling the model to be fit without parametric assumptions. Applying a nonlinear extension of the model to eight real-world hypergraph datasets, we find that the simplicial fraction increases monotonically with the strength of preferential attachment up to the gelation transition at $α> 1$, establishing preferential attachment as a simpliciality-enforcing mechanism.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Celephais: efficient spectral initial data code for precessing compact binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07653)
*Hao-Jui Kuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为 Celephais 的紧凑天体初始数据构建代码，基于 Kadath 谱库，无需施加赤道对称性即可构建双中子星和黑洞-中子星的初始数据。通过利用全局多域 Jacobian 的稀疏结构和场的近似宇称分离，采用 MUMPS 分解矩阵并作为刷新右预处理子用于 Jacobian-free Newton-Krylov 迭代，避免了稠密存储。自适应 hp 细化方案在质量比为 20 的黑洞-中子星基准测试中，以约三分之一的未知量恢复均匀网格的约束精度。研究还将 post-Newtonian 力平衡估计扩展到任意自旋取向，并完成了包括进动双中子星偏心率降低测试和全演化验证。
