### [214] [Non-semisimple open-closed 3d TFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08057)
*Ingo Runkel,Yilong Wang*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文基于球面有限张量范畴（无需半单性）及其投射理想上的双边修正迹，通过通用构造定义了一个取值于向量空间的开闭三维拓扑场论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已有的闭三维拓扑场论推广到带自由边界和着色图的开闭情形，从而在非半单球面有限张量范畴的框架下建立更一般的拓扑不变量。

Method: 从多手柄体不变量和色映射出发构造新的三维流形不变量，再通过通用构造（universal construction）得到开闭三维拓扑场论。该理论的态射范畴由带角的三维边界的配边构成，边界划分为胶合边界和自由边界，自由边界配备满足可容许性条件的C-着色图。

Result: 建立了取值于向量空间的开闭三维拓扑场论，其构造基于非半单球面有限张量范畴上的双边修正迹，推广了以往依赖于半单范畴和单侧修正迹的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为在非半单张量范畴框架下系统地构造三维开闭拓扑场论提供了新的途径，将多手柄体不变量与色映射相结合，拓展了三维拓扑量子场论的适用范围。

Abstract: Given a spherical finite tensor category C, not necessarily semisimple, and a two-sided modified trace on its projective ideal, we define an open-closed three-dimensional topological field theory with values in vector spaces. The bordism category has as morphisms three-dimensional bordisms with corners, whose boundary is partitioned into the gluing boundary, parametrised by source and target surface, and the unparametrised free boundary. The free boundary is equipped with an embedded C-coloured graph satisfying an admissibility condition. Our construction starts from a new three-manifold invariant based on the multi-handlebody invariant of [arXiv:1809.07991] and the chromatic maps of [arXiv:2302.04509]. The open-closed topological field theory is then obtained via the universal construction.

</details>


### [215] [Generalized Quantum Minors Generate Quantized Coordinate Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08234)
*Ayan Dey*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文解决了量子坐标环O_q(G)在G为F_4型时的量子聚类代数结构问题，通过统一论证覆盖G_2、F_4和E_8三种情形。核心方法是利用拟负小晶体（quasi-minuscule crystal）的组合学，替代已有E_8型证明中依赖Lusztig典范基的方法，从而证明O_q(F_4)也具有量子聚类代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Oya、Qin和Yakimov已证明除F_4型外所有单连通单复代数群G的量子坐标环O_q(G)由广义量子小生成元生成并具有量子聚类代数结构。F_4型是该结果的最后一个缺口，需要专门解决以完成这一完整分类。

Method: 作者采用统一论证方法，同时对G_2、F_4和E_8三种例外型进行处理。核心策略是"引导"（bootstrap）已有的E_8型证明框架，但其关键部分原本依赖Lusztig量子伴随表示的典范基，作者将其替换为拟负小晶体（quasi-minuscule crystal）的组合学工具。

Result: 成功证明了O_q(F_4)也由广义量子小生成元生成，因此同样具有量子聚类代数结构，弥补了F_4型这一最后的空缺。

Conclusion: 至此，对于所有单连通单复代数群G（包括例外型G_2、F_4、E_8），量子坐标环O_q(G)均具有量子聚类代数结构，完整解决了该领域的分类问题。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a simply connected simple complex algebraic group. It is proved by Oya, Qin, and Yakimov \cite{OQY} that the quantized coordinate ring $\mathcal{O}_q(G)$ is generated by generalized quantum minors, and therefore carries a quantized cluster algebra structure, for all $G$ but type $F_4$. In this article, we settle the $F_4$ case by an argument uniform across $G_2$, $F_4$, and $E_8$. The main idea is to bootstrap the existing proof in type $E_8$, which relies on Lusztig's canonical basis of the quantum adjoint representation, and replace it with the combinatorics of the quasi-minuscule crystal. Consequently, $\mathcal{O}_q(F_4)$ also carries a quantized cluster algebra structure.

</details>


### [216] [Coherent sheaves on projective spaces and a lifting of the integral form of the Cartan subalgebra for quantum sl(2)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09711)
*Mikhail Khovanov*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文通过射影空间上的等变相干层范畴，对量子sl(2)的Cartan子代数积分形式的提升进行了范畴化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子群的范畴化是量子拓扑和表示论中的一个核心方向。对量子sl(2)的Cartan子代数进行范畴化，有助于理解量子群的深层结构及其在低维拓扑中的应用。

Method: 利用射影空间上的等变相干层范畴来构造范畴化。具体而言，通过等变相干层的K-理论或其他范畴层面的不变量，实现对量子sl(2) Cartan子代数积分形式提升的范畴化描述。

Result: 成功构造了量子sl(2) Cartan子代数积分形式提升的范畴化，通过射影空间上的等变相干层范畴实现。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子sl(2)的Cartan子代数提供了新的范畴化框架，深化了量子群与代数几何之间的联系。

Abstract: We categorify a lifting of the integral form of the Cartan subalgebra for quantum sl(2), via categories of equivariant coherent sheaves on projective spaces.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [GIM and Elliptic Lie algebras via Ringel--Hall Lie algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07877)
*Changjian Fu,Zhanhong Liang,Ming Lu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为对称化广义交矩阵(GIM)构造了带对合的无圈带值箭图，通过轨道范畴构造2-周期三角范畴，证明GIM代数同构于积分Ringel-Hall李代数，并将其应用于四类椭圆李代数，建立到Ringel-Hall李代数的满态射并验证实根空间上的单射性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义交矩阵(GIM)代数与Ringel-Hall李代数之间的对应关系是表示论中的核心问题。已有方法（如Peng-Xiao构造）需要合适的2-周期三角范畴作为桥梁，但如何为任意对称化GIM系统地构造这样的范畴，以及如何将其应用于椭圆李代数，此前尚不清楚。本文旨在提供一种统一的构造方案，并为椭圆型李代数建立与Ringel-Hall李代数之间的联系。

Method: (1) 对任意对称化GIM矩阵C，构造无圈的带值箭图(Q,d)并赋予对合θ；(2) 考虑有界导出范畴D的轨道范畴D/(θ∘Σ)，证明其具有典范三角结构且为2-周期的；(3) 应用Peng-Xiao构造，证明gim(C)同构于该轨道范畴对应的积分Ringel-Hall李代数；(4) 对四类椭圆Dynkin图（D4^(1,1)、E6^(1,1)、E7^(1,1)、E8^(1,1)），通过商去适当的箭图得到有限维代数A，构建对应的2-周期三角范畴及Ringel-Hall李代数，建立从椭圆李代数到积分Ringel-Hall李代数的满同态。

Result: 证明了任意对称化GIM代数gim(C)与由轨道范畴D/(θ∘Σ)构造的积分Ringel-Hall李代数同构。对四类椭圆李代数，建立了从椭圆李代数到其积分Ringel-Hall李代数的满态射，并确认了该映射在实根空间上的单射性。全空间单射性作为猜想提出。

Conclusion: 该工作构建了GIM代数与Ringel-Hall李代数之间的统一桥梁，为理解任意对称化GIM代数提供了新的几何-范畴论视角。对椭圆型李代数的研究进一步验证了这一框架的有效性，并留下全空间单射性作为待解决问题，暗示了更深层的结构对应关系。

Abstract: For any symmetrizable generalized intersection matrix (GIM) $C$, we construct an acyclic valued quiver $(Q,\mathbf{d})$ endowed with an involution $θ$. Let $\mathcal{D}$ be the bounded derived category of finite-dimensional representations of $(Q,\mathbf{d})$, and let $Σ$ stand for the suspension functor of $\mathcal{D}$. We show that the orbit category $\mathcal{D}/(θ\circΣ)$ carries a canonical triangulated structure and is $2$-periodic. Applying Peng--Xiao's construction to this orbit category, we prove that the GIM algebra $\operatorname{gim}(C)$ is isomorphic to the integral Ringel--Hall Lie algebra associated with $\mathcal{D}/(θ\circΣ)$.
  As a further application of the above machinery, we investigate elliptic Lie algebras of types $D_4^{(1,1)}$, $E_6^{(1,1)}$, $E_7^{(1,1)}$ and $E_8^{(1,1)}$. For each elliptic Dynkin diagram, we define a finite-dimensional algebra $A$ by taking an appropriate quotient of the acyclic quiver $Q$ attached to the GIM matrix $C$. From the resulting $2$-periodic triangulated categories, we build the corresponding Ringel--Hall Lie algebras, and establish a surjective Lie algebra homomorphism from each elliptic Lie algebra to its integral Ringel--Hall counterpart. This map is conjectured to be injective, and its injectivity on real root spaces is confirmed.

</details>


### [218] [Transfer of abelian model structures to equivariant categories and homotopy squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08141)
*Zhenxing Di,Liping Li,Li Liang,Guoliang Tang,Rongmin Zhu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限群G作用于具有足够投对象的Grothendieck范畴A（且|G|在A中可逆），证明了从A到其等变范畴A^G的abelian模型结构的提升定理，并建立了相应同伦范畴之间的（至多retract意义下的）三角等价。此外构造了一个交换方块，其水平函子为三角等价、垂直比较函子为至多retract意义的三角等价，该方块联系了提升后的等变模型范畴上的导出函子与原同伦范畴上导出函子的等变化。在模范畴背景下，用PGF Hovey三元组加以说明，并将其应用于由Frobenius双模和伴型稳定等价诱导的同伦方块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在表示论与同调代数中，群作用下的等变范畴及其模型结构具有重要地位。作者希望建立从一般Grothendieck范畴到等变范畴的模型结构提升一般性定理，并进一步建立导出函子之间的交换性关系（模去retract等价），从而统一并推广相关领域中的具体构造。

Method: 利用有限群G作用（阶数可逆）在Grothendieck范畴上的结构，通过粘合/提升abelian模型结构到等变范畴A^G；借助retract意义下的三角等价建立同伦范畴间的比较；构造四边形的交换图，水平方向为三角等价、竖直方向为retract意义下的三角等价，以建立等变导出函子与导出函子等变化之间的联系。

Result: 证明了从A到A^G的abelian模型结构的提升定理，建立了同伦范畴间的（至多retract）三角等价；构造了联系等变模型范畴导出函子与原导出函子等变化的交换方块。在模范畴情形，以PGF Hovey三元组作为实例说明，并应用于Frobenius双模和伴型稳定等价诱导的同伦方块。

Conclusion: 该定理为有限群作用下的Grothendieck范畴提供了一套统一的模型结构提升与导出函子比较框架，并在模范畴的PGF理论及稳定等价等经典主题中得到了具体应用，具有广泛的理论推广价值。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a finite group acting on a Grothendieck category $\mathcal{A}$ with enough projectives, such that $|G|$ is invertible in $\mathcal{A}$. We prove a general lifting theorem for abelian model structures from $\mathcal{A}$ to its equivariant category $\mathcal{A}^G$, and establish a triangle equivalence up to retracts between the corresponding homotopy categories. We also construct a commutative square whose horizontal functors are triangle equivalences and whose vertical comparison functors are triangle equivalences up to retracts. This square relates derived functors on the lifted equivariant model categories to the equivariantizations of the derived functors on the original homotopy categories. In the module category setting, we illustrate the above results using the PGF Hovey triples, and apply them to homotopy squares induced by a Frobenius bimodule and by a stable equivalence of adjoint type.

</details>


### [219] [Enumerating iterated tilted algebras in type $A$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08222)
*Alexander E. Black,Jonathan E. Gordon,Ray Maresca*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了A_n型迭代倾斜代数的同构类与凸n+1边形上非交叉生成树（模旋转）之间存在一一对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代倾斜代数（iterated tilted algebras）是表示论中的重要研究对象，但对其同构分类问题一直缺乏系统的组合刻画。本文旨在通过建立迭代倾斜代数与组合对象之间的对应关系，为这类代数的分类提供新的视角。

Method: 在迭代倾斜代数的同构类与奇异集（exceptional sets）的Hom-Ext箭图同构类之间建立联系，进而利用非交叉生成树（non-crossing spanning trees）在凸n+1边形上模旋转的组合结构来实现一一对应。

Result: 证明了A_n型迭代倾斜代数的同构类与凸n+1边形上模旋转的非交叉生成树之间存在双射。

Conclusion: 该结果为A_n型迭代倾斜代数提供了完整的组合分类，将代数分类问题转化为组合数学中的非交叉生成树分类问题。

Abstract: We show that isoclasses of iterated tilted algebras in type $A_n$ are in bijection with non-crossing spanning trees up to rotation on a convex n+1-gon. This is done by constructing a relationship between iterated tilted algebras up to isomorphism and exceptional sets up to isomorphic Hom-Ext quiver.

</details>


### [220] [On the Godement-Jacquet functional equation for finite matrix monoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08250)
*Elad Zelingher*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文基于先前与Harman和Snowden合作得到的矩阵幺半群代数K[Mat_n(F_q)]秩理想的单位显式公式，建立了对GL_n(F_q)不可约表示及该代数成立的规则化Godement-Jacquet泛函方程，并由此给出对应不可约表示的原始中心幂等元的显式表达式。值得注意的是，公式中的系数与退化的非交换高斯和密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者的动机是将先前工作中发现的矩阵幺半群代数秩理想单位的显式公式应用于更深层的结构分析，即通过建立规则化的Godement-Jacquet泛函方程，为GL_n(F_q)的表示理论提供新的工具，并最终给出原始中心幂等元的显式构造。这一谱系方法源于Godement-Jacquet在局部域上研究自守形式与L-函数的经典工作，本工作将其推广到有限域矩阵代数的表示论语境中。

Method: 作者利用先前推导的矩阵幺半群代数K[Mat_n(F_q)]秩理想中单位的明确公式，将这些单位构造为调和分析中的核函数，进而建立规则化（regularized）的Godement-Jacquet泛函方程。这一泛函方程在GL_n(F_q)的不可约表示以及矩阵幺半群代数的不可约表示上均成立。基于该泛函方程，作者进一步对原始中心幂等元进行分解，提取出其显式系数表达式，并揭示了这些系数与退化非交换高斯和之间的深刻联系。

Result: 文章成功建立了适用于GL_n(F_q)不可约表示及K[Mat_n(F_q)]不可约表示的规则化Godement-Jacquet泛函方程，并以此为工具得到了对应不可约表示的原始中心幂等元的显式公式。该公式在K的特征不整除|GL_n(F_q)|的条件下有效，且系数与退化非交换高斯和建立了明确关联。

Conclusion: 这项工作将Godement-Jacquet泛函方程的思想成功引入有限域矩阵代数的表示理论，并通过显式公式深化了对原始中心幂等元结构的理解。系数与退化非交换高斯和的联系暗示了更深层的算术结构，为后续研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: In previous work with Harman and Snowden, we found explicit formulas for units of rank ideals of the matrix monoid algebra $\mathbb{K}\left[\operatorname{Mat}_n\left(\mathbb{F}_q\right)\right]$, where $\mathbb{K}$ is an algebraically closed field with characteristic not dividing $q$. In this work, we use these explicit formulas to establish a regularized Godement--Jacquet functional equation valid for irreducible representations of $\operatorname{GL}_n\left(\mathbb{F}_q\right)$ and of the algebra $\mathbb{K}\left[\operatorname{Mat}_n\left(\mathbb{F}_q\right)\right]$. Using this functional equation we give an explicit expression for primitive central idempotents corresponding to irreducible representations of $\mathbb{K}\left[\operatorname{Mat}_n\left(\mathbb{F}_q\right)\right]$, where the characteristic of $\mathbb{K}$ does not divide $\left|\operatorname{GL}_n\left(\mathbb{F}_q\right)\right|$. Interestingly, the coefficients in this formula are closely related to degenerate non-abelian Gauss sums.

</details>


### [221] [Monoidal structures arising from $B_\infty$-algebras with applications to Hopf algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08511)
*Gongxiang Liu,Zhengfang Wang,Mengdie Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文给出了带$B_\infty$-结构的$A_\infty$-代数$A$的右$A_\infty$-模导出范畴上的幺半结构的显式构造，通过诱导函子$\iota$及张量积$\boxtimes_A$证明其构成幺半三角范畴。该构造应用于有限维Hopf代数，证明Koszul对偶函子是三角弱幺半的，并在局部化子范畴上给出幺半三角等价，从而为Krause猜想提供了纯代数的替代证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代数几何与表示论中，导出范畴上的幺半结构（尤其是张量积）是核心工具。作者旨在为$A_\infty$-模的导出范畴构造显式的幺半结构，并希望将其应用于Hopf代数的表示范畴，以纯代数方式复现并证明Krause关于导出范畴幺半等价的猜想。

Method: 利用$B_\infty$-代数结构（brace结构），构造从右$A_\infty$-模范畴到$A_\infty$-双模范畴的诱导函子$\iota$，并定义张量积$M\boxtimes_A N = M\overset{\infty}{\otimes}_A \iota(N)$。通过显式的$A_\infty$-双模拟同构（如$\iota(A)\simeq A$）验证单位约束与结合约束，证明幺半三角范畴结构。随后将其应用于有限维Hopf代数$H$的Yoneda dg代数，研究Koszul对偶函子的幺半性质。

Result: 证明了$(\mathcal{D}^r_\infty(A),\boxtimes_A,A)$构成幺半三角范畴；对有限维Hopf代数$H$，Koszul对偶函子$\mathrm{Hom}_H(\mathcal{Y}(H,\Bbbk),-)$是三角弱幺半的，且在由$\mathcal{Y}(H,\Bbbk)$生成的局部化子范畴（$H$局部时即整个$\mathcal{K}(\mathrm{Inj}\text{-}H)$）上为幺半三角等价；对分次交换代数恢复通常的张量积，并说明brace $B_\infty$结构及幺半结构本质上依赖于所选取的Hopf结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为$A_\infty$-代数导出范畴上的幺半结构提供了系统且显式的构造框架，并以纯代数方法证明了Krause关于幺半等价的猜想（由Benson--Krause通过分类空间$BG$建立），同时揭示了幺半结构对Hopf结构的本质依赖性。

Abstract: We give an explicit construction of monoidal structures on derived categories of right $A_\infty$-modules over an $A_\infty$-algebra $A$ equipped with a $B_\infty$-structure. Given such a $B_\infty$-algebra $A$, we construct an induction functor \[ι\colon \mathcal{D}^{\rm{r}}_\infty(A) \longrightarrow \mathcal{D}^{\rm{bi}}_\infty(A)\] from right $A_\infty$-modules to $A_\infty$-bimodules and define \[M\boxtimes_A N=M\overset{\infty}{\otimes}_Aι(N).\] We prove that $(\mathcal{D}^{\rm{r}}_\infty(A),\boxtimes_A,A)$ is a monoidal triangulated category: the unit and associativity constraints are induced by explicit quasi-isomorphisms of $A_\infty$-bimodules, including \[ι(A)\simeq A \qquad\text{and}\qquad ι(M)\overset{\infty}{\otimes}_Aι(N)\simeq ι(M\boxtimes_A N).\] We apply this construction to finite-dimensional Hopf algebras $H$, the Yoneda dg algebra $\mathcal{Y}(\Bbbk,\Bbbk)$ of the trivial $H$-module carries a natural brace $B_\infty$-structure, and hence its derived category carries the monoidal structure constructed above. We show that the Koszul duality functor \[\rm{Hom}_H(\mathcal{Y}(H,\Bbbk),-)\colon \mathcal{K}(\rm{Inj}\text{-}H)\longrightarrow \mathcal{D}(\mathcal{Y}(\Bbbk,\Bbbk))\] is triangulated lax monoidal, and that its restriction to the localizing subcategory generated by the injective resolution $\mathcal{Y}(H,\Bbbk)$ is a monoidal triangulated equivalence. If $H$ is local, this localizing subcategory is all of $\mathcal{K}(\rm{Inj}\text{-}H)$. In particular, this gives an alternative, purely algebraic proof of the monoidal equivalence conjectured by Krause and established by Benson--Krause through the classifying space $BG$. Finally, our examples recover the usual tensor product for graded commutative algebras and show that the resulting brace $B_\infty$ and monoidal structures can depend essentially on the chosen Hopf structure.

</details>


### [222] [A new class of irreducible modules over the BMS-Kac-Moody algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08651)
*Qiu-Fan Chen,Yi-Jun Bai*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文通过将有限个不可约模Φ(λ,a,b,r,h(t))与不可约受限模做张量积，构造了一类BMS-Kac-Moody代数的非权模，给出了这些张量积模不可约的充要条件，确定了两模同构的条件，并证明所得不可约模是新的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BMS-Kac-Moody代数的非权模表示理论是当前研究热点，构造新的不可约非权模并研究其结构与分类具有重要意义。

Method: 采用张量积方法，将有限个不可约模与不可约受限模做张量积来构造非权模，并通过分析模的不可约性与同构条件来刻画其结构性质。

Result: 得到了张量积模不可约的充分必要条件，确定了两个此类模同构的判定条件，并通过与已知非权模的比较证明所构造的不可约模是新的。

Conclusion: 本文成功构造了一类新的BMS-Kac-Moody代数不可约非权模，完善了该类代数的非权表示理论。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct a class of non-weight modules over the BMS-Kac-Moody algebra by taking tensor products of finitely many irreducible modules $Φ(λ,\a,\b,\r,h(t))$ with irreducible restricted modules. We obtain the necessary and sufficient conditions for these tensor product modules to be irreducible, and determine the corresponding conditions for two such modules to be isomorphic. Moreover, we compare these modules with other known non-weight modules, showing that these irreducible modules are new.

</details>


### [223] [Quasi-triangular Jordan D-bialgebras and extended relative Rota-Baxter operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08688)
*Dilei Lu,Dongping Hou,Yuanchang Lin*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入了拟三角Jordan D-双代数，通过拓展相对Rota-Baxter算子研究Jordan Yang-Baxter方程的解，并建立了可分解Jordan D-双代数与二次Rota-Baxter Jordan代数之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jordan Yang-Baxter方程（JYBE）在Jordan代数理论中具有重要意义，但对其解的构造与分类研究尚不充分。本文旨在通过算子理论方法系统化地研究JYBE，并建立相关的代数结构框架。

Method: 本文采用算子方法：首先引入拓展相对Rota-Baxter算子的概念，利用对称化-反对称化分解将问题简化为同态对与相对Rota-Baxter算子两类情形；随后将这些算子形式应用于二次Jordan代数和半直积Jordan代数，构造JYBE的显式解；最后引入可分解Jordan D-双代数及二次Rota-Baxter Jordan代数的概念，并证明严格对应关系。

Result: 证明了拓展相对Rota-Baxter算子可诱导新的Jordan代数结构；给出了JYBE解（对称部分不变）的算子刻画；证明了任意Jordan D-双代数的Drinfeld经典double具有可分解Jordan D-双代数结构；建立了零权与非零权二次Rota-Baxter Jordan代数分别与三角及可分解Jordan D-双代数之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 本文通过算子框架统一和深化了对Jordan Yang-Baxter方程及Jordan D-双代数的理解，建立了从JYBE解到D-双代数结构再到Rota-Baxter Jordan代数的完整理论链条，为后续研究提供了系统性工具。

Abstract: This paper introduces quasi-triangular Jordan D-bialgebras, which are constructed from solutions of the Jordan Yang-Baxter equation (JYBE) with invariant symmetric parts. We first develop a systematic operator approach to the JYBE by introducing the notion of extended relative Rota-Baxter operators on Jordan algebras. Such operators with their extensions are shown to induce new Jordan algebra structures. Moreover, the symmetrizer-antisymmetrizer decomposition of linear maps reduces the study of extended relative Rota-Baxter operators to both pairs of homomorphisms of Jordan algebras and relative Rota-Baxter operators, respectively. This operator framework subsequently yields a characterization of solutions of the JYBE whose symmetric parts are invariant. These operator forms are further investigated in the context of quadratic Jordan algebras and semi-direct product Jordan algebras, respectively, leading to explicit constructions of solutions of the JYBE. A factorizable Jordan D-bialgebra is then introduced as a special class of quasi-triangular ones, which naturally factorizes the underlying Jordan algebra. We prove that the Drinfeld classical double of any Jordan D-bialgebra admits a factorizable Jordan D-bialgebra structure. Finally, the operator perspective gives rise to the notion of quadratic Rota-Baxter Jordan algebras: those of zero weight yield triangular Jordan D-bialgebras, while those of nonzero weight are shown to be in one-to-one correspondence with factorizable Jordan D-bialgebras.

</details>


### [224] [Jacobi descent charts and logarithmic quotient coordinates for split symmetric spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08974)
*Jonathan Sparling*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在非Archimedean局部域上，针对等秩分裂对称对(G,H)，构造了逼近幂零纤维的正则族Q(q)的有限下降图。在每个图上，Cartan提升在F上定义，将稀疏Jacobi族共轭到h中。该族Q(0)为主幂零元，且邻接商在族上的一般秩等于G的奇指数个数。文章还证明了对数Jacobian精确抵消相对Weyl判别式，从而正规化H°-商密度是参数环面上的乘法Haar测度；并通过Kummer理论分类了定义Cartan提升的扭曲平方根覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作由无穷小局部相对迹公式中θ-固定侧幂零纤维附近的奇异贡献所驱动，目的是理解在幂零纤维附近正则家族的几何结构和测度性质，为相对迹公式的深入分析提供工具。

Method: 构造有限下降图（finite descent charts），在每个图上定义F上的Cartan提升，将稀疏Jacobi族Q(q)共轭到h中。利用Kummer理论分类覆盖以刻画有理分支，并通过Weyl判别式与Jacobi行列式的精确抵消来建立测度公式。对于(Sp_{2n}, GL_n)情形，在Hurwitz坐标下给出显式构造，Cartan提升通过交换长根SL_2-子群因子化。

Result: 证明了邻接商在Q(q)族上的一般秩等于G的奇指数个数；对分裂对称对，该秩等于rank H，因此在等秩情形下映射一般为有限étale。证明了ds/|D_H^G|^{1/2} = C∏d^×z_α，即归一化H°-商密度是参数环面上的乘法Haar测度。经过有限clopen细化后，Iwasawa高度在val(z_α)上是分片线性的。

Conclusion: 该构造为等秩分裂对称对的无穷小相对迹公式提供了显式且统一的局部几何框架，尤其对(Sp_{2n}, GL_n)给出了完全显式的构造，表明该框架在计算上是可行的，并具有更广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a non-Archimedean local field of characteristic zero and residue characteristic different from $2$, and let $(G,H)$ be an equal-rank split symmetric pair with $G$ split semisimple and adjoint and with an $F$-split maximal $θ$-split torus. Motivated by the singular contribution near the nilpotent fiber on the $θ$-fixed side of the infinitesimal local relative trace formula, we construct finite descent charts for regular families in $\mathfrak h$ approaching that fiber. On each chart, a Cartan lift defined over $F$ conjugates the sparse Jacobi family \[ Q(q)=\sum_{α\inΔ}(γ_αn_{-α}+q_αn_α) \] into $\mathfrak h$. The family $Q(q)$ is regular for every $q$ and takes the principal nilpotent value $Q(0)$. The restriction of the adjoint quotient to this family has generic rank equal to the number of odd exponents of $G$. For split symmetric pairs this rank equals $\operatorname{rank}H$; hence in equal rank the map is generically finite étale. Kummer theory classifies the twisted square-root covers on which the Cartan lifts are defined, and these covers exhaust the rational branches.
  On every branch, with $q_α=z_α^2$, the quotient Jacobian cancels the relative Weyl discriminant exactly: \[ \frac{ds}{|D_H^G|^{1/2}}=C\prod_{α\inΔ}d^\times z_α. \] Thus the normalized $H^\circ$-quotient density is a multiplicative Haar measure on the parameter torus, uniformly across all rational twists. After a finite clopen refinement, the associated Iwasawa heights are piecewise affine in the valuations $\operatorname{val}(z_α)$. For $(\operatorname{Sp}_{2n},\operatorname{GL}_n)$, the construction is explicit in Hurwitz coordinates, and the Cartan lift factors through commuting long-root $\operatorname{SL}_2$-subgroups.

</details>


### [225] [Rickard's question on standard derived equivalences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09062)
*Wei Hu,Changchang Xi,Jin Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了一系列非标准的导出等价例子，否定了Rickard于1991年提出的"域上的代数之间每个导出等价都是标准的"这一猜想，并给出了该猜想成立的一个充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rickard在1991年提出了一个开放性问题：在公共域上的代数之间，是否每一个导出等价都是标准的（即同构于某个倾斜复形的张量积）？这个问题的答案对于理解导出等价的本质结构和代数表示论中的分类问题具有重要意义。

Method: 作者构造了一系列具体的代数例子，这些代数之间存在导出等价但该等价不是标准的。此外，作者通过深入分析导出等价的代数与几何结构间的关系，提出并证明了一个充要条件来描述此类非标准等价出现的边界。

Result: 成功构造了一系列非标准的导出等价例子，从而否定了Rickard的问题（答案为否定）。同时给出了Rickard问题为真的充要条件，即明确了在何种条件下所有导出等价都必然是标准的。

Conclusion: Rickard的猜想被否定：并非所有导出等价都是标准的。作者通过具体反例和充要条件对此问题给出了完整的回答。

Abstract: In 1991 Rickard asked whether every derived equivalence of algebras over a common field is standard. We construct a series of examples of non-standard derived equivalences, and thus answer the question negatively. Moreover, we give a necessary and sufficient condition for the question to be true.

</details>


### [226] [Growth in affine Hecke categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09197)
*Kevin Coulembier,Jensen O'Sullivan,Daniel Tubbenhauer*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究仿射Hecke范畴中张量幂的渐近增长（等价于仿射Hecke代数中Kazhdan-Lusztig基元素的幂次渐近），在任意仿射类型下证明了一般性界，在仿射型A1中确定了精确渐近，并在仿射型A2中对若干自然族建立了相应结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿射Hecke范畴中的张量幂及其对应的Kazhdan-Lusztig基元素幂次，在表示论和几何表示论中具有重要意义。理解其渐近增长行为有助于深入认识仿射Hecke代数的代数结构及其范畴化性质，但目前缺乏系统性的渐近分析结果。

Method: 通过范畴论和表示论技术，在任意仿射Weyl类型下建立一般性上界/下界估计；在仿射型A1中利用具体的代数结构进行精确计算以确定渐近常数；在仿射型A2中对多个自然的Kazhdan-Lusztig基元素族分别分析其幂次增长行为。

Result: 获得了任意仿射类型下张量幂增长的通用界；完全确定了仿射型A1中Kazhdan-Lusztig元素幂次的精确渐近；在仿射型A2中对多个自然族给出了渐近增长的具体结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为仿射Hecke范畴张量幂的渐近行为提供了系统性的刻画，填补了该方向在一般仿射类型下的空白，并为后续在更一般仿射类型中建立精确渐近提供了框架和猜想基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the asymptotic growth of tensor powers in affine Hecke categories, or equivalently of powers of Kazhdan--Lusztig basis elements in affine Hecke algebras. We prove general bounds in arbitrary affine type, determine precise asymptotics in affine type A1, and establish corresponding results for several natural families in affine type A2.

</details>

### [227] [Celestial Holography, A Hitchhiker's Guide to the Celestial Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07568)
*Faïçal Barzi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文是天体全息学的入门级综述，从AdS/CFT对应出发，系统介绍渐近平直时空中量子引力的全息描述框架，包括Bondi-Sachs形式体系、BMS渐近对称群，以及通过Mellin变换将四维散射振幅重铸为二维天体共形场论关联函数的核心全息映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AdS/CFT对应在量子引力全息描述中的巨大成功，物理学家自然希望将全息原理推广到更切合实际的渐近平直时空（闵可夫斯基时空），以理解天体物理和引力波探测等现实场景中的量子引力问题。本文旨在为新手提供系统性的概念框架和技术词汇，降低进入该领域的门槛。

Method: 论文采用教学性综述的方法：(1) 以AdS/CFT对应作为出发点和类比基础；(2) 介绍Bondi-Sachs形式体系与零无穷远几何；(3) 阐述无穷维BMS渐近对称群的发现；(4) 详细解释通过Mellin变换将四维散射振幅映射为二维天体球上CCFT关联函数的核心全息映射过程。

Result: 提供了一个直观但仍保持数学精确性的天体全息学入门教程，覆盖从AdS/CFT到Bondi-Sachs形式体系、BMS对称群、直至天体共形场论全息映射的完整知识路径，使新手能够快速掌握该领域的概念图景与核心技术语言。

Conclusion: 本文作为天体全息学的首篇入门导引，旨在装备新研究者以必要的概念理解和技术词汇，使其能够探索渐近平直时空中量子引力全息描述这一快速发展领域的前沿问题。

Abstract: This primer offers an first introduction to Celestial Holography, a rapidly evolving framework aiming to describe quantum gravity in asymptotically flat spacetimes. We start from the established foundation of the AdS/CFT correspondence before venturing into the wilds of Minkowski space. The journey covers the essential Bondi-Sachs formalism and the geometry of null infinity ($\mathscr{I}^\pm$), the discovery of the infinite-dimensional BMS asymptotic symmetry group. We explain the core holographic map given by Mellin transform that recasts four-dimensional scattering amplitudes as correlation functions of a conjectured 2D Celestial Conformal Field Theory (CCFT) on the celestial sphere. The presentation is made intuitive but the mathematics is still precise (hopefully!). It is designed to equip newcomers with both the conceptual landscape and the technical vocabulary to explore this exciting frontier.

</details>


### [228] [Organizing transitions and their cascades: Generalized symmetry enforcement in massless flows or Higgs transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08428)
*Yoshiki Fukusumi,Yuma Furuta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了幺正极小模型质量重正化群流中广义对称性的作用，证明了未破缺的融合环对称性FR(SU(2)_{p-2})消除了红外理论中的所有相关微扰，从而使红外相成为（弱）对称性强制的无隙相。通过折叠技巧，该对称性对应（半）整数自旋非简单流，并揭示了其在相关和危险无关微扰共振效应中的根本作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在理解广义对称性在无质量重正化群流或Higgs相变中的作用，特别是探究幺正极小模型M(p,p+1)→M̄(p-1,p)系列流中的对称性保护机制及其物理后果。

Method: 作者从融合环对称性FR(SU(2)_{p-2})出发，通过详细的对称性分析证明其消除了红外理论M̄(p-1,p)中的全部相关微扰；利用折叠技巧(folding trick)将未破缺对称性解释为（半）整数自旋非简单流，并借助coset/level-rank对偶结构联系非阿贝尔任意子；最后通过共振效应分析揭示相关和危险无关微扰的相互作用机制。

Result: 证明了融合环对称性能消除所有相关微扰，使红外理论M̄(p-1,p)在标度分析层面上稳定，可作为对称性强制的无隙相；揭示了（半）整数自旋非简单流在共振效应中的根本作用，并指出共振可能引发级联相变或流向未探索定点，而对称性可充当此类反常流的阻止器。

Conclusion: 广义对称性（融合环对称性）在拓扑相变和无质量流中扮演了关键的稳定化角色，不仅能强制执行无隙相，还能抑制可能导致级联相变的异常重整化群流，为理解对称性保护的无隙相和相变级联提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We study the role of generalized symmetry in massless renormalization group flows or Higgs transitions. In particular, we revisit the massless renormalization group flows in unitary minimal models, $\mathbf{M}(p,p+1) \rightarrow \overline{\mathbf{M}(p-1,p)}$ preserving the fusion ring symmetry $\text{FR} (SU(2)_{p-2})\subset \mathbf{M}(p,p+1)$ where $p$ is an integer satisfying $p>3$. In this series of flows, we demonstrate that the unbroken fusion ring symmetry $\text{FR} (SU(2)_{p-2})$ eliminates all relevant perturbations in the $\overline{\mathbf{M}(p-1,p)}$ model. Hence, the infrared theory $\overline{\mathbf{M}(p-1,p)}$ is stable at the level of the scaling analysis and can be interpreted as a (weak-)symmetry-enforced gapless phase in contemporary theoretical physics. Phenomenologically, by the folding trick, the unbroken fusion ring symmetry corresponds to a (half-)integer spin nonsimple current, a variant of the Cooper pair involving nonabelian anyons generated from the coset or level-rank duality structures. Moreover, we demonstrate that the structure of (half-)integer spin nonsimple current plays a fundamental role in causing the resonance effect of relevant and dangerously irrelevant perturbations. This resonance effect may result in the cascade of phase transitions (or the system flows to unexplored fixed points), and the symmetry can be a stopper of such unconventional flows.

</details>


### [229] [Three-loop onset of the wormhole length in DSSYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07634)
*Eleonora Alfinito,Matteo Beccaria*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了正弦-膨胀引力（sine-dilaton gravity）中爱因斯坦-罗森桥（Einstein-Rosen bridge）的长度与双重标度SYK模型（DSSYK）的Krylov展开复杂度之间的对偶关系。通过三圈图计算并在闭式形式下给出了热场双态在t=0时的起始长度L0（即初始热Hartle-Hawking态的制备复杂度），并给出了其微观的、无歧义的确定方法。作者还通过精确递推关系绕过了三圈时的鞍点分析，并在低温极限下将半经典级数重整为Schwarzian耦合λβ的展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结果仅在一圈水平下建立了sine-dilaton引力中的爱因斯坦-罗森桥长度与DSSYK模型Krylov复杂度之间的对应关系，而L0（热场双态在t=0时的桥长）的更高阶修正及其与微观模型之间的精确定量联系尚不清楚。此外，L0中的加法常数在引力侧仅由全息重整化方案的选择决定，缺乏从微观模型出发的无歧义确定。

Method: 作者利用sine-dilaton引力的弦论世界面（disk level）与DSSYK模型之间的对偶关系：将L0识别为λ乘以热态的平均弦数。两圈贡献通过对DSSYK两点函数在重合插入点处的鞍点求值获得，其中各自发散的贡献仅在求和中相消。三圈计算绕开鞍点分析，改用精确递推关系以低成本生成高温长展开，并针对提出的Ansatz用大量独立数据点进行验证。低温极限下，利用每个圈阶贡献β的一次幂这一性质，将半经典级数重整化为λβ的展开，其领头系数与独立的一圈Schwarzian计算对照验证。同一方法还用于计算长度方差和三阶累积量至两圈。

Result: 在闭式形式下获得了L0到三圈的结果，给出了与任意逆温度β的关系；L0被确认为λ乘以热态平均弦数，从而在微观层面无歧义地确定了引力侧原本仅由重整化方案选择决定的加法常数。两圈贡献验证了发散项的精确抵消机制；三圈通过递推关系生成的Ansatz得到大量独立数据点的验证。低温极限下得到λβ展开的领头系数，与独立的Schwarzian一圈计算一致。长度方差和三阶累积量的半经典展开也计算至两圈。

Conclusion: 本文在sine-dilaton引力与DSSYK模型之间建立了更高精度的对偶对应，特别是将桥长的起始值L0从仅依赖重整化方案的选择提升为微观确定的物理量。这加深了对量子引力中复杂性/体积对偶的理解，并为研究热态制备复杂度提供了新的计算工具。此外，低温极限下重整化为Schwarzian耦合λβ的展开，进一步揭示了半经典与超对称量子力学极限之间的联系。

Abstract: The length of the Einstein-Rosen bridge in sine-dilaton gravity at disk level equals the Krylov spread complexity of the dual double-scaled SYK (DSSYK) model, with the double-scaling parameter $λ$ controlling the semiclassical expansion. We compute the onset value, $L_{0}$, in the thermofield double state at $t=0$ and arbitrary inverse temperature $β$, through three loops and in closed form, extending the known one-loop result. The quantity $L_{0}$ represents the preparation complexity of the initial thermal Hartle-Hawking state. In DSSYK, $L_{0}$ is $λ$ times the average chord number of the thermal state, providing a microscopic, unambiguous determination of an additive constant otherwise fixed only by a choice of holographic renormalization scheme. The two-loop contribution follows from a saddle point evaluation of the DSSYK two-point function at coincident insertion points, where individually divergent contributions cancel only in their sum. At three loops, we bypass the saddle point analysis using an exact recursion relation that generates long high-temperature expansions at low cost, fixing and testing a proposed Ansatz against many independent data points. Finally, in the low-temperature regime each loop order contributes one further power of $β$, reorganizing the semiclassical series into an expansion in the Schwarzian coupling $λβ$, whose leading coefficient we check against an independent one-loop Schwarzian computation. The same methods, applied to the length variance and third-order cumulant at $t=0$, give their semiclassical expansion through two loops.

</details>


### [230] [Understanding Non-Split 2-Group Symmetry: (3+1)D SymTFT, Anomaly and Bordism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08766)
*Zhenbang Gu,Ran Luo,Yi-Nan Wang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了具有非平凡Postnikov类的有限非分裂2-群对称性，以最简单的Z2 0-形式对称性×Z2 1-形式对称性（由非平凡Postnikov类交织）为例，计算了d≤5维时空中的定向和自旋配边群来对其异常进行分类，并在d=3情形下利用融合2-范畴和(3+1)D TQFT语言研究了其对称性TFT/TO及边界条件，完成了该类非分裂有限2-群的范畴化Landau范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非分裂2-群对称性（即具有非平凡Postnikov类的2-群）在物理系统中广泛出现，但其异常分类和对称性拓扑场论结构尚未被系统研究。理解这类广义对称性对于深入认识量子场论中的对称性原理及其反常至关重要。

Method: （1）通过计算定向配边群Ω_{d+1}^{SO}(B𝒢)和自旋配边群Ω_{d+1}^{Spin}(B𝒢)（d≤5）来分类2-群对称性𝒢的异常；（2）在d=3情形下，利用融合2-范畴语言构造对称性TFT/TO，写出(3+1)D TQFT作用量；（3）分类对称性TFT的极小拓扑边界条件和物理边界条件；（4）通过Hom(Ω̃_4^{SO}(B𝒢),U(1))≅H^4(B𝒢;U(1))≅Z2刻画2-群异常并实施范畴化Landau范式。

Result: 完成了d≤5维时空中2-群对称性的异常分类；在d=3时构造了对称性TFT/TO的完整框架，包括融合2-范畴描述和(3+1)D TQFT作用量；分类了所有极小的拓扑与物理边界条件；通过H^4(B𝒢;U(1))≅Z2刻画了2-群异常，并将Landau范式推广到非分裂有限2-群情形。

Conclusion: 非分裂有限2-群对称性具有丰富且可完整分类的异常结构与TFT实现，其对称性TFT/TO框架和范畴化Landau范式为理解含非平凡Postnikov类的广义对称性提供了系统性的理论工具，并可推广到更一般的2-群甚至更高群对称性。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of finite, non-split 2-group symmetries with a non-trivial Postnikov class, focusing on the simplest example with a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 0-form symmetry and a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 1-form symmetry, intertwined together by the non-trivial Postnikov class in $H^3(B\mathbb{Z}_2;\mathbb{Z}_2)\cong\mathbb{Z}_2$, denoted by $\mathcal{G}$. We classify the anomalies of this 2-group symmetry for physical theories in $d$-dimensional spacetime, by computing the oriented bordism groups $Ω_{d+1}^{\rm SO}(B\mathcal{G})$ and the spin bordism groups $Ω_{d+1}^{\rm Spin}(B\mathcal{G})$ for $d\leq 5$. For the case of $d=3$, we investigate the Symmetry TFT/TO of the 2-group symmetry $\mathcal{G}$ using the language of fusion 2-categories, as well as (3+1)D TQFT actions, for the cases without or with the 2-group anomaly classified by $\operatorname{Hom}\left(\widetildeΩ_4^{\rm SO}(B\mathcal{G}),U(1)\right) \cong H^4(B\mathcal{G};U(1))\cong\mathbb{Z}_2$. We classify the minimal topological and physical boundary conditions of the Symmetry TFTs, and carry out the categorical Landau paradigm for such non-split finite 2-group.

</details>


### [231] [Towards a Holographic dual of Carrollian BCFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07658)
*Pronoy Chakraborty,Ritankar Chatterjee,Priyadarshini Pandit*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Carrollian框架建立了平坦版本AdS₃/BCFT₂对应的基础，在平直时空的类光无穷远识别了边界Carrollian共形代数（BCCA），并证明了由EOW膜限制下的全局及渐近对称性与BCCA对称性精确一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将AdS₃/BCFT₂对应推广到平直时空版本，利用Carrollian框架建立平直时空中的全息对应关系。这是将AdS/BCFT全息原理延伸到渐近平坦时空的重要尝试，有望为平直时空中的量子引力与边界共形场论之间的联系提供新的理论框架。

Method: 作者首先讨论最近发现的边界Carrollian共形代数（BCCA），即Carrollian BCFT的对称性代数。然后在平直时空的类光无穷远处，通过适当选择EOW膜来识别该代数。对EOW膜的性质进行了双重分析：一是从其AdS对应物出发取平坦极限，二是内在直接分析。最后验证了全局对称性和渐近对称性分别与BCCA的对称性完全一致。

Result: 作者成功在平直时空的类光无穷远处构造了与BCCA相关的EOW膜配置，并从AdS弦平直极限的角度验证了其正确性。严格的对称性匹配表明，由EOW膜限制下的平坦时空全局对称性和渐近对称性恰好对应BCCA的对称结构，为平坦的AdS₃/BCFT₂对应提供了坚实的理论基础。

Conclusion: 该工作为平直版本的全息BCFT对应奠定了理论基础，建立了Carrollian全息原理与边界共形场论之间的桥梁。BCCA在类光无穷远处的实现及其与EOW膜限制下对称性的一致性，为后续研究平坦时空中的量子纠缠、黑洞信息等问题提供了新的框架。

Abstract: In this work we aim to lay the foundation of flat version of AdS$_3$/BCFT$_2$ correspondence through Carrollian framework. We begin with discussing the recently discovered Boundary Carrollian Conformal Algebra (BCCA), the symmetry algebra of Carrollian BCFT. We identify this algebra at the null infinity of flat spacetime with appropriate choice of the end-of-the-world (EOW) brane. The properties of this EOW brane have been analysed both intrinsically and by taking flat limit from its AdS counterpart. We also find that the global as well as asymptotic symmetries of flat spacetime restricted by this EOW brane coincide exactly with the symmetries of BCCA.

</details>


### [232] [Superconformal indices and black hole saddles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07660)
*Maciej Kolanowski,Donald Marolf,Zi-Yue Wang,Wenwen Zheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文利用AdS/CFT对应关系研究N=4 SU(N)超对称杨-米尔斯理论的超共形指标，指出在大Im τ和Im σ极限下，CFT指标快速趋近1，从而排除了此前被认为相关的大N指数贡献的黑洞鞍点，并提出一种基于实Lorentz时空与余维2奇异点的整体路径积分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdS/CFT对应暗示超共形指标可通过整体理论在大N极限下用鞍点求和计算，但此前提出的某些鞍点（与大N指数贡献相关）在特定参数极限下与CFT指标行为矛盾，需要重新审视路径积分定义。

Method: 作者提出将整体路径积分定义为对实Lorentz号时空（带余维2奇异点）的积分，该定义仅允许满足指标上界约束的鞍点，并通过Stokes现象强制这一上界的成立。该框架同样应用于AdS_4中ABJM超共形指标的计算。

Result: 新方法排除了此前被认为在大Im τ和Im σ极限下相关的鞍点，仅保留满足CFT指标趋近1约束的鞍点；对ABJM情形得到类似结论。

Conclusion: 基于实Lorentz时空的路径积分框架能够自然解释CFT指标在特定极限下的行为，通过Stokes现象强制执行边界约束，提供了关于非微扰整体鞍点合理性的新理解。

Abstract: The AdS/CFT correspondence implies that the superconformal index ${\mathcal I}$ in ${\mathcal N=4}$ SU(N) supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory can be computed using the dual bulk theory. In particular, in the limit of large $N$, the index should be given by a sum over appropriate saddles. However, ${\mathcal I}$ depends on potentials $σ, τ, \vec Δ$ and, at large ${\rm Im}\, τ= {\rm Im}\, σ$, the CFT index ${\mathcal I}$ rapidly approaches $1$ at all values of $N$. As a result, black hole saddles associated with exponentially large contributions in $N$ cannot contribute in this limit. This in particular excludes saddles that were previously suggested to be relevant in such regimes. We thus consider an approach to the bulk path integral motivated by taking it to be defined as an integral over real Lorentz-signature spacetimes with codimension-2 singularities. This approach leads only to saddles that satisfy the above bound, and to the enforcement of this bound via Stokes phenomena. We also find similar results for bulk AdS$_4$ calculations of the ABJM superconformal index.

</details>


### [233] [R7-branes and AdS$_{\text{9}}$ solutions in type IIB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07664)
*Giuseppe Dibitetto,Cameron Gibson,Nicolò Petri,Colin Sterckx*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了IIB型超引力中新发现的AdS9背景，在更便利的坐标系中重写了这些解并计算了包括全息中心荷在内的相关可观测量。随后研究了由IIB轴-介子场源的非超对称7-膜构型，找到了以AdS9背景为近地平线几何的显式解析7-膜解，并探讨了这些解与R7膜之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高维AdS背景（AdS9）在IIB型超引力中的存在性与结构，并理解其对应的膜构造，这对于扩展AdS/CFT对应以及弦理论中非超对称膜的研究具有重要意义。

Method: 首先选取更合适的坐标系重写AdS9背景解以简化计算；然后利用IIB轴-介子场构造非超对称7-膜配置，求其显式解析解；最后通过施加反射幺模τ→-τ̄的幺模变换来建立这些7-膜解与R7膜的联系。

Result: 找到了以AdS9为近地平线几何的显式解析7-膜解，计算出全息中心荷等可观测量，并通过反射幺模建立了与R7膜的联系。

Conclusion: AdS9背景可通过7-膜构造实现，且与R7膜在反射幺模下相关联，进一步丰富了非超对称膜背景的图景。

Abstract: We consider the recently found $\mathrm{AdS}_9$ backgrounds in type IIB supergravity. We start by reviewing the solutions in a more convenient set of coordinates, which allows us to explicitly compute a few relevant observables, including the holographic central charge. Subsequently, we study non-supersymmetric 7-brane configurations sourced by the IIB axio-dilaton. We find explicit analytic 7-brane solutions that possess the above $\mathrm{AdS}_9$ backgrounds as their near-horizon geometry. Finally, we discuss the relation of these 7-brane solutions to the R7 branes by imposing the reflection monodromy $τ\rightarrow -\bar τ$ in the transverse plane.

</details>


### [234] [Updates on Calabi-Yau manifolds from pairs of non-compact Calabi-Yau manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07694)
*Nam-Hoon Lee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了通过光滑化拟Fano流形的正规交叉并集来构造Calabi-Yau流形的方法进展，涵盖自2010年引入物理学界以来的发展。内容包括构造具有无界第二Betti数的Calabi-Yau三维流形、具有极小Hodge数的流形，以及大于三维的首次非Kähler例子，并解释了Landau-Ginzburg模型在镜像构造中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Calabi-Yau流形在弦论和代数几何中具有核心地位，但系统地构造具有特定拓扑性质（如无界Betti数、极小Hodge数）的Calabi-Yau流形长期是难题。通过光滑化正规交叉并集（即"半稳定退化"方法）提供了一种新的系统构造途径，自2010年引入物理学界后已有重要进展，值得综述总结。

Method: 该方法的核心思想是取若干拟Fano（quasi-Fano）流形的正规交叉并集（normal crossing union），通过对其执行光滑化（smoothing）操作，构造出Calabi-Yau流形。具体包括：利用拟Fano流形的性质构造具有特定拓扑不变量（如Betti数、Hodge数）的Calabi-Yau三维流形；利用类似的组合几何技术扩展到更高维，首次构造出大于三维的非Kähler Calabi-Yau例子；并结合Landau-Ginzburg模型进行镜像对称构造。

Result: 成功构造了具有无界（unbounded）第二Betti数的Calabi-Yau三维流形族，以及具有非常小Hodge数的Calabi-Yau流形。此外，将构造方法推广到高维，首次获得了维度大于三的非Kähler Calabi-Yau流形例子，并阐明了Landau-Ginzburg模型在这些镜像构造中的作用。

Conclusion: 光滑化正规交叉并集方法已成为构造Calabi-Yau流形的重要工具，能够系统性地产生具有极端拓扑性质的流形，并延伸到非Kähler情形，为弦论中的紧致化和镜像对称提供了更丰富的几何实例。

Abstract: We discuss developments in the construction of Calabi--Yau manifolds by smoothing normal crossing unions of quasi-Fano manifolds, following the introduction of this method to the physics community in 2010. We describe constructions of Calabi--Yau threefolds with unbounded second Betti numbers and with very small Hodge numbers, as well as the first non-Kähler examples in dimensions greater than three. The role of Landau--Ginzburg models in mirror constructions is also explained.

</details>


### [235] [Supertranslations are Soft Dressings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07699)
*Asaad Elkhidir,Donal O'Connell,Radu Roiban*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究电动力学和引力中无质量间隙导致的大质量粒子渐近态定义不唯一的问题，在KMOC形式主义下证明相干态 dressing 通过大规范/BMS变换修改可观测量，并统一解释了这些效应对散射数据、冲击参数和角动量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于电动力学和引力中不存在质量间隙，大质量粒子的渐近态并非唯一确定，这种 dressing 自由度对经典物理学观测量（如散射数据、角动量）的影响此前未被系统研究。作者希望通过KMOC形式主义弄清这些自由度如何以经典方式体现。

Method: 在KMOC形式主义中研究大质量粒子态相干态dressing的经典后果；展示相干态位移算符可被补全为守恒荷（引力情形即BMS荷），该荷在散射数据上实现大规范/BMS变换；通过软因子分解而非底层局域对称性构造；在无规范对称性的标量理论中展示相同结构。

Result: 证明了相干态dressing通过大规范/BMS变换修改可观测量；dressing参数即大规范/BMS变换参数，且可观测量对变换参数的单/偶极部分不敏感（全阶成立）；在两粒子态上考察了大规范变换对散射的影响，获得冲击参数的普适平移，并澄清了辐射角动量的框架依赖性与总角动量的框架无关性；以史瓦西度规的Kerr-Schild形式（canonical BMS框架）与De Donder形式（内在框架）间的变换作为具体例证。

Conclusion: 大质量粒子渐近态的dressing自由度在经典层面等价于大规范/BMS变换，由软因子分解而非局域对称性驱动，其对观测量（特别是角动量）的影响依赖于框架选择，但总角动量保持框架无关。

Abstract: Asymptotic states of massive particles in electrodynamics and gravity are not uniquely defined because of the absence of a mass gap. Working in the KMOC formalism, we study the classical implications of this freedom in the dressing of massive particle states. We show that coherent-state dressings modify observables by a large gauge/BMS transformation. The associated coherent-state displacement operator can be completed into a conserved charge, the BMS charge in the gravitational case, which implements large gauge transformations on scattering data. The parameter of our dressing is directly the large gauge/BMS transformation parameter, and we explain that observables do not depend on monopolar and dipolar parts of the transformation parameter to all orders. Our construction is driven by soft factorization rather than by an underlying local symmetry, and we illustrate this by exhibiting the same structure in a scalar theory with no gauge symmetry. We explore the action of large gauge transformations on two-particle states for scalar, abelian gauge and graviton mediators, obtaining a universal shift of the impact parameter and clarifying the frame dependence of the radiated angular momentum vis à vis the frame-independence of the total angular momentum. The transformation connecting the canonical BMS frame, in which the Schwarzschild metric takes the Kerr-Schild form, to the intrinsic frame, in which it takes the De Donder form, illustrates these effects concretely.

</details>


### [236] [Bumpy horizons from non-linear sigma models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07736)
*Fabrizio Canfora,Nicolás Grandi,Carla Henríquez-Báez,Julio Oliva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了在任意偶数维度下，当爱因斯坦方程的源由一类特定的非线性sigma模型的应力-能量张量给出时，可以构造具有非恒定曲率的黑洞视界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑洞视界通常具有恒定曲率（如球对称、平面对称或双曲对称），但非恒定曲率的黑洞视界在物理上更一般且具有理论意义。本文旨在扩展黑洞解的构造范围，在任意偶数维度下实现非恒定曲率的视界，突破以往的限制。

Method: 使用一类特定的非线性sigma模型作为爱因斯坦方程的源（物质场），利用其应力-能量张量构造非恒定曲率的黑洞视界解，并在任意偶数维度下验证解的可行性。

Result: 成功在任意偶数维度下构造出具有非恒定曲率视界的黑洞解，该解由特定类别的非线性sigma模型提供物质源支撑。

Conclusion: 非恒定曲率的黑洞视界在任意偶数维度中是可行的，所需物质源为一类合适的非线性sigma模型，这为更一般的黑洞解族提供了新的构造途径。

Abstract: We show that black hole horizons with non-constant curvature can be constructed in any even dimension, when the source of the Einstein equations is given by the stress-energy tensor of a non-linear sigma model of a particular class

</details>


### [237] [Ricci-flat metrics from gauged linear $σ$-models without RG flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09918)
*Dmitri Bykov,Ondrej Hulik,Ulf Lindström,Martin Roček,Rikard von Unge*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类通过具有不定号号的规范线性西格玛模型（GLSMs）构造的广义conifold上的Ricci-flat Kähler度量，通过引入带负号动能项的shadow坐标（超场），给出了复锥（基为射影空间乘积）上的显式Ricci-flat度量，并提供了Kähler势的一般公式及详细实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索通过GLSM构造显式Calabi-Yau度量的新途径，特别是利用具有不定号号的GLSM来生成广义conifold上的Ricci-flat Kähler度量，为Calabi-Yau度量和环面几何的研究提供新方向。

Method: 在GLSM框架中引入带有负号动能项的shadow坐标（超场），利用这些不定号号模型构造广义conifold上的Kähler势，进而得到显式的Ricci-flat Kähler度量。提供了Kähler势的一般公式，并通过详细实例加以说明。此外，给出了新型广义Kähler规范化的解释。

Result: 成功导出了复锥（射影空间乘积上）的显式Ricci-flat度量及对应的Kähler势一般公式，并通过具体实例验证了构造的有效性。同时提出了不定号号模型的几何意义相关的开放问题。

Conclusion: 该工作为Calabi-Yau度量的显式构造和环面几何研究开辟了新方向，并深化了对不定号号GLSM及广义Kähler规范化几何内涵的理解。

Abstract: We investigate a class of Ricci-flat Kähler metrics on generalized conifolds constructed via gauged linear sigma models (GLSMs) with indefinite signature. By introducing shadow coordinates (superfields) entering the sigma model with negative signature kinetic term, we show that these GLSMs yield explicit Ricci-flat metrics on complex cones over products of projective spaces. We provide a general formula for the resulting Kähler potentials, along with detailed examples. Our results suggest new directions for the study of Calabi-Yau metrics and toric geometry, and raise interesting questions about the geometric meaning of indefinite signature models. We also give an interpretation in terms of a novel generalized Kähler gauging.

</details>


### [238] [Incidence Relations for Self-Dual Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07774)
*Joon-Hwi Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Penrose非线性引力子构造框架下，通过Kerr-Schild坐标实现了自对偶黑洞时空（Eguchi-Hanson、自对偶Taub-NUT和自对偶Plebański-Demiański）的显式局部incidence relation构造，并在Plebański第二heavenly方程框架下实现了Dunajski-Mason递归，得到了闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Penrose非线性引力子构造中，自对偶时空作为弯曲twistor空间中全纯twistor线的模空间出现，其显式参数化由incidence relation给出。然而对于自对偶黑洞时空，这一incidence relation的显式构造此前尚缺乏具体实现。作者旨在填补这一空白，为自对偶黑洞时空提供显式、闭合的incidence relation表达式。

Method: 作者采用Kerr-Schild坐标来描述自对偶黑洞时空，通过实施Dunajski-Mason递归方法，在Plebański第二heavenly方程的框架下构造incidence relation。关键步骤包括显式求出Plebański标量，并据此推导出各自对偶黑洞时空的闭式incidence relation公式。

Result: 成功为Eguchi-Hanson、自对偶Taub-NUT和自对偶Plebański-Demiański三种自对偶黑洞时空构造了显式的incidence relation闭式解。特别值得注意的是，自对偶Taub-NUT解的incidence relation对引力耦合常数呈现线性依赖关系。此外，还初步勾勒了在此类自对偶黑洞背景上构造零静态质量场的应用。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种实用的局部构造方法，将twistor理论与自对偶黑洞时空显式联系起来，并给出了闭合解析表达式。这为在自对偶黑洞背景下进一步研究零静态质量场以及相关量子方面的问题奠定了基础。

Abstract: In Penrose's nonlinear graviton construction, a self-dual spacetime arises as the moduli space of holomorphic twistor lines in curved twistor space, whose explicit parametrization is concretely given by the incidence relation. We demonstrate a concrete local construction of the incidence relations for self-dual black hole spacetimes in Kerr-Schild coordinates: Eguchi-Hanson, self-dual Taub-NUT, and self-dual Plebański-Demiański. This is facilitated by implementing the Dunajski-Mason recursion in the framework of Plebański's second heavenly equation, for which we explicitly find the Plebański scalar. Our results describe closed-form formulae. Notably, for the self-dual Taub-NUT solution, the exact incidence relation exhibits linear dependence on the gravitational coupling. The construction of zero-rest-mass fields on self-dual black hole backgrounds is briefly sketched as an application.

</details>


### [239] [Four-Fermion Condensates in Curved Spacetimes: A Functional Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07907)
*Stephon Alexander,Heliudson Bernardo,Jiatong Yan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从功能化角度统一区分了四费米相互作用的三种物理场景（微扰散射、真空NJL不稳定性、有限密度BCS配对），计算了标量通道NJL平均场在弯曲时空中的单圈局域贡献，导出了协变量子有效作用量，并在FLRW背景上得到了能量密度和压强，验证了与平直时空平均场计算的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四费米相互作用在粒子物理、多体系统和含费米子的引力理论中普遍出现，但同一局域算符在不同物理场景（微扰散射、NJL真空凝聚、BCS有限密度配对）中对应的相互作用通道、二次型核和量子态均不同，需要澄清这些区别并计算其在弯曲时空中的量子效应。

Method: 采用教学式功能方法：先在in-out泛函中展示态数据，再构造in-in（闭时路径）泛函以计算期望值。对NJL鞍点使用Hubbard-Stratonovich变换引入辅助场以移动费米子质量，通过parity偶的Dirac行列式生成局域体积项、曲率项和曲率平方项，导出协变量子有效作用量，并特化到空间平坦FLRW背景计算能量密度和压强。

Result: 得到了NJL平均场在弯曲时空中产生的局域体积、曲率和曲率平方算符，给出了协变量子有效作用量及FLRW背景下的能量密度和压强；常数凝聚极限与平直时空直接平均场计算结果一致。同时阐明了有限密度BCS配对所需的额外态数据和通道数据，并讨论了弯曲时空中的重整化条件。

Conclusion: 本文提供了四费米相互作用不同物理场景的统一功能化视角，完成了NJL平均场在弯曲时空中的单圈量子效应计算，确认了平直与弯曲时空的一致性，并为有限密度BCS系统及弯曲时空重整化问题的进一步研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Four-fermion interactions appear in effective descriptions of particle physics, many-body systems, and gravitational theories with fermions. Although the same local operator may enter perturbative scattering, a vacuum Nambu-Jona-Lasinio (NJL) instability, or finite-density Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) pairing, these regimes are distinguished by their interaction channels, quadratic kernels, and quantum states. We give a pedagogical functional account of these distinctions and compute the local one-loop contribution of a scalar-channel NJL mean field to the energy-momentum tensor in curved spacetime. We first display the state data in the in-out functional and construct the closed-time-path functional required for an in-in expectation value. For the NJL saddle, a Hubbard-Stratonovich field shifts the fermion mass, and the parity-even Dirac determinant generates local volume, curvature, and curvature-squared operators. We find the covariant quantum effective action before specializing to a spatially flat FLRW background and derive the corresponding energy density and pressure. The constant-condensate limit agrees with the direct flat-space mean-field calculation. We also explain which additional state and channel data are required for finite-density BCS pairing and comment on renormalization conditions in curved spacetimes.

</details>


### [240] [The quadratic growth of Krylov spread complexity in the BTZ black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09922)
*Aranya Bhattacharya,Mario Flory,Michal P. Heller,Emiliano Rizza,Tim Schuhmann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对热场双态（TFD）的Krylov扩散复杂度进行了批判性分析，基于配分函数构造在任意维度下从半经典全息配分函数重建边界复杂度，并针对BTZ黑洞和双尺度SYK模型进行验证。研究发现经典极限必须在复杂度重建后才能取，在二维CFT中观察到与体积复杂度不同的中间偏离和渐近二次增长行为，并将其匹配到由无穷多个外曲率不变量构成的广义复杂度=任意事物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息复杂度是理解黑洞内部增长的关键量，但此前仅在二维dilaton引力中有明确对应。需要一个维度无关的边界重建方案来捕获黑洞内部在更高维度下的增长行为。

Method: 基于配分函数构造Krylov扩散复杂度，利用半经典全息配分函数在任意维度重建边界复杂度；在双尺度SYK模型中对比精确解与半经典结果；将其应用于Hawking-Page温度以上的大中心荷二维CFT（对应BTZ黑洞），并构造由无穷级数外曲率不变量构成的广义复杂度=任意事物体进行匹配。

Result: 在SYK模型中证明经典极限须在复杂度重建后取，逐个Lanczos系数取极限会丢失关键信息。在BTZ对应的二维CFT中，发现复杂度早期呈二次增长，中间出现偏离，之后回到渐近二次增长而非标准的线性晚期行为。该行为可由无穷外曲率不变量的广义复杂度物成功匹配。

Conclusion: 该构造提供了从黑洞热力学到Krylov动力学的系统路径，可自然推广到更高维全息黑洞，为黑洞内部增长与边界复杂度建立更广泛的联系。

Abstract: The boundary quantity that captures the growth of black-hole interiors quantified by holographic complexity remains unknown beyond 2d dilaton gravity. We provide a critical analysis of a partition-function construction of Krylov spread complexity for thermofield-double states that provides a dimension-independent boundary reconstruction from semiclassical holographic partition functions, while developing the present dynamical and bulk construction for the BTZ saddle. In the double-scaled Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model, where exact and semiclassical results can be compared, we show that the classical limit is reliable only when taken after the complexity has been reconstructed; taking this limit at the level of individual Lanczos coefficients discards essential information. Applying the construction to a large-central-charge two-dimensional conformal field theory above the Hawking-Page temperature dual to a Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli black hole, we find an intermediate departure from early-time quadratic growth followed by behavior compatible with a return toward asymptotically quadratic growth, rather than the linear late-time behavior of the volume and the standard finite-functional complexity = anything class. We then match this boundary behavior to a generalized complexity = anything bulk object built from an infinite series of extrinsic-curvature invariants. The construction provides a systematic route from black-hole thermodynamics to Krylov dynamics and can naturally be extended to higher-dimensional holographic black holes.

</details>


### [241] [One gravity theory on the two-dimensional p-adic space and conjectures on $p$-adic AdS/CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07919)
*Feng Qu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过高维嵌入方法结合距离、测度和曲率的研究，在p进数上的二维空间中构建了引力理论，并通过与实数情形的对比（如dS、AdS和欧氏空间的曲率分别为正、负、零）验证了理论的合理性。最后提出两个猜想：p进AdS/CFT可在任意空间中计算且结果本质上相同，以及p进CFT本质上唯一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将引力理论推广到p进数空间，这一方向对于理解p进AdS/CFT对偶具有重要意义，可能为全息原理和量子引力研究提供新的数学框架。

Method: 采用高维嵌入方法（higher-dimensional embedding approach），结合距离（distance）、测度（measure）和曲率（curvature）的研究，在p进数上的二维空间中构建引力理论。同时基于已有文献补充计算，并通过与实数情形的对比来验证理论的合理性。

Result: 成功在p进数二维空间上构建了引力理论，并通过实数情形的对比验证了其合理性：dS空间的曲率为正、AdS空间的曲率为负、欧氏空间的曲率为零，这与实数情形一致。此外提出了两个关于p进AdS/CFT的猜想。

Conclusion: p进空间上的引力理论可以在高维嵌入框架下合理构建，且其几何性质与实数情形保持一致。作者进一步推测p进AdS/CFT对偶的普适性以及p进CFT的唯一性。

Abstract: By means of a higher-dimensional embedding approach, together with the study of distance, measure, and curvature, we construct a gravitational theory on the two-dimensional space over $p$-adic numbers. Before obtaining the final gravitational theory, we supplement some calculations based on existing literature and justify the reasonableness of the gravitational theory by comparison with the real-number case-for instance, the curvatures of de Sitter(dS), anti-de Sitter(AdS), and Euclidean spaces should be positive, negative, and zero, respectively. Finally, we put forward two conjectures: (1) $p$-adic AdS/CFT can be computed in arbitrary spaces, not only in $p$-adic AdS space($p$AdS), and the results are essentially the same; (2) $p$-adic CFT is essentially unique.

</details>


### [242] [Gauge transformations in Z-space in (anti)holomorphic sector of HS theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08062)
*D. A. Batyaev,A. V. Korybut*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了对(反)全纯生成系统的一致形变，旨在建立到Vasiliev理论(反)全纯截断的映射。新形式体系中，原先刚性定义的主场Λ可被d_z-精确的射影单形式移动，由此诱导高阶场重定义，并证明了此类场重定义的非平凡性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在将arXiv:2209.01966中的(反)全纯生成系统映射到Vasiliev理论的(反)全纯截断，为此需要引入一致形变。原体系中场Λ是刚性固定的，缺乏灵活性；作者希望允许Λ被射影单形式移动，从而在两种形式体系之间建立联系，并系统分析由此产生的场重定义及其可被规范消去的部分。

Method: 作者允许原体系中刚性定义的主场Λ被d_z-精确的射影单形式移动，全面描述了射影单形式的类并证明了相应的射影恒等式。对C中n阶的d_z-精确形式诱导(n+1)阶及更高阶的场重定义，给出了(n+1)阶场重定义的显式表达式。对零形中C的二阶规范变换进行了分析，识别出二阶场重定义中可被规范消去的子部分的充要条件。最后证明了Λ移动所诱导的场重定义是非平凡的。

Result: 论文提供了新的形变公式，允许Λ被d_z-精确的射影单形式移动；系统化了射影单形式类并证明相应射影恒等式；给出d_z-精确形式的n阶项诱导(n+1)阶场重定义的显式表达式；通过分析C的二阶规范变换，确定了可被规范消去的二阶场重定义分量的充要条件；并证明了由此产生的场重定义是非平凡的。

Conclusion: 该工作成功构建了一个一致形变的(反)全纯生成系统，通过允许Λ被d_z-精确射影单形式移动，为通向Vasiliev理论(反)全纯截断提供了映射。关键结论是场重定义非平凡性得到证明，且二阶可规范消去部分的充要条件得以明确，这为理解两种形式体系之间的深层联系奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We consider a consistent deformation of the (anti)holomorphic generating system of [arXiv:2209.01966], aiming to provide a map to the (anti)holomorphic truncation of the Vasiliev theory. In our new formulation, the previously rigidly defined master-field $Λ$ can be shifted by $\mathrm{d}_z$-exact projective one-forms. The class of projective one-forms is described comprehensively, and corresponding projective identities are proven. $\mathrm{d}_z$-exact forms of the order $n$ in $C$ induce field redefinition of order $(n+1)$ and beyond. Explicit expression for the $(n+1)$-th order field redefinitions are provided. An analysis of the gauge transformation of the second order in $C$ in zero-forms is performed, identifying necessary and sufficient conditions for the part of the field redefinition of the second order that can be gauged away. The field redefinitions induced by the shift of $Λ$ are proven to be nontrivial.

</details>


### [243] [3d $\mathcal N=4$ rank-zero mirror symmetry, TQFT interfaces, and Zagier duality of Nahm sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08780)
*Yutaka Yoshida*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从$(E_8,T_1)$ Nahm和构造了一个3d $\mathcal N=2$ $U(1)^8$ Chern-Simons物质理论$\mathcal T$，其A-twisted真空半指标精确再现了$T_{10}M_{\rm eff}(11,2)$的真空特征标。通过粒子-涡旋对偶和规范群化构造了第二理论$\mathcal T^\vee$，两者在3d $\mathcal N=4$ rank-zero镜像SCFT中流动。在$(\mathcal D,D_c)$边界条件下，$\mathcal T$的A-twisted Wilson环半指标与$\mathcal T^\vee$的B-twisted Wilson环半指标之间的镜像映射逐分量对应Zagier变换，实现了$(E_8,T_1)$与$(T_1,E_8)$ Nahm系统之间的Zagier对偶，并通过对偶界面给出模二元形式$Z_{\mathcal I}(\tau)=\chi_{(E_8)_1}(\tau)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将Zagier对偶（量子模形式对偶）具体实现为三维镜像对称和界面结构的物理构造，从而将数论中的Nahm求和与超共形场论中的半指标、镜像对称等物理概念建立直接联系。

Method: 从$(E_8,T_1)$ Nahm和出发构造3d $\mathcal N=2$ $U(1)^8$ Chern-Simons物质理论$\mathcal T$（level矩阵为$C_{E_8}$）；利用粒子-涡旋对偶和规范群化得到第二理论$\mathcal T^\vee$（level矩阵为$C_{E_8}^{-1}$）；通过精确超共形指标匹配验证其流向3d $\mathcal N=4$ rank-zero镜像SCFT；在$(\mathcal D,D_c)$边界条件下建立$A$-twisted与$B$-twisted Wilson环半指标间的镜像映射（即Zagier变换）；使用Bethe方程形式化验证$A/B$-twisted TQFT数据在定向反转和$E_8$ framing相因子下的一致性。

Result: A-twisted真空半指标精确再现真空特征标；四个不同的Wilson环半指标在验证阶数内与其他特征标一致。超共形指标匹配支持两理论流向3d $\mathcal N=4$ rank-zero镜像SCFT。镜像映射逐分量与Zagier变换一致，实现了$(E_8,T_1)$与$(T_1,E_8)$ Nahm系统的Zagier对偶。$A/B$-twisted TQFT数据在定向反转和$E_8$ framing相因子$e^{-2\pi\mathrm{i}/3}$下一致。对偶界面给出模二元形式$Z_{\mathcal I}(\tau)=\sum_i\chi_i(\tau)\chi_i^\vee(\tau)=\chi_{(E_8)_1}(\tau)$。

Conclusion: Zagier对偶被具体实现为三维镜像对称和界面结构的一部分，首次将$(E_8,T_1)$与$(T_1,E_8)$ Nahm系统之间的对偶关系通过3d $\mathcal N=2$/$\mathcal N=4$超对称场论的镜像机制加以物理实现和验证，揭示了模形式对偶与超共形场论之间深刻的联系。

Abstract: Starting from the $(E_8,T_1)$ Nahm sum for the vacuum character of $T_{10}M_{\rm eff}(11,2)$, we construct a three-dimensional (3d) $\mathcal N=2$ $U(1)^8$ Chern--Simons matter theory $\mathcal T$ with level matrix $C_{E_8}$. Its $A$-twisted vacuum half-index reproduces the character exactly, while four distinguished Wilson loop half-indices agree with the remaining characters to the orders checked. Applying particle--vortex duality and gauging yields a second 3d $\mathcal N=2$ Chern--Simons matter theory $\mathcal T^\vee$ with level matrix $C_{E_8}^{-1}$. Exact superconformal-index matching supports the claim that the two theories flow to 3d $\mathcal N=4$ rank-zero mirror SCFTs. For the $(\mathcal D,D_c)$ boundary condition, the mirror map between the $A$-twisted Wilson loop half-indices of $\mathcal T$ and the $B$-twisted Wilson loop half-indices of $\mathcal T^\vee$ coincides componentwise with the Zagier transformation, realizing a Zagier duality between the complete $(E_8,T_1)$ and $(T_1,E_8)$ Nahm systems. Using the Bethe-equation formalism, we show that the corresponding $A/B$-twisted TQFT data agree up to orientation reversal and the $E_8$ framing phase $e^{-2π\mathrm{i}/3}$. The duality interface gives the torus bilinear $Z_{\mathcal I}(τ)=\sum_iχ_i(τ)χ_i^\vee(τ)=χ_{(E_8)_1}(τ)$, where $\{χ_i\}$ and $\{χ_i^\vee\}$ are the corresponding five-component character bases. Thus Zagier duality is realized as part of a concrete 3d mirror and interface structure.

</details>


### [244] [Complex CFTs: Holography and Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08292)
*Luis Camargo-Carlos,Michael Gutperle*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过全息方法和二维共形场论研究了复共形场论及其边界（interface）。全息方面证明了复共轭CFT的Im-flip性质，构造了复Janus解并计算了全息可观测量，数值上获得了复RG流界面；CFT方面为自由玻色子CFT（含/不含背景电荷）构造了复界面，构建了边界态并计算了透射和反射系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究复共形场论和此类理论之间的界面，旨在理解复参数空间中CFT的结构与性质，以及在全息（AdS/CFT）框架下的对应描述。

Method: 全息方法（AdS/CFT对应）结合二维CFT技术：通过全息手段证明Im-flip性质，构造复Janus解计算全息可观测量，数值求解复RG流界面；在CFT侧利用边界态方法构造复界面，对自由玻色子CFT（有无背景电荷两种情形）计算透射与反射系数。

Result: 成功证明了复共轭CFT的Im-flip性质；构造了复Janus全息解并提取了可观测量；数值上获得了复RG流界面解。在CFT侧，为自由玻色子CFT（含与不含背景电荷）构建了复界面边界态，并在两种情形下分别计算了透射和反射系数。

Conclusion: 该工作系统地建立了复CFT界面在全息与CFT两侧的对应描述，证明Im-flip性质，提供复Janus解、复RG流界面以及自由玻色子CFT复界面的显式构造与散射系数计算，为理解复共形场论的结构与动力学奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: We investigate complex conformal field theories and interfaces between such theories, using methods of holography and two dimensional CFT. We use holography to prove the Im-flip property of complex conjugate CFTs. We construct a complex Janus solution and calculate some holographic observables. Complex RG-flow interfaces are obtained numerically. On the CFT side, complex interfaces are constructed for free boson CFTs with and without background charge. Boundary states are constructed, and transmission and reflection coefficients are calculated in both cases.

</details>


### [245] [The explicit Lorentz invariant QED pair production rates from superstrings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08712)
*J. X. Lu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Type II超弦理论中两个平行Dp膜（1≤p≤6）系统，利用开弦对产生机制推导出Schwinger效应的新途径，并在场论极限下系统地获得了不同维度下带电/反带电质量粒子（标量、旋量或矢量）的显式洛伦兹不变的QED对产生率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Schwinger效应描述了强电场下真空中粒子对的产生，本文旨在从弦理论的角度重新推导该效应，提供一个统一且系统的框架，能够在不同维度下获得各种自旋粒子（标量、旋量、矢量）的QED对产生率，且结果具有显式洛伦兹不变性。

Method: 考虑两个平行Dp膜（1≤p≤6）的系统，其中一个D膜携带最一般的恒定电磁背景场（允许对产生）。利用开弦对产生率，通过取场论极限，系统地推导出QED对产生率。这一方法将弦理论结果与量子电动力学结果联系起来。

Result: 从开弦对产生率出发，在场论极限下系统地获得了不同维度下带电/反带电质量粒子的显式洛伦兹不变的QED对产生率，涵盖了标量、旋量和矢量三种自旋类型的粒子。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条从弦理论到QED Schwinger效应的系统化新途径，能够在多种维度下统一地重现并推广带电粒子对的产生率，展示了弦理论与量子场论之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We present a new avenue to the usual Schwinger effect via the open string pair production for a system of two Dp branes with $1 \le p \le 6$ in Type II superstrings. The two Dp are placed parallel at a separation with one of them carrying the most general constant electromagnetic background allowed for the pair production. By taking the so-called field theory limit, we obtain systematically, from the open string pair production rate, the respective \textit{explicit Lorentz invariant} QED pair production rate in diverse dimensions for a pair of charged/anti-charged massive particles, which can be scalars, spinors or vectors.

</details>


### [246] [Carrollian Quantum Mechanics: Time-like, Space-like and Hybrid Sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08384)
*Mehdi Ahmadi-Jahmani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过系统地进行c→0极限下Klein-Gordon方程的极端相对论收缩，建立了Carroll量子力学的全面理论框架。该过程揭示了三个不同扇区——类时、类空和混合扇区，每个扇区由Carroll不变波动方程控制并伴随一致的几率诠释。类时扇区表现出新颖的时间隧穿现象，满足|T|²=1+|R|²关系；类空扇区则需快子色散关系，产生零范数态；混合扇区兼具两者特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carroll对称性作为光速c→0的极限对称性，近年受到越来越多关注。然而，关于Carroll量子力学的一致理论框架仍然缺乏，特别是如何从标准的相对论量子力学（如Klein-Gordon方程）出发，系统构建Carroll不变的量子力学描述，并理解其概率诠释和物理图像尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者对Klein-Gordon方程在c→0的极限下进行系统的极端相对论收缩（ultra-relativistic contraction），从数学上分解出三个不同扇区（类时、类空、混合），每个扇区导出Carroll不变的波方程。对每个扇区，推导对应的连续性方程、几率密度和流；并应用粒子在盒子中以及隧穿现象等典型量子系统进行验证分析。

Result: （1）类时扇区发现时间隧穿现象，满足|T|²=1+|R|²，反映Klein-Gordon范数的不定性质；（2）类空扇区需要快子色散关系，能量本征态的密度为零（零范数null态），在Carroll物理中因零几何结构获得物理意义；（3）混合扇区兼具两个扇区特征，有两个等价表述（可快子或非快子），可理解为非齐次Klein-Gordon方程；（4）对三个扇区均推导了连续性方程、几率密度和流。

Conclusion: 该工作构建了统一的Carroll量子力学理论框架，不仅从Klein-Gordon方程出发系统得到了三个Carroll不变的扇区及其概率诠释，还揭示了时间隧穿、零范数态等新颖物理现象。这些结果为Carrollian物理中的量子力学研究提供了坚实的理论基础，并开辟了在Carrollian几何/物理背景下探索量子现象的新方向。

Abstract: We develop a comprehensive theoretical framework for Carrollian quantum mechanics by performing systematic ultra-relativistic contractions of the Klein--Gordon equation in the limit $c \to 0$. This limiting process uncovers three distinct sectors---time-like, space-like, and hybrid---each governed by a Carroll-invariant wave equation and accompanied by a consistent probabilistic interpretation. The time-like sector exhibits a novel temporal tunneling phenomenon, characterized by the relation $|\mathcal{T}|^2 = 1 + |\mathcal{R}|^2$, which reflects the indefinite character of the Klein--Gordon norm. In the space-like sector, the presence of spatial propagation necessitates a tachyonic dispersion relation, which forces the density to vanish for energy eigenstates, yielding zero-norm (null) states that gain physical relevance within Carrollian physics due to the underlying null geometric structure. The hybrid sector combines features of both sectors and admits two distinct formulations, which may be either tachyonic or non-tachyonic, and can be interpreted as an inhomogeneous Klein--Gordon equation. For all three sectors, we derive the corresponding continuity equations, probability densities, and currents, and investigate canonical quantum systems---including the particle in a box and tunneling phenomena---within the Carrollian regime.

</details>


### [247] [Conformally mapping HKLL to flat space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08593)
*Nirmalya Kajuri,Rhitaparna Pal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS$_3$中共形耦合标量场的HKLL重构问题的两个共形图像。第一个映射将问题转化为爱因斯坦静态宇宙一半上的Dirichlet问题，再通过共形变换映射到由均匀加速圆形Dirichlet镜像包围的(2+1)维Minkowski空间区域中的无质量标量场。但全局HKLL问题对应的平直空间问题并非具有任意边界数据的通用局部镜像问题——镜像响应数据必须属于从规范AdS模式继承的受限谱类。作者在平直空间中内禀地表述并解决了这个受限镜像问题，并发现了一个有效的smearing函数本身不满足运动方程的例证。此外还考虑了AdS-Rindler楔重构，通过共形映射到平直空间的适当坐标系，给出了smearing函数爆发的简单解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HKLL重构是AdS/CFT对应中体态重构的核心方法，但直接构造HKLL积分核（smearing函数）在一般背景下具有技术困难。利用共形映射将AdS中的HKLL问题映射到更简单的几何（如平直空间）是一种有吸引力的途径。作者旨在理解这种共形映射方法在AdS_3背景下处理共形耦合标量的可行性与限制，特别是探究镜像问题与全局HKLL问题之间的谱受限关系，以及AdS-Rindler楔中smearing函数爆发的机制。

Method: 作者采用共形映射方法。首先将AdS_3中共形耦合标量的HKLL重构问题映射为半爱因斯坦静态宇宙（half-ESU）上的Dirichlet问题，再通过进一步共形变换将其映射到由均匀加速圆形Dirichlet镜像围成的Minkowski空间区域中的无质量标量问题。作者在平直空间中内禀地构建受限镜像问题的解，分析镜像响应数据的谱限制，并研究AdS-Rindler楔重构经共形映射后在平直空间的对应坐标表示。

Result: 作者发现：（1）全局HKLL问题对应的平直空间镜像问题不是通用局部镜像问题，而是受限于规范AdS模式谱类的特殊问题；（2）构造了该受限镜像问题的解，并找到一例有效的smearing函数本身不满足运动方程的核；（3）通过AdS-Rindler楔的共形映射到平直空间适当坐标系，给出了smearing函数在Rindler视界处爆发的简单几何解释。

Conclusion: 共形映射方法虽然可以将AdS中的HKLL重构问题转化为更简单几何中的问题，但得到的镜像问题具有受限谱结构，不能视为任意边界数据的通用局部问题。这一发现对理解HKLL重构的普适性以及AdS-Rindler楔重构中smearing函数的奇异性有重要启示。

Abstract: We study two conformal images of the HKLL reconstruction problem for a conformally coupled scalar on AdS$_3$. The first mapping is to a Dirichlet problem on one half of the Einstein static universe. A further conformal transformation maps this half-ESU problem to a massless scalar in a region of $(2+1)$-dimensional Minkowski space bounded by an uniformly accelerated circular Dirichlet mirror. However, the global HKLL problem, the resulting flat-space problem is not a generic local mirror problem with arbitrary boundary data. Rather, the mirror response data must belong to a restricted spectral class inherited from normalizable global AdS modes. We formulate and solve this restricted mirror problem intrinsically in flat space. In the process, we find an example of a valid smearing function which itself does not satisfy the equations of motion. We further consider AdS-Rindler wedge reconstruction. By conformal mapping to an appropriate coordinate system in flat space, we find a simple way to see the blow-up of the smearing function.

</details>


### [248] [Thermodynamic stability and geometric thermodynamics of charged black holes in Rastall-massive gravity under quintessence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08660)
*Mohamed Chabab,Samir Iraoui,Hicham Sriba*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Rastall引力与有质量引力相结合的框架下，推导了嵌入quintessence场的带电黑洞新解，并将其相关积分常数视为热力学变量，系统研究了该黑洞的热力学性质、稳定性及相变行为，发现其热力学行为类似于RN-AdS黑洞和范德瓦尔斯流体，并通过Quevedo度量验证了标准热力学与几何热力学的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Rastall引力和有质量引力组合框架下带电黑洞在quintessence场中的热力学行为，并寻找黑洞热力学临界相变的间接观测信号，尤其是通过光子球观测量建立与热力学相变之间的联系。

Method: 在渐近平坦时空中结合Rastall引力和有质量引力推导带电quintessence黑洞解，将quintessence场的积分常数作为热力学变量；分析不同极限情况；针对态参数ω_q=-2/3的黑洞进行详细的热力学性质和稳定性分析，研究影响参数对小-大黑洞相变的作用；建立碰撞参数及不稳定圆形光子轨道与热力学相变的联系；使用Quevedo度量进行热力学几何验证。

Result: 发现光子球观测量可为黑洞热力学临界转变提供间接观测特征；quintessence参数的有效贡献使整体热力学行为与带电RN-AdS黑洞及范德瓦尔斯流体类似；Quevedo度量的临界行为与标准热力学完全一致，相结构和发散点与热容及Gibbs自由能分析所识别的结果精确吻合。

Conclusion: 在Rastall-有质量引力框架下，quintessence场中的带电黑洞展现出与RN-AdS黑洞类似的热力学性质，其光子球观测量可成为热力学相变的潜在观测探针，且标准热力学与几何热力学描述相互验证、高度一致。

Abstract: In the present paper, we derive a new charged black hole solution embedded in a quintessence field within a framework combining the Rastall and massive gravity in an asymptotically flat spacetime. We interpret the integration constant associated with the structural properties of the quintessence field as a thermodynamical variable. In this context, several limiting cases have been explored with the aim to shed light on the role of the model parameters. Then, we analyse the thermodynamic properties and stability of the charged black holes in flat spacetime, where the quintessence field is characterized by the state parameter \( ω_q = -2/3 \). Detailed studies are perfomed to see how the various parameters affect phase transitions between small and large black holes. In particular, we establish a connection between the impact parameter and unstable circular photon orbits with the thermodynamic phase transitions. Our analysis suggest that the photon sphere observables could remarkably provide an indirect observational signature of black hole thermodynamic critical transitions. Generally, thanks to the effective contribution of the quintessence parameter, we found that the overall thermodynamic behavior basically resembles that of the charged RN--AdS black hole with thermal features analogous to of a Van der Waals fluid ones. Finally, we have checked these findings using thermodynamic geometry with the Quevedo metric. Our results showed that the critical behaviour derived from the standard thermodynamics is fully consistent with geothermodynamics description, where the phase structure and divergence points exactly coincide with those identified from heat capacity and Gibbs free energy analyses.

</details>


### [249] [An explicit four-corner dictionary for (2,p) minimal Liouville gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08669)
*A. Alexandrov,V. Belavin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了(2,p)极小刘维尔引力的四种代数描述及其对偶关系，系统地建立了它们在色散-free tau-函数层面上的显式对应，并通过广义Kontsevich积分变换将x↔y互换实现为树级框架变换，在(2,5)至(2,11)模型中验证了推导的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: (2,p)极小刘维尔引力存在四种代数描述（FM-y侧、FM-x侧、SC-y侧、SC-x侧），它们之间由对偶性相互关联，但此前这些描述之间的显式对应关系尚未被系统阐明，特别是在色散-free tau-函数的层面上。

Method: 作者在色散-free tau-函数层面建立了四种描述的显式对应：在FM侧分别基于A1流形（Q(y)=y²+u₁）和A_{p-1}流形，在SC侧分别基于Chebyshev曲线及其x↔y交换。FM-y和SC-y需要KdV时间与刘维尔耦合之间的共振变换，而另外两种不需要。通过三点函数比的归一化无关性验证CFT一致性，匹配全振幅给出单次插入因子，重现带符号的Verlinde矩阵。x↔y交换通过势为Φ^p/p的广义Kontsevich积分变换的树级框架变换实现。

Result: 四种公式化中归一化无关的三点比都与共形场论一致，匹配全振幅固定了单个每次插入因子，该因子重现了带符号的Verlinde矩阵。广义Kontsevich积分变换实现了两谱曲线描述间的x↔y交换，并在(2,5)至(2,11)系列模型中得到了验证。

Conclusion: 四种代数描述在色散-free层次上被统一，x↔y对偶的谱曲线间关系在亏格零时被严格建立为树级框架变换，为极小刘维尔引力的代数结构提供了完整的全景描述。

Abstract: (2,p) minimal Liouville gravity admits four algebraic descriptions interrelated by dualities. On the Frobenius manifold (FM) side the theory is built either on the $A_{1}$ manifold of $Q(y)=y^{2}+u_{1}$ ($y$-side) or on the $A_{p-1}$ manifold in the variable $x$ ($x$-side). On the spectral curve / topological recursion (SC) side the analogous choice is the Chebyshev curve and its $x \leftrightarrow y$ swap. FM-$y$, SC-$y$ require a resonance transformation between the KdV times and the Liouville couplings, while the other two do not. We work out the explicit correspondence between the four approaches at the level of the dispersionless tau-function. The normalisation-independent three-point ratios agree with conformal field theory in all four formulations, and matching the full amplitudes fixes a single per-insertion factor reproducing the signed Verlinde matrices. The $x \leftrightarrow y$ swap relating the two spectral curve descriptions, recently established in general by Dekinga, Shadrin and Verlinde, is realised at genus zero as a tree-level frame change of the generalised Kontsevich integral transform with potential $Φ^{p}/p$. The derivation is validated across (2,5)--(2,11) models.

</details>


### [250] [Running soft theorems in effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08792)
*Jonah Berean-Dutcher,Maria Derda,Riley Stewart*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了光子与π介子的软定理在一圈阶下含局域有效场论（EFT）修正的行为。在带维度五Pauli算符的大质量QED中，亚领头软光子定理的局域解析部分被硬积分区域重整化，而对数软项不受高维算符修正。类似结构也出现在双软π介子定理中：对数贡献系数完全由非线性sigma模型（NLSM）拉氏量决定，而手征EFT中四导数算符的Wilson系数出现在局域亚领头项中，其形式由重整化群方程决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索软定理在有效场论框架下受到高维算符修正的结构，理解哪部分软定理信息是普适的（由最低阶拉氏量决定），哪部分会接收高维算符的贡献，以及这些贡献如何受到重整化群方程约束。

Method: 采用有效场论方法，分别研究大质量QED（含维度五Pauli算符）和手征有效理论（Chiral EFT，含四导数算符）中的软定理，通过区分硬积分区域与软积分区域的贡献，分析局域解析项与对数软项的系数，并结合重整化群方程推导Wilson系数的形式。

Result: 发现光子与π介子软定理存在类比结构：对数的软发散贡献不受高维算符影响（分别由Pauli算符的RG平凡性和NLSM拉氏量决定），而局域亚领头项会接收高维算符的贡献，其形式由重整化群方程唯一确定。

Conclusion: 软定理的对数部分具有普适性，不受EFT截断效应干扰；而局域亚领头修正则与高维算符的重整化群行为紧密关联，揭示了软定理结构中"普适"与"算符依赖"部分的清晰划分。

Abstract: We study soft theorems for photons and pions with local effective field theory (EFT) corrections up to one loop order. In massive QED with a dimension-five Pauli operator, the local analytic part of the subleading soft photon theorem is renormalized by the hard integration region. On the other hand, as was previously known, the logarithmic soft terms do not receive corrections from higher-dimensional operators. We find that analogous structure appears in the double-soft pion theorem: the coefficients of logarithmic contributions are controlled entirely by the Nonlinear Sigma Model (NLSM) Lagrangian, while the Wilson coefficients of four-derivative operators in Chiral EFT appear in local subleading terms, in a form that is dictated by their RG equations.

</details>


### [251] [Holography in flipped AdS/$\mathbb{Z}$: Another approach to dS holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08837)
*Bin Chen,Zezhou Hu,Xin-Cheng Mao,Shan-Ming Ruan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文通过解析延拓构建了de Sitter空间的替代全息描述，提出了"翻转AdS/ℤ"(fAdS)空间及其对应的边界理论"翻转CFT"(fCFT)，并验证了fCFT₂的Cardy公式能复现dS₃和Kerr-dS₃中宇宙学视界的Bekenstein-Hawking熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的dS/CFT对应关系中存在许多微妙问题，作者希望探索解析延拓作为一种更建设性的路径来实现de Sitter全息性。

Method: 作者证明de Sitter空间中的量子场论与一种称为"翻转AdS/ℤ"(fAdS)的时空中的量子场论之间存在解析延拓关系；随后为一般渐近fAdS时空构建了外推字典，并计算了全息两点函数；最后通过fCFT₂的Cardy公式检验其对dS₃和Kerr-dS₃宇宙学视界熵的复现。

Result: 成功构造了fAdS时空的全息描述（fCFT），得到的全息两点函数与基于fCFT共形对称性的独立推导一致；fCFT₂的Cardy公式精确复现了纯dS₃和Kerr-dS₃中宇宙学视界的Bekenstein-Hawking熵。

Conclusion: fCFT可作为de Sitter物理的替代全息描述的出发点，为dS全息对偶提供了新的建设性途径。

Abstract: Motivated by the subtleties in the conventional dS/CFT correspondence, we explore analytic continuation as a constructive route to de Sitter holography. We show that quantum field theories in de Sitter space and in a spacetime that we call flipped $\mathrm{AdS}/\mathbb{Z}$ (fAdS) are related by analytic continuation. We then develop a holographic description of fAdS in terms of a boundary theory referred to as flipped CFT (fCFT). In particular, we construct the extrapolate dictionary for general asymptotically fAdS spacetimes and compute the holographic two-point functions, finding agreement with an independent derivation based on the conformal symmetry of fCFT. The analytic continuation relation between fAdS and dS further suggests that fCFT may provide a starting point for an alternative holographic description of de Sitter physics. As a nontrivial check of this picture, we show that the Cardy formula of fCFT$_2$ reproduces the Bekenstein--Hawking entropies of the cosmological horizons in both pure dS$_3$ and Kerr-dS$_3$.

</details>


### [252] [Numerical Study of Scalar Field Theory on the Fuzzy Onion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08855)
*Matej Hrmo,Samuel Kováčik,Juraj Tekel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过哈密顿蒙特卡洛数值模拟研究了模糊洋葱模型（由半径逐渐增大的同心模糊球组成的三维矩阵模型）上标量场论的相结构，识别了不同场相位，研究了动态相变现象，重建了相变线，并与模糊球的相结构进行了对比，最终识别了均匀相边界和临界边界两个关键边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊球模型是研究非交换几何上量子场论的标准框架，但其三维推广——模糊洋葱模型——的相结构尚不清楚。理解该模型的相结构对于探索更高维非交换几何中的物理现象具有重要意义。

Method: 采用哈密顿蒙特卡洛（Hamiltonian Monte Carlo）数值模拟方法，对模糊洋葱模型上的标量场论进行数值模拟，通过模拟数据分析其相结构、识别场相位、研究动态相变并重建相变线。

Result: 识别出了该理论的场相位，描述了动态相变现象，重建了相变线，并与模糊球的已建立相结构进行了对比。最终确定了两条相变边界：均匀相边界（uniform phase boundary）和介于无序相与非均匀相之间的临界边界（critical boundary）。

Conclusion: 模糊洋葱模型的相结构展现出比模糊球更为丰富的现象，包括动态相变和两条不同的边界（均匀相边界和临界边界），这为理解三维矩阵模型中非交换场论提供了新的见解。

Abstract: We study the behaviour of the scalar field theory on the fuzzy onion model -- a three-dimensional matrix model consisting of concentric fuzzy spheres of gradually increasing radii. We use a numerical method of Hamiltonian Monte Carlo simulations to study the phase structure of this theory. We identify the field phases, investigate and describe a phenomenon of dynamical phase transitions and attempt to reconstruct phase transition lines. We compare the results with the well-studied phase structure of the fuzzy sphere. Finally, we identify two boundaries on the phase transitions of the theory, a uniform phase boundary and a critical boundary between the disordered and non-uniform phase.

</details>


### [253] [Production of Primordial Black Holes in the Double D3-Brane - anti-D3-Brane Inflation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08870)
*S. -H. Henry Tye*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文将著名的D3-反D3膜暴涨模型自然扩展到具有两对D3-反D3膜的情形，揭示了弦理论中的一个新颖特征：当第一对膜湮灭时产生D1弦和F1弦，而剩余D3-反D3膜对的D3荷场强导致介电3-膜的形成——一种中性、有限尺寸的束缚态，可作为早期宇宙中原始黑洞的种子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对弦理论中成功暴涨模型（D3-反D3膜）的自然扩展，探索两对膜系统带来的新物理特征，并研究其对早期宇宙结构的可能影响。

Method: 基于Myers效应的非对易几何性质，分析剩余D3-反D3膜对的D3荷场强如何导致介电3-膜（3-膜与D1弦的中性有限尺寸束缚态）的形成。

Result: 介电3-膜表现为几乎没有压力的物质，其质量范围宽广，能够在早期宇宙中作为原始黑洞的种子。

Conclusion: 两对D3-反D3膜的暴涨模型不仅产生弦，还通过Myers效应形成介电3-膜，这些膜为早期宇宙中原始黑洞的形成提供了新的可能机制。

Abstract: A natural extension of the successful $D3$-${\bar D}3$-brane inflation model in string theory is one with two $D3$-${\bar D}3$-brane pairs, giving rise to a novel feature in string-theory. When the first pair annihilates, $D1$-strings and $F1$-strings are produced. Following the non-commutative geometric properties in string theory, it is shown by Myers that the presence of the $D3$-charge field strength of the remaining $D3$-${\bar D}3$-brane pair leads to the formation of dielectric $3$-branes -- neutral, finite-size bound states of $3$-branes with $D1$-strings. They behave as matter with little or no pressure. Spanning a wide range of masses, they seed primordial black holes in the early universe.

</details>


### [254] [Matrix Multiverses Meet Multiple Mythologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08972)
*Sidan A,Tom Banks,Willy Fischler*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个宇宙学模型，其中各种类型的渐近德西特（dS）宇宙及其半径R_n位于一个最大熵平坦p=ρ Friedmann-Robertson-Walker（FRW）宇宙中黑洞的内部。这些dS宇宙会以α_n R_n ln(R_n/δ_n)的时间尺度衰变并与最大熵宇宙达到平衡，该时间尺度远短于dS循环时间，且内部探测器无法判断自己是否属于这样的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在解决dS宇宙中量子态数目、衰变时间尺度以及"选择'自然地不自然'的物理常数"等问题，为某些宇宙学初始条件或物理常数的数学上不自然取值提供理论依据，可能与智慧生命存在条件（人择原理）相关。

Method: 构建一个嵌套模型：将渐近dS宇宙嵌入到一个最大熵平坦p=ρ FRW宇宙中黑洞的内部；利用以色列（Israel）结条件描述壳体与dS视界之间的初始距离δ_n；通过量纲分析和模型依赖参数α_n估算衰变/平衡时间尺度为α_n R_n ln(R_n/δ_n)。

Result: 模型表明dS宇宙具有大量量子态，其衰变与平衡时间呈对数形式依赖初始壳体距离，时间尺度远短于dS循环（recurrence）时间，后者在此模型中无意义；探测器无法区分自己是否处于此类嵌套结构中。

Conclusion: 该模型为物理常数或宇宙学初始条件具有数学上不自然取值提供了可行性基础，即那些"其他值无法导致任何形式的智慧生命"的取值可能是宇宙结构的必然结果，从而可以支持人择式论证。

Abstract: We present a model in which asymptotically de Sitter universes of various types, and de Sitter (dS) radii $R_n$, live in the interiors of black holes in a maximally entropic flat $p = ρ$ Friedmann-Robertson-Walker universe. These dS universes clearly have many quantum states. We argue that they decay and equilibrate with the maximal entropy universe on time scales of order $α_n R_n {\rm ln} (R_n /δ_n)$, where $α_n$ are model-dependent dimensionless constants and $δ_n$ are the initial distances between the shell, satisfying the Israel junction conditions, and the dS horizon. These are time-scales as viewed by a detector following a trajectory far from the would-be cosmological horizon. These times are all exponentially shorter than dS recurrence times, which have no meaning in this model. It is impossible for a detector inside one of the dS universes to determine whether it is actually part of such a structure. This model could be used as the basis for claiming that certain constants of nature or cosmological initial conditions were chosen to have mathematically unnatural values because other values could not lead to any form of intelligent life.

</details>


### [255] [Superselected ghost theory: perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09017)
*Bob Holdom*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于精确的鬼奇偶性的超选择规则，为鬼量子场论赋予概率解释。由于该鬼奇偶性在标准微扰展开的有限阶中不被保持，作者通过哈密顿量的相似变换 h=gHg⁻¹ 构造了保持鬼奇偶性的微扰理论（Z₂PT），该理论类似于旧式微扰理论（OFPT）但存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鬼量子场论中存在超选择规则，可以基于精确的鬼奇偶性赋予概率解释，但标准的微扰展开无法保持这一鬼奇偶性，因此需要一种新的微扰理论框架来在有限阶中保持该对称性并维持概率解释的有效性。

Method: 通过对哈密顿量施加相似变换 h=gHg⁻¹，得到展开式 h=h₀+h₁+h₂+...，从而构造保持鬼奇偶性的微扰理论（Z₂PT）。该展开与旧式微扰理论（OFPT）类似但有关键区别：超选择规则选取主值（principal value）处方来处理跨扇区能量分母，且该处方的选取由跃迁类型而非粒子种类决定。

Result: 在三阶，h₁ 与 h₂ 的乘积与 h₃ 结合可在远离消失分母时重现 OFPT 的结果。在四阶且 h₁=0 时，h₂² 与 h₄ 满足类似的关系。来自消失分母的接触项将 Z₂PT 与 OFPT 区分开来，但在这些阶次中不会改变局域原初 UV 发散。

Conclusion: Z₂PT 是标准微扰理论的有效替代方案，能够在有限阶保持鬼奇偶性并维持概率解释，同时与 OFPT 在非奇异区域保持一致，其差异（接触项）不影响局域紫外发散结构。

Abstract: A superselection rule based on an exact ghost parity can endow a ghost QFT with a probability interpretation. However, this ghost parity is not respected at finite order in the standard perturbative expansion. A ghost-parity-preserving perturbation theory (Z$_2$PT) is obtained through a similarity transformation of the Hamiltonian, $h=g H g^{-1}=h_0+h_1+h_2+...$. The resulting expansion is reminiscent of old-fashioned perturbation theory (OFPT) with some significant differences. The superselection rule selects the principal-value prescription for cross-sector energy denominators, with the prescription determined by the type of transition rather than the particle species. At third order, products of $h_1$ and $h_2$ combine with $h_3$ to reproduce OFPT away from vanishing denominators. At fourth order, when $h_1=0$, we show that $h_2^2$ and $h_4$ satisfy the analogous relation. Contact terms from vanishing denominators distinguish Z$_2$PT from OFPT but do not alter the local primitive UV divergences through these orders.

</details>


### [256] [Long-range multipartite entanglement in holographic gapless systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09056)
*Xiantong Chen,Xuanting Ji,Xin-Xiang Ju,Wen-Peng Li,Ya-Wen Sun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 利用全息对偶研究三维无带隙系统中强耦合下的多体纠缠，发现一类多体纠缠量共享由IR几何决定的大距离标度指数，在hyperscaling-violating IR几何中可实现衰减、对数、次广延乃至体率等不同标度行为，并分析了各向异性IR几何下的方向性纠缠行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无带隙量子系统支持任意长距离的关联，产生幂律长程纠缠。已有工作多关注两体纠缠（如全息纠缠熵），但多体纠缠在大距离下的行为尚不清楚，特别是在强耦合（无法用微扰论处理）和无带隙条件下，多体纠缠能否表现出尺度增长行为并得到增强是一个开放问题。
