Method: 采用全息对偶（AdS/CFT）框架，研究三维无带隙系统中强耦合下的多体纠缠。考虑hyperscaling-violating IR几何以实现不同的标度区域，并分析各向异性IR几何AdS3×R²。利用全息工具计算一类多体纠缠量（如多部纠缠度量）的大距离标度行为。

Result: 发现一大类多体纠缠量共享相同的前导大距离标度指数，该指数由IR几何决定。在hyperscaling-violating参数范围内，长距离多体纠缠可表现出衰减、对数增长、次广延增长甚至体率（volume law）行为。在各向异性几何AdS3×R²中，长程多体纠缠沿一个方向存活，而在横向带隙方向变为短程。

Conclusion: 全息无带隙相可以支持丰富且增强的长程多体纠缠，这为底层IR物理提供了一种非局域刻画。多体纠缠的大距离标度行为由IR几何唯一决定，表明多体纠缠可作为探测强耦合无带隙系统IR物理的有效工具。

Abstract: Gapless quantum systems support correlations over arbitrarily long distances, giving rise to power-law long-range entanglement. We investigate long-range entanglement at strong coupling for three-dimensional gapless systems using holography, asking whether multipartite entanglement can exhibit scale-growing behavior and become enhanced at large distances. We show that a broad class of multipartite entanglement quantities share the same leading large-distance scaling exponent determined by the IR geometry. To realize different scaling regimes, we consider hyperscaling-violating IR geometries. Depending on the parameters, long-distance multipartite entanglement can decay, become logarithmic, grow subextensively, or reach a volume law. We also analyze an anisotropic IR geometry \(\mathrm{AdS}_{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{2}\), where long-range multipartite entanglement survives along one direction but becomes short-ranged in the transverse gapped directions. These results show that holographic gapless phases can support rich and enhanced long-range multipartite entanglement, providing a nonlocal characterization of the underlying IR physics.

</details>


### [257] [Holographic multipartite entanglement dynamics in AdS$_3$-Vaidya](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09304)
*Xin-Xiang Ju,Ya-Wen Sun,Yang Zhao,Hao-Ran Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过AdS3/CFT2对偶研究全局淬火后全息热化过程中多体纠缠的动态重组。使用n方信息、Markov gap和三体multi-entropy等探针，发现多体纠缠的空间范围先扩张后收缩，涉及更多方数的纠缠弛豫更慢，且纠缠在局域热化后仍可能保持增强状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子多体系统中多体纠缠在远离平衡态（如全局淬火）后的动态重组机制，尤其是它在全息热化过程中的时空演化行为，是量子信息与引力全息对偶交叉领域的核心问题。

Method: 在AdS3/CFT2框架下，选择n方纠缠楔完全连通而所有少方纠缠楔断开的全息构型，利用(-1)^n I_n（n方信息）排除少方贡献以探测集体多体纠缠；同时计算Markov gap和genuine tripartite multi-entropy作为三方纠缠的补充探针。

Result: 多体纠缠空间范围随时间先扩张后收缩；不同方数的纠缠在早期时间尺度相当，但更多方数纠缠弛豫更慢，体现从短距离到长距离的瞬态传播。Markov gap在局域热化后仍可保持增强，而genuine tripartite multi-entropy呈非单调演化并回归真空值。

Conclusion: 全局淬火会跨空间尺度重新分配纠缠并重组其多体结构，多体纠缠在热化过程中遵循先扩展后收缩的动力学，且不同方数的纠缠具有不同的弛豫时间尺度，揭示了全息热化中多体纠缠重组的丰富结构。

Abstract: We study how multipartite entanglement is dynamically reorganized during holographic thermalization following a global quench in AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$. We first use the $n$-partite information $(-1)^n I_n$ to probe collective multipartite entanglement in holographic configurations where the full $n$-region entanglement wedge is connected while all fewer-party ones are disconnected, thereby excluding fewer-party contributions. The spatial range of multipartite entanglement first expands and then contracts as the system approaches its late-time locally thermal state. Entanglement involving different numbers of parties develops on comparable early-time scales, while the entanglement that involves more parties relaxes more slowly, revealing a transient propagation from shorter to longer spatial distances. We further compute the Markov gap and the genuine tripartite multi-entropy as complementary probes of tripartite entanglement. The Markov gap can remain enhanced after local thermalization, whereas the genuine tripartite multi-entropy undergoes a nonmonotonic evolution and returns to its vacuum value for the adjacent tripartition considered in this work. These results show that a global quench redistributes the entanglement across spatial scales and reorganizes its multipartite structure.

</details>


### [258] [Nonperturbative Stabilization of D-Instantons in the Bosonic IIB Matrix Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09598)
*Yu-An Chen,Hikaru Kawai,Henry Liao,Cheng-Tsung Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究玻色型IIB（IKKT）矩阵模型中D瞬子位置p^(i)_μ（d个厄米矩阵的对角分量）的坍缩问题，证明其单圈有效势导致的坍缩是单圈截断的假象，实际非坍缩是非微扰效应。通过将两体扇区分解为N=2副本，精确计算U(2)模型发现两个D瞬子不会坍缩。作者引入保持对角与非对角扇区分离的规范固定方案，以及辅助鬼-鬼BRST构造处理剩余U(1)^N对称性，生成新的鬼四腿顶点。该Faddeev-Popov行列式支持系统性大分离展开，将有效势组织成多体分解。精确N=2配分函数在有限分离处有限，但其Lorenz规范形式在分离量级为1时出现负区域，归因于Lorenz规范的Gribov歧义，并通过最大对规范（包含微扰真空的经典框架）解决，其中短程力有限且排斥，两体势在有限分离处形成稳定极小值。结果与p^{(i)}_μ稳定非坍缩分布一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色型IIB（IKKT）矩阵模型是M理论和非微扰弦理论的重要候选，但其对角分量（D瞬子位置）的单圈有效势会将其拉向单点坍缩，这一"坍缩问题"在单圈阶下看似不可避免。作者质疑该结论，认为坍缩可能只是单圈截断的人为产物，真实物理可能是非坍缩的，这关系到矩阵模型作为离散时空/瞬间子凝聚描述的有效性。

Method: 作者首先指出两体扇区在N×N模型中因子化为N=2副本，因此精确两体相互作用可由U(2)模型完全捕获。然后采用一种保持对角与离对角扇区区分的U(N)规范固定方案，并通过辅助鬼-鬼（auxiliary-ghost）BRST构造处理剩余U(1)^N对称性，该构造产生新的鬼四腿顶点。由此得到的Faddeev-Popov行列式支持系统性大分离展开，将有效势组织为两体、三体及更高体的多体分解。随后精确求解N=2配分函数，发现Lorenz规范存在Gribov歧义导致的负区域问题，遂改用最大对规范（maximal diagonal gauge）解决，在该规范下短程力为有限且排斥的，两体势在有限分离处产生稳定极小值。

Result: 精确U(2)模型显示两个D瞬子在有限分离处不会坍缩，两体势存在稳定的极小值。Lorenz规范在分离量级约1处出现负概率区域，该问题源于Gribov歧义，通过最大对规范消除后，短程力变为有限且排斥。上述结果表明矩阵模型对角分量的非坍缩是真实的非微扰效应，而非微扰（全圈）两体相互作用已包含这一信息。

Conclusion: D瞬子位置的非坍缩是非微扰效应，单圈有效势的坍缩结论是截断的人为产物。两体层次精确成立的非坍缩与稳定极小值表明矩阵模型可能存在稳定的瞬间子凝聚分布；完整的多体非坍缩分布和详细统计需要更高体势的计算，留待后续工作。

Abstract: We study the bosonic type IIB (IKKT) matrix model and the fate of the D-instanton positions $p^{(i)}_μ$, the diagonal components of the $d$ Hermitian matrices, whose one-loop effective potential infamously drives them to a single point. We argue that this collapse is an artifact of the leading (one-loop) truncation, while the actual non-collapse of the $p^{(i)}_μ$ is a nonperturbative effect: it is invisible at one loop but already present in the exact (all-loop) two-body interaction. Since the two-body sector of the $N\times N$ model factorizes into copies of $N=2$, this interaction is captured exactly by the $\mathrm{U}(2)$ model, and we find that the two D-instantons do not collapse onto each other. To set up the computation, we gauge-fix the $\text{U}(N)$ symmetry in a way that keeps the diagonal and off-diagonal sectors distinct, and we handle the residual $\mathrm{U}(1)^N$ symmetry with an auxiliary-ghost BRST construction. This construction generates a new ghost four-leg vertex; the resulting Faddeev-Popov determinant admits a systematic large-separation expansion that organizes the effective potential into a many-body decomposition, separating the interaction into two-body, three-body, and higher-body contributions. The exact $N=2$ partition function is finite at finite separation; its naive Lorenz-gauge form develops a negative region at separations of order one, which we trace to a Gribov ambiguity of the Lorenz gauge and resolve with the maximal diagonal gauge--the classical frame containing the perturbative vacuum--where the short-distance force is finite and repulsive, so that the two-body potential develops a stable minimum at finite separation. These results are consistent with a stable, non-collapsed distribution of the $p^{(i)}_μ$; establishing the detailed distribution and full $N$-body non-collapse requires the higher-body potentials and is left to future work.

</details>


### [259] [Schwinger-Keldysh effective field theory of type-B Goldstone: near-diagonal geometry and Berry term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09776)
*Pei Zheng,Mei Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了有限温度下类型B戈德斯通模式的Schwinger-Keldysh有效场论，利用近对角几何将r型场解释为物理戈德斯通构型、a型场解释为切向位移，通过Berry曲率的转移（transgression）直接获得类型B戈德斯通所必需的Berry项，并验证该Berry转移与动力学KMS条件相容。在此框架下，对陪集流形上全局定义的张量进行分类以构建保守、耗散和噪声项，并在存在耗散时计算了两个具体模型示例中戈德斯通模式的色散关系和两点关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型B戈德斯通模式与类型A不同，其低能有效场论需要Berry项来描述动力学，且需要正确处理有限温度下的实时（Schwinger-Keldysh）轮廓结构。本文旨在建立一种几何上自洽的有限温度有效场论框架，不仅涵盖保守动力学，还能系统地纳入耗散和噪声效应，从而统一描述类型B戈德斯通模式在有限温度下的完整物理行为。

Method: 本文采用Schwinger-Keldysh（SK）路径积分形式的有效场论方法。核心创新在于引入"近对角几何"（near-diagonal geometry），其中r型场对应物理戈德斯通配置，a型场对应陪集流形上的切向位移。Berry项通过对Berry曲率的transgression直接获得，并证明其满足动力学KMS条件。作者进一步对陪集流形上全局定义的张量进行分类，以此系统构建保守项、耗散项和噪声项。最后选取两个具体模型示例，在耗散存在下计算色散关系和两点关联函数。

Result: 成功建立了类型B戈德斯通模式的有限温度SK有效场论框架。证明了Berry项的transgression构造与动力学KMS条件相容，表明该几何框架在有限温度实时动力学中自洽。通过对陪集流形上张量的系统分类，完整的保守-耗散-噪声结构得以构建。两个具体模型示例的计算展示了在耗散影响下戈德斯通模式的色散关系如何修正，以及相应的两点关联函数的具体形式。

Conclusion: 该工作为类型B戈德斯通模式在有限温度下的实时动力学提供了完整的有效场论描述，确立了Berry项的几何来源（Berry曲率的transgression），并证明其与涨落-耗散结构（KMS条件）兼容。这一框架为研究凝聚态和量子场论中具有类型B戈德斯通模式的系统（如磁性系统、超流体等）在有限温度下的动力学性质提供了统一的工具。

Abstract: In this work, we formulate a finite temperature Schwinger-Keldysh effective field theory for type-B Goldstone modes. In particular, we organize the required two time contour structure by a near-diagonal geometry in which the r-type field is interpreted as the physical Goldstone configuration and the a-type field is identified as corresponding tangent displacement. Within this geometric viewpoint, the Berry term essential for type-B Goldstones is obtained directly through the transgression of the Berry curvature. Moreover, we show that this exact Berry transgression is compatible with dynamical KMS condition. In order to construct the conservative, dissipative and noise sectors, we classify possible tensors globally defined on the coset manifold. Based on this framework, we discuss in detail two concrete model examples. The dispersion relations of the Goldstone modes and the associated two-point correlation functions are calculated in the presence of dissipation.

</details>


### [260] [Thermal Origin of Black Hole Quasinormal Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09797)
*D. Giataganas,G. F. Giudice,A. Kehagias,F. Quevedo,A. Riotto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明黑洞合并后产生的准正则模（QNMs）具有精确的热力学解释：探测弦在近光子环几何中传播时，其世界面上会诱导出由光子环Lyapunov指数决定温度的Rindler视界，黑洞QNM由此作为热激发涌现出来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索黑洞QNM更深层的物理本质，将引力波特征频率与热力学框架联系起来，理解光子环在其中的核心作用。

Method: 通过探测弦在近光子环几何中的传播分析诱导Rindler视界；从微观（不稳定横向世界面涨落）和宏观（开放热量子系统的因果响应函数极点结构）两个互补视角推导QNM谱。

Result: 确立了黑洞QNM的精确热力学诠释：黑洞的特征振铃是位于光子环上的热系统的延迟响应，其温度由光子环的Lyapunov指数决定。

Conclusion: 黑洞振铃本质上是光子环上热系统的回应，这为理解黑洞QNM提供了统一的热力学框架，连接了引力波天文学与量子热力学。

Abstract: When a black hole rings after a merger, it emits gravitational waves at characteristic frequencies known as quasinormal modes (QNMs). In the eikonal limit, these modes are governed by the unstable circular light orbits that form the photon ring. In this work, we demonstrate that the ringing of a black hole has a precise thermal interpretation. A probe string propagating in the near-ring geometry acquires an induced Rindler horizon on its worldsheet, with a temperature set by the Lyapunov exponent of the photon ring. Out of this structure, the black hole QNMs emerge as thermal excitations, so that the characteristic ringing of a black hole is the retarded response of a thermal system living on the photon ring. We explicitly derive the QNM spectrum from two complementary perspectives: microscopically, via unstable transverse worldsheet fluctuations, and macroscopically, through the pole structure of the causal response function of an open thermal quantum system.

</details>


### [261] [Closed-form thermal threshold functions for the proper-time renormalisation group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09803)
*Daniele Rizzo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为有限温度正常时滞重整化群（PTRG）推导出了热阈值函数的闭式表达式，通过Poisson重求和将Matsubara求和转化为快速收敛的卷绕数级数，并利用代数恒等式将高阶阈值函数归约到同一闭式。该结果使LPA和LPA'近似与既有结果一致，并首次实现了固定点作为正则化器连续函数的追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限温度正常时滞重整化群框架中，热阈值函数此前只能通过逐Matsubara模式数值求值，缺乏解析闭式表达。这一限制阻碍了对重整化群流方程的理论分析，也限制了固定点作为正则化器连续函数的研究。

Method: 对标准单参数正则化器族，采用Poisson重求和将Matsubara求和转化为由修正Bessel函数组成的快速收敛卷绕数级数；通过单一代数恒等式将任意高阶阈值函数归约到同一闭式（仅在核参数上平移）。尖端正则化器作为m→∞精确极限恢复，并分解为场依赖与纯热两部分。基于该闭式构建LPA和LPA'近似，并与Wetterich方程优化正则化器交叉验证。

Result: LPA和LPA'近似与既有结果一致；将零温度反常维数构造推广到有限温度；首次实现了固定点作为正则化参数的连续函数追踪，结果显示其变化平滑且有界，整个参数线上无特殊或病态点。

Conclusion: 该工作为PTRG热阈值函数提供了统一的解析框架，克服了数值逐模式求积的局限，并开辟了在连续正则化参数空间中系统研究固定点行为的途径。

Abstract: We derive closed-form expressions for the thermal threshold functions of the finite-temperature proper-time renormalisation group (PTRG): until now these have been evaluated numerically, Matsubara mode by Matsubara mode. For the standard one-parameter regulator family, Poisson resummation of the Matsubara sum yields a rapidly convergent winding-number series of modified Bessel functions, and a single algebraic identity reduces every higher threshold function to this same closed form at a shifted kernel parameter. The sharp proper-time regulator, recovered as the exact $m\to\infty$ endpoint of the family with a controlled $O(1/m)$ approach, factorises into a field-dependent and a purely thermal piece.
  Built on them, the local potential approximation (LPA) and its refinement to a running anomalous dimension (LPA$'$) for the $O(N)$-symmetric theory reduce to established results, all cross-checked against the exact Wetterich equation with the optimised regulator. Two findings go beyond reduction. First, the known zero-temperature anomalous-dimension construction is extended here to finite temperature. Second, because the closed form holds for any real regulator parameter, the refined truncation's fixed point can for the first time be tracked as a continuous function of the regulator, rather than at a handful of isolated points, and is found to vary smoothly and remain bounded, with no special or pathological point anywhere on the line.

</details>


### [262] [On the Renormalization in Conformal Quantum Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09858)
*Ioseph L. Buchbinder,Petr M. Lavrov,Thomas M. Sangy,Ilya L. Shapiro*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了弯曲时空中经典共形理论的单圈发散问题，该问题在理论具有规范不变性时具有非平凡性。作者利用BRST对称性证明了一圈共形不变重整化的可行性，并将结果推广到共形量子引力及共形引力与共形物质场耦合的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典共形理论在弯曲时空中的单圈发散问题在规范不变情况下具有非平凡性，因为规范固定项和鬼场作用量本身并不共形。作者旨在利用BRST对称性严格证明共形不变重整化的可行性，并将其推广到共形量子引力领域。

Method: 利用BRST对称性，证明规范固定项和鬼场对有效作用量共形变化的贡献相互抵消，从而保证共形不变的重整化。作者扩展了早期关于外部引力场中共形物质场的结果，并将其应用于共形量子引力（Weyl平方引力），同时给出了半经典理论中一圈共形不变发散的详细一般性证明。

Result: 通过BRST对称性证明了共形量子引力（包括Weyl平方引力）的一圈共形不变重整化是可行的，尽管其规范固定比一般情形更为复杂。同时给出了半经典理论中一圈共形不变发散的详细一般性证明。

Conclusion: 共形不变重整化可以通过BRST对称性在圈图中实现，但共形反常仍出现在有效作用量的有限部分中。该框架可推广到共形量子引力及其与共形物质场耦合的情形。

Abstract: One-loop divergences in classically conformal theory in curved spacetime is a nontrivial issue if the theory under consideration possesses gauge invariance. In this case, quantization involves introducing the gauge-fixing term and the action of ghosts, both of which are not conformal. The formal proof of the conformal invariant renormalizability in this situation, including interacting theories, has been given in the paper from 1984 by one of the present authors. Owing to BRST symmetry, the contributions of the gauge-fixing and ghost sectors to the conformal variation of the effective action cancel each other. This cancellation does not hold in the finite part of the one-loop and higher-loops effective action, that results in the anomaly. In this paper, we extend the early results on conformal matter fields in external gravitational field and apply them to conformal quantum gravity, including the case of conformal gravity coupled to other conformal matter fields. The gauge-fixing in the Weyl-squared gravity is more complicated, nevertheless, the proof of the one-loop conformal invariant renormalization using BRST symmetry is possible. As a preliminary to conformal quantum gravity, we also present a detailed general proof of conformal invariant one-loop divergences in the corresponding semiclassical theory.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [Obstructions to lifting quaternionic torus actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07597)
*Panagiotis Batakidis,Ioannis Gkeneralis*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将全局和局部四元数环面作用提升到主四元数环面丛上的问题。作者首先建立了Hattori--Yoshida障碍理论框架的四元数类比，给出了提升存在的充要判据，并证明了所有提升（模规范共轭）由非交换H^1集分类。接着将该全局理论应用于局部四元数环面作用，通过拉回到轨道空间的万有覆盖来解扭局部作用，定义了规范值非交换下降缺陷，证明了局部作用可提升当且仅当全局提升障碍消失且下降缺陷可平凡化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的环面作用提升理论从交换（abelian）情形推广到非交换的四元数环面（Q^k=(Sp(1))^k）情形。对于局部环面作用的提升问题，缺乏系统性的障碍理论框架，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: （1）建立Hattori--Yoshida障碍理论框架的四元数类比，利用Borel构造X_G=EG×_G X诱导的限制映射刻画提升存在性；（2）引入规范群G(P)≅Γ(Ad(P))中的因子集（factor set）来度量伪提升（pseudo-lift）与真正作用的偏差，将提升判据转化为该因子集的可平凡化问题；（3）将全局理论应用于局部作用：拉回到轨道空间的万有覆盖解扭局部Q^n作用，定义规范值非交换下降缺陷（descent defect），利用其交叉余循环恒等式和变换律给出局部作用提升的充要条件。

Result: 获得了全局四元数环面作用可提升的充要判据（因子集可平凡化），并且证明了所有提升模规范共轭的集合由一个带基点（pointed）的非交换H^1集分类。对于局部作用，证明了其可提升当且仅当全局提升障碍消失且非交换下降缺陷可平凡化。在交换情形下该理论退化为经典的局部环面作用提升障碍理论。

Conclusion: 本文建立了四元数（非交换）环面作用提升的系统性障碍理论，统一处理了全局和局部两种情形，并自然推广了经典的Hattori--Yoshida理论及局部环面作用提升理论。

Abstract: We study the problem of lifting global and local quaternionic torus actions to principal quaternionic torus bundles. Let \(Q^k=(\operatorname{Sp}(1))^k\cong (S^3)^n\), let \(G\) be a compact Lie group acting on a connected, locally finite CW complex \(X\), and let \(Q^k\longrightarrow P\longrightarrow X\) be a principal \(Q^k\)-bundle. We first formulate a quaternionic analogue of the obstruction-theoretic framework of Hattori--Yoshida. The existence of a lifted \(G\)-action implies that the isomorphism class of \(P\) lies in the image of the restriction map induced by the Borel construction \(X_G=EG\times_GX\). In particular, the second Chern class admits an equivariant extension. Once a continuous pseudo-lift has been chosen, its failure to define a genuine action is measured by a factor set with values in the generally nonabelian gauge group \(\mathcal G(P)\congΓ(\operatorname{Ad}(P))\). We obtain a necessary and sufficient lifting criterion in terms of the trivializability of this factor set, and show that, once a single lift exists, the set of all lifts modulo gauge conjugacy is classified by a pointed nonabelian \(H^1\)-set.
  We then apply this global theory to local quaternionic torus actions. Pulling back to the universal covering of the orbit space, untwists a local \(Q^n\)-action and produces a globally defined action on the pulled-back manifold. A preliminary lift of this global action need not be compatible with the deck transformations. We define a gauge-valued nonabelian descent defect, establish its crossed-cocycle identities and transformation law, and prove that the original local action lifts if and only if the global lifting obstruction vanishes and the descent defect is trivializable. In the abelian case, these constructions reduce to the classical obstruction theory for lifts of local torus actions.

</details>


### [264] [The canonical submersion of $\mathcal{S}$-manifolds and transverse Kähler-Einstein structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08072)
*Ioannis Chrysikos*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了$(2n+s)$维$\mathcal{S}$-流形（配备特征联络）的完整（holonomy）性质。$\mathcal{S}$-流形将Sasakian几何推广到任意CR余维数$s\geq 1$，当被视为具有平行斜挠率（parallel skew-torsion）的几何时，其完整群具有可约、不可分解的特殊类型。文中还证明任意$\mathcal{S}$-流形可局部定义为Kähler流形上的Riemannian淹没，建立了底空间Kähler-Einstein条件与全空间广义$\eta$-Einstein条件之间的双射对应，并将经典Sasakian对应推广到任意CR余维数。作为对Ricci平坦平行斜挠率度量联络的应用，证明了$\mathcal{S}$-流形是${\rm Ric}^{\nabla}$-平坦的当且仅当它是具有Einstein常数$\lambda=4s$的横截Kähler-Einstein流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sasakian几何在低维CR余维数下已被充分研究，但其到更高CR余维数的推广——即$\mathcal{S}$-流形——的完整性质尚未被系统探索。由于$\mathcal{S}$-流形可与具有平行斜挠率的几何相联系，研究其完整群结构对于理解这类广义几何的曲率性质和Einstein条件具有重要意义，同时也能将经典的Sasakian对应关系推广到任意CR余维数。

Method: 本文采用特征联络（characteristic connection）框架研究$\mathcal{S}$-流形的完整性质。具体方法包括：分析$\mathcal{S}$-流形的完整群分解结构，证明其可约、不可分解且属于特殊类型；构造局部Riemannian淹没到Kähler流形，建立曲率关系；通过底空间Kähler-Einstein条件与全空间$\eta$-Einstein条件建立双射对应；利用横截Kähler几何与特征叶状结构联系$\eta$-Einstein条件；最终通过Ricci平坦条件${\rm Ric}^{\nabla}$-flat推导出横截Kähler-Einstein与Einstein常数$\lambda=4s$的等价刻画。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 任意CR余维数$s\geq 1$的$\mathcal{S}$-流形提供具有平行斜挠率的几何例子，其完整群可约、不可分解且为特殊类型；(2) 任意$\mathcal{S}$-流形存在局部定义的到Kähler流形上的Riemannian淹没；(3) 底空间Kähler-Einstein条件与全空间广义$\eta$-Einstein条件之间存在双射对应；(4) $\eta$-Einstein $\mathcal{S}$-流形与横截Kähler-Einstein度量之间存在双射，推广了经典Sasakian对应；(5) $\mathcal{S}$-流形是${\rm Ric}^{\nabla}$-平坦的当且仅当它是横截Kähler-Einstein且Einstein常数$\lambda=4s$，并以Sasakian例子加以说明。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了$\mathcal{S}$-流形的holonomy性质，证明其在具有平行斜挠率的几何中占据特殊地位。通过建立Kähler-Einstein与$\eta$-Einstein之间的双射对应，以及Ricci平坦性与横截Kähler-Einstein的等价关系，将Sasakian几何的核心结论系统推广到任意CR余维数，为高性能几何和挠率几何的研究提供了统一框架。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the study of the holonomy properties of $(2n+s)$-dimensional $\mathcal{S}$-manifolds equipped with their characteristic connection. These structures generalize Sasakian geometry to higher CR-codimensions and, when viewed as geometries with parallel skew-torsion, share many holonomy features with the Sasakian case. We show that $\mathcal{S}$-manifolds of arbitrary CR-codimension $s\geq 1$ provide examples of geometries with parallel skew-torsion whose holonomy is reducible, indecomposable, and of special type. We also deduce that any $\mathcal{S}$-manifold admits a locally defined Riemannian submersion over a Kähler manifold. We describe the corresponding curvature relations and establish a bijective correspondence between the Kähler-Einstein condition on the base space and a generalized $η$-Einstein condition on the total space. As every $\mathcal{S}$-manifold comes with a characteristic foliation whose transverse geometry is Kähler, it is natural to relate the $η$-Einstein condition to the transverse metric, leading to a bijection between $η$-Einstein $\mathcal{S}$-manifolds and transverse Kähler-Einstein metrics, extending the classical Sasakian correspondence to arbitrary CR-codimensions. As an application to Ricci-flat metric connections with parallel skew-torsion, we prove that an $\mathcal{S}$-manifold is ${\rm Ric}^{\nabla}$-flat if and only if it is transverse Kähler-Einstein with Einstein constant $λ=4s$. An illustration of this characterization is presented by a Sasakian example.

</details>


### [265] [The sharp volume gap for Kähler manifolds with positive Ricci curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08193)
*Chi Li,Minghao Miao,Kewei Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了一个尖锐的体积间隙估计：在Ricci曲率满足Ric(ω)≥(n+1)ω的n维紧致Kähler流形中，若它不是复射影空间P^n，则其体积至多为quadric超曲面Q^n或乘积空间P^1×P^{n-1}上的Kähler-Einstein度量体积，且等号成立当且仅当等距于这两种情形之一。同时给出了K半稳定toric log Fano对的尖锐体积间隙估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在填补Kähler几何中关于体积间隙（volume gap）的精确估计空白。此前已知紧致Kähler流形在正Ricci曲率条件下体积有上界（由Bishop-Gromov比较定理给出），但针对具有更多结构（Kähler、复射影）的情形，需要更精确的常数和等号情形刻画。此外，将该结果推广到K半稳定toric对数Fano对，为代数几何中的K稳定性理论提供了体积估计的新工具。

Method: 通过比较几何与复代数几何方法，利用Ric(ω)≥(n+1)ω的强曲率条件，结合Kähler流形的复结构与射影嵌入，证明体积间隙的尖锐常数。等号情形的刻画通过分析极端情形下的几何结构来实现，将体积达到上界的流形分类为quadric Q^n或P^1×P^{n-1}上的Kähler-Einstein度量。对于K半稳定toric log Fano对，则利用toric几何的凸分析工具和K稳定性条件推导类似的体积间隙估计。

Result: 得到了尖锐的体积间隙估计：在Ric(ω)≥(n+1)ω条件下，非P^n的n维紧致Kähler流形体积不超过(2^{n+1}π^n n^n)/(n+1)^n = (2n^n/(n+1)^n)·vol(P^n, ω_FS)。等号成立当且仅当流形等距于Q^n或P^1×P^{n-1}上的Kähler-Einstein度量。同时获得了K半稳定toric log Fano对的相应尖锐估计。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了Kähler几何中一个完整的体积间隙分类定理，将紧致Kähler流形在正Ricci曲率下的极端体积情形完全刻画为两种标准几何模型（quadric和乘积空间）。这不仅推广了经典的Frobenius-Bishop-Gromov型结果，还为K稳定性理论下的log Fano对提供了新的体积上界估计。

Abstract: We prove a sharp volume gap estimate: if an $n$-dimensional compact Kähler manifold $(X, ω)$ satisfies $\mathrm{Ric}(ω)\ge (n+1)ω$ and $X\not\cong \mathbb{P}^n$, then $\mathrm{vol}(X, ω)\le \frac{2n^n}{(n+1)^n}\mathrm{vol}(\mathbb{P}^n,ω_{\mathrm{FS}})=\frac{2^{n+1} \, π^n \, n^n}{(n+1)^n}$. Moreover $\mathrm{vol}(X, ω)= \frac{2^{n+1} \, π^n \, n^n}{(n+1)^n}$ occurs if and only if $(X, ω)$ is biholomorphically isometric to the Kähler-Einstein metric on the quadric hypersurface $Q^n$ or on the product $\mathbb{P}^1\times \mathbb{P}^{n-1}$. We also obtain sharp volume gap estimates for K-semistable toric log Fano pairs.

</details>


### [266] [The Harmonic Variational Principle for the Einstein-Hilbert Functional](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08233)
*Sergey Stepanov,Irina Tsyganok*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在紧凑黎曼流形上引入受调和规范条件限制的Einstein-Hilbert泛函变分原理，证明调和临界度量与紧致梯度Ricci孤子度量之间的一一对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Einstein-Hilbert变分原理要求度量变分满足适当的约束条件，作者希望通过引入调和规范条件来建立变分临界点与Ricci孤子之间的联系，从而为理解Ricci孤子提供一个新的变分框架。

Method: 在n维紧致黎曼流形(M,g)上，对Einstein-Hilbert泛函施加调和规范条件（要求允许的度量变分满足调和规范），推导出相应的Euler-Lagrange方程。利用伴随Bianchi算子的像以及Perelman定理进行结构分析，建立调和临界度量与Ricci孤子之间的联系。

Result: 证明了体积保持调和变分下度量临界的充要条件是其Einstein张量与度量的倍率之差落在伴随Bianchi算子的像中。每个调和临界度量决定一个紧致Ricci孤子，由Perelman定理知其为梯度Ricci孤子；反之亦然。还证明了规范单形式与孤子势的负微分相差一个Killing单形式，Ricci张量负定时规范单形式消失且度量为Einstein，非Einstein的调和临界度量必为收缩型。

Conclusion: 建立了调和临界度量与紧致梯度Ricci孤子度量之间的等价性，为梯度Ricci孤子的存在性提供了新的变分刻画。

Abstract: Let (M,g) be a compact n-dimensional Riemannian manifold, n>2. We introduce a restricted variational principle for the Einstein-Hilbert functional by requiring the admissible metric variations to satisfy the harmonic gauge condition. We derive the corresponding Euler-Lagrange equation and show that a metric is critical with respect to all volume-preserving harmonic variations if and only if its Einstein tensor differs from a multiple of the metric by an element of the image of the adjoint Bianchi operator. We prove that every harmonic critical metric determines a compact Ricci soliton whose soliton constant is given by the normalized Einstein-Hilbert functional. By Perelman's theorem, every such metric is in fact the metric of a compact gradient Ricci soliton. Conversely, every compact gradient Ricci soliton satisfies the restricted Euler-Lagrange equation. Thus, a compact Riemannian metric is harmonic critical if and only if it is the metric of a compact gradient Ricci soliton. We further show that the gauge one-form differs from the negative differential of a soliton potential by a Killing one-form. In particular, if the Ricci tensor is negative definite, then the gauge one-form vanishes and the metric is Einstein. Moreover, every non-Einstein harmonic critical metric is necessarily shrinking.

</details>


### [267] [Canonical Connections and Algebraic Ricci Solitons on Lorentzian 4-Dimensional Nilpotent Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08370)
*Youssef Ayad*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在带有乘积结构的四维洛伦兹幂零李群上，计算了典范联络和Kobayashi-Nomizu联络及其曲率，并分类了与这些联络相关的代数Ricci孤立子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在洛伦兹几何和幂零李群背景下，自然联络（如典范联络和Kobayashi-Nomizu联络）及其曲率结构是理解几何与分析性质的关键。此外，代数Ricci孤立子作为Ricci流中的自相似解，在黎曼几何中已有广泛研究，但在洛伦兹幂零李群上以及相对于非黎曼联络的背景下尚未得到系统处理。

Method: 本文针对四维洛伦兹幂零李群，在配备乘积结构的条件下，显式地计算典范联络和Kobayashi-Nomizu联络及其曲率张量；随后利用这些计算，对相应的代数Ricci孤立子方程进行求解和分类。

Result: 给出了四维洛伦兹幂零李群上典范联络和Kobayashi-Nomizu联络的曲率表达式，并完成了与这两种联络相关的代数Ricci孤立子的完整分类。

Conclusion: 在洛伦兹幂零李群的乘积结构框架下，典范联络和Kobayashi-Nomizu联络的几何性质可以被完整刻画，且对应的代数Ricci孤立子具有明确的结构分类，为后续研究更高维或更一般情形奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we compute the canonical and Kobayashi-Nomizu connections, together with their curvature, on Lorentzian four-dimensional nilpotent Lie groups endowed with a product structure. We also classify the algebraic Ricci solitons associated with these connections.

</details>


### [268] [On rigidity of Finsler manifolds without conjugate points and with constant $S$-curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08387)
*Anthony J. García,José Barbosa Gomes,Rafael O. Ruggiero*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究闭合Finsler流形在无共轭点且具有常S-曲率条件下的刚性现象，证明在某些正则性假设下，这类流形必为Riemannian流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Finsler几何中，S-曲率是衡量体积形变的重要不变量。作者希望探索在无共轭点流形上，常S-曲率这一条件对度量结构的刚性约束，特别是在与Green丛和测地流动力学相关联的情形下，回答何时Finsler结构退化为Riemannian结构这一问题。

Method: 作者将Cartan向量场作为Jacobi场进行分析，并研究其与Green丛几何的相互作用。在C^ω（实解析）情形下，利用常S-曲率和连续Green丛假设结合双曲闭测地线的存在性推导刚性结论；在C^∞情形下，需要额外假设测地流是传递的。

Result: 证明了一个闭C^ω Finsler流形若具有常S-曲率、连续Green丛且存在双曲闭测地线，则必为Riemannian流形。在C^∞情形下，若测地流传递，则同样结论成立。作为推论，对具有均匀可见性万能覆盖的Finsler流形获得刚性结果。

Conclusion: 常S-曲率与双曲测地动力学条件共同强有力地约束了Finsler结构，迫使它退化为Riemannian度量。这为Finsler几何中的刚性理论提供了新的结果，并连接了S-曲率、Green丛与测地流的传递性等概念。

Abstract: We study rigidity phenomena in closed Finsler manifolds without conjugate points under assumptions on the $S$-curvature. We prove that a closed $C^ω$ Finsler manifold with constant $S$-curvature, continuous Green bundles, and admitting a hyperbolic closed geodesic must be Riemannian. In the $C^\infty$ setting, the same conclusion holds under the additional assumption that the geodesic flow is transitive. As a consequence, we obtain rigidity results for Finsler manifolds with uniform visibility universal covering. Our approach is based on the analysis of the Cartan vector field as a Jacobi field and its interaction with the geometry of Green bundles.

</details>


### [269] [Open full-metric formation and local marked first-order asymptotic moduli of FIK blowdown singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08533)
*Henry Shin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Feldman–Ilmanen–Knopf (FIK)收缩奇点在光滑黎曼度量全空间中具有非空开性形成盆地，并在更小邻域上建立了局部带标记的一阶渐近模量。这首次在无对称性、无Kähler条件、无有限维调参的情况下，给出了以非紧非柱状收缩子为模型的有限时间奇点形成的全度量开性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决Ricci流有限时间奇点形成理论中的一个根本性问题：此前关于几类奇点（如O'Neil–Stolarsky、柱状收缩子等）的形成定理要么依赖对称性假设，要么需要Kähler结构或有限维参数调节，尚无针对FIK非紧非柱状收缩子的开性形成定理。作者的目标是证明FIK奇点在完全一般的四维黎曼流形上能够从开集初始度量中自然涌现，并提供完备的一阶形变坐标。

Method: 通过加权FIK算子的谱分析识别其九维非负特征空间（对应尺度和微分同胚方向），使用精确调制（exact modulation）方法消除该块。证明在带散射边界的加权函数空间中，线性化算子在正交补上具有一致谱间隙，从而建立Ricci流解的开性存在性和紧性。在较小的little-Hölder h^{2,α}邻域上，通过固定正时刻重启构造一阶剖面坐标，利用分裂C^1浸没性质将振幅映射到特征空间E_1。

Result: 在任意闭连通定向四维黎曼流形上，给定植入点和正时间界，存在相对C^{2,α}-开集的光滑度量，其Ricci流在该时间前形成局部FIK奇点并具有全局Type-I曲率控制。曲率在植入区域外一致有界；固定约定的标记重缩放沿完整奇异时间序列收敛到远古FIK流；例外球面具有面积、内蕴直径和曲率的精确FIK渐近。在小h^{2,α}邻域上建立了标记一阶剖面坐标，振幅为分裂C^1浸没且为到E_1的局部投影，唯一实现振幅的横截圆盘、Jacobi场收敛性和二次尺度/相位响应均由振幅完全决定。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对非紧非柱状收缩子模型的有限时间奇点的全度量形成定理，完全不需要对称性、Kähler条件或有限维调参。FIK奇点在完全一般的黎曼流形上自然地、可稳定地形成，其局部一阶模量由E_1上的振幅坐标完整刻画，为奇点理论中的模量（moduli）分析提供了完整框架。

Abstract: We prove that the Feldman--Ilmanen--Knopf (FIK) blowdown singularity has a nonempty open nonlinear formation basin in the full space of smooth Riemannian metrics and, on a smaller neighborhood, local marked first-order asymptotic moduli. Given a closed connected oriented Riemannian four-manifold, an implantation point, and a positive time bound, its oriented blow-up admits a relatively $C^{2,α}$-open set of smooth metrics whose Ricci flows form, before that time, a localized FIK singularity with global Type-I curvature control. No symmetry, Kähler condition, or finite-dimensional tuning is imposed. Curvature stays uniformly bounded outside the implantation region; fixed-convention marked rescalings converge along the full singular-time sequence to the ancient FIK flow; and the exceptional sphere has sharp FIK asymptotics for area, intrinsic diameter, and curvature. To our knowledge, this is the first open full-metric formation theorem for a finite-time singularity modeled on a noncompact, noncylindrical shrinker.
  A self-contained spectral certification identifies the weighted FIK operator's nine-dimensional nonnegative space with the scaling and diffeomorphism directions, and exact modulation removes this block. On a smaller little-Hölder $h^{2,α}$ neighborhood, a fixed positive-time restart yields the marked first-profile coordinate $\mathfrak{A}_1=λ_\infty^{-γ_1}V_\infty\in E_1$. This amplitude is a split $C^1$ submersion and locally the projection onto $E_1$. Equality of amplitudes characterizes marked first-order agreement; a transverse disk uniquely realizes each sufficiently small amplitude; normalized two-flow differences converge to the corresponding Jacobi field; and the amplitude determines the first quadratic scale and phase response. This gives a complete local marked first-order profile coordinate for FIK blowdown.

</details>


### [270] [An Alexandrov-type theorem in warped product manifolds with radial density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08548)
*Jinchuan Bai,Chao Xia*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在带径向密度的一类翘积流形中，对闭嵌入超曲面建立了Heintze-Karcher不等式，并由此证明了常加权平均曲率超曲面的Alexandrov型定理。作为特例，证明了欧氏空间中闭嵌入的λ-自展开子必为以原点为中心的圆球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Heintze-Karcher不等式是几何分析中的重要工具，将它与加权几何（径向密度）结合，可以研究带密度的翘积流形中超曲面的几何性质。此外，自展开子与平均曲率流密切相关，证明其在欧氏空间中必须是标准球面，有助于理解平均曲率流的奇点分类问题。

Method: 作者在带径向密度的翘积流形中，利用比较几何技术和适当的曲率条件，建立了闭嵌入超曲面的Heintze-Karcher不等式。基于该不等式，结合平衡公式（balance formula）或类似方法，推导出常加权平均曲率超曲面的Alexandrov型刚性定理。

Result: 证明了在满足给定条件的翘积流形中，闭嵌入常加权平均曲率超曲面必为测地球（加权意义下的球面）。作为直接推论，得到了欧氏空间中闭嵌入λ-自展开子必是以原点为中心的圆球面的结论。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典的Heintze-Karcher不等式和Alexandrov定理推广到加权翘积流形背景，并解决了λ-自展开子的球面刚性猜想在闭嵌入情形下的一个重要情形，对平均曲率流奇点分类和加权几何的进一步研究具有理论意义。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a Heintze--Karcher inequality for closed embedded hypersurfaces in a class of warped product manifolds endowed with radial density. As a consequence, we prove Alexandrov-type theorem for constant weighted mean curvature hypersurfaces in such spaces. In particular, we prove that a closed embedded $λ$-self-expander in the Euclidean space must be a round sphere centered at the origin.

</details>


### [271] [The Fino--Vezzoni conjecture on homogeneous spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08665)
*Joseph Kwong*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明：具有紧致迷向子群的复齐次空间的紧致离散商空间，若同时容许平衡度量与pluriclosed度量，则必为Kähler流形；若其实第一Chern类消失，则从任意不变pluriclosed度量出发的pluriclosed流全局存在并光滑收敛到平坦Kähler度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在探讨复齐次空间的紧致离散商空间在何种条件下成为Kähler流形，以及pluriclosed流的长时间存在性与收敛性问题。这是复几何与几何流领域的基础性问题，对于理解非Kähler流形的几何结构具有重要意义。

Method: 作者通过分析复齐次空间上平衡度量与pluriclosed度量并存的条件，结合第一Chern类的消失假设，利用pluriclosed流的解析性质来推导Kähler性，并证明流的全局存在性与收敛性。

Result: 主要结果有二：(1) 当紧致离散商空间同时具有平衡度量和pluriclosed度量时，它必然是Kähler的；(2) 当实第一Chern类为零时，从任意不变的pluriclosed度量出发的pluriclosed流对所有时间存在，并光滑收敛到一个平坦Kähler度量。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了平衡条件与pluriclosed条件的共存对复齐次空间商流形Kähler性的强烈约束，并通过pluriclosed流的收敛性给出了平坦Kähler结构的存在性，为理解非Kähler复流形的分类提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We prove that a compact discrete quotient of a complex homogeneous space with compact isotropy is Kähler whenever it admits both a balanced metric and a pluriclosed metric. Moreover, if its real first Chern class vanishes, then the pluriclosed flow starting from any invariant pluriclosed metric exists for all time and converges smoothly to a flat Kähler metric.

</details>


### [272] [Scalar Curvature Flexibility in the Riemannian Burnett Compactness Class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08707)
*Jingbo Wan*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明：在无边界连通光滑n维流形（n≥3，若流形开放则κ≤0）上，任何满足Scal≥κ的光滑黎曼度量g0都可以作为满足Scal=κ的光滑黎曼度量序列gi的局部一致极限，且gi在W^{1,∞}中局部一致有界。由此结合Gromov的C^0稳定性定理，得到闭包恒等式：{Scal=κ}在C^{0,α}_{loc}拓扑下的闭包等于{Scal≥κ}（对所有α∈(0,1)），且α<1的限制是锐利的。特别地，κ=0时证明了并强化了Huneau和Luk提出的黎曼逆Burnett猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机是理解标量曲率约束下黎曼度量的近似与极限问题。具体而言，作者旨在建立满足Scal≥κ的度量可以被满足Scal=κ的度量序列在合适的拓扑意义下逼近，从而将"不等式约束"的度量空间视为"等式约束"度量空间的闭包，并证明并强化经典的逆Burnett猜想。

Method: 作者通过构造满足严格标量曲率约束的度量序列gi→g0，使得每个gi满足Scal_{g_i}=κ，同时保证gi在W^{1,∞}范数下局部一致有界。论文的核心方法涉及对流形进行适当的局部处理和度量变形技术，以实现对标量曲率的精确控制。对于开流形情形，需要处理κ≤0的条件限制。

Result: 证明了任何满足Scal≥κ的光滑度量都是满足Scal=κ的光滑度量序列的局部一致极限（序列在W^{1,∞}中局部一致有界）。作为推论，得到了闭包恒等式，即在C^{0,α}_{loc}拓扑（0<α<1）下，常标量曲率κ度量的闭包恰为标量曲率≥κ的度量集合。同时证明了α<1的锐利性（即α=1时结论不成立）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了黎曼几何中关于标量曲率下限约束的稠密性和稳定性结果，在κ=0情形下证明并强化了Huneau-Luk的黎曼逆Burnett猜想，为理解标量曲率约束下度量空间的拓扑结构提供了新的深刻见解。

Abstract: Let $M$ be a connected smooth $n$-manifold without boundary, where $n\geq3$, and let $κ\in\mathbb{R}$, with $κ\leq0$ if $M$ is open. We prove that every smooth Riemannian metric $g_0$ with $\mathrm{Scal}_{g_0}\geqκ$ is a locally uniform limit of smooth Riemannian metrics $g_i$ with $\mathrm{Scal}_{g_i}=κ$ that are locally uniformly bounded in $W^{1,\infty}$. As a corollary, combining this with Gromov's $C^0$-stability theorem, we obtain the perhaps surprising identity \[ \overline{\{g:\mathrm{Scal}_g=κ\}}^{\,C^{0,α}_{\mathrm{loc}}}=\{g:\mathrm{Scal}_g\geqκ\}, \quad \forall α\in(0,1). \] The restriction $α<1$ is sharp. At $κ=0$, this proves and strengthens the Riemannian reverse-Burnett conjecture of Huneau and Luk.

</details>


### [273] [Response Geometry for Einstein metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08777)
*Anna Siffert*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文为光滑参数化的爱因斯坦度量族建立了一种"响应几何"框架：通过探针观测的微分定义响应丛态射，拉回观测丛上的固定度量得到正半定响应张量，其核恰好是第一阶不可见参数方向的空间。在完全响应轨迹（complete-response locus）上，该张量成为黎曼度量，可用于局部刚性分析和定量重建估计。文中还研究了秩亏轨迹、商几何、Gram与条件算子、响应体积及简单响应特征值的协变变分公式，并证明了在显式可实现性假设下，有限维参数空间的每个方向可由单个标量观测检测。该框架应用于爱因斯坦探测原理的调和探针包，并以有限维区间矩阵例子和标记的单位体积平坦二环面为例做了完全显式的说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在参数化爱因斯坦度量族中，如何通过探针观测来区分不同度量（即参数方向的可观测性/不可见性），并对此建立定量的几何与数值分析工具，是反问题与几何探测领域的核心问题。现有方法缺乏统一的几何框架来刻画观测映射的局部刚性与重建性，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 核心工具是响应丛态射（response bundle morphism）的构造。作者将观测的微分视为从参数丛到观测丛的态射，通过拉回预先给定的观测丛度量，定义正半定响应张量。其核刻画了不可见方向，而完全响应轨迹上该张量退化为黎曼度量。方法还包括：秩亏轨迹的商几何分析、Gram算子与条件算子、响应体积、响应特征值的协变变分公式，以及单一标量观测对有限维参数空间的全方向检测定理。将所有构造实例化到爱因斯坦探测原理的调和探针包上。

Result: 得到的主要结果包括：(1) 响应张量核与不可见方向空间精确对应；(2) 完全响应轨迹上的局部刚性与定量重建估计；(3) 秩亏轨迹与商几何的结构描述；(4) 单标量观测检测全方向的存在性定理；(5) 有限维区间矩阵例子给出定量公式；(6) 标记单位体积平坦二环面上，三个调和能量观测可全局重建标记度量，诱导的响应度量具有显式的正曲率双曲面实现，且条件数朝尖点（cusp）方向退化。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的响应几何框架，将爱因斯坦度量族的可观测性、刚性与重建问题转化为响应张量及其伴随几何对象的研究。完全显式的平环例子不仅验证了理论框架，还展示了正曲率响应度量与条件数退化等有趣现象，为未来在更一般几何反问题中的应用奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We develop a response geometry for smooth parameterized families of Einstein metrics equipped with canonical probe data. The differential of the probe observations defines a response bundle morphism; pulling back a fixed metric on the observation bundle gives a positive-semidefinite response tensor whose kernel is exactly the space of first-order invisible parameter directions. On the complete-response locus this tensor is Riemannian and yields local rigidity and quantitative reconstruction estimates whenever the response is locally realized by an observation map.
  We study rank-defect loci, the associated quotient geometry, Gram and conditioning operators, response volume, and covariant variation formulas for simple response eigenvalues. Under an explicit realizability hypothesis we also show that a single scalar observation can be chosen to detect every direction of a finite-dimensional parameter space. These constructions are applied to the harmonic-probe package arising from the Einstein Detection Principle. A finite-dimensional interval-matrix example illustrates the quantitative formulas, while marked unit-volume flat two-tori provide a fully explicit model: three harmonic-energy observations reconstruct the marked metric globally, the induced response metric admits an explicit positively curved hyperboloid realization, and its conditioning deteriorates toward the cusp.

</details>


### [274] [Sasaki with torsion manifolds and string backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08781)
*Beatrice Brienza,Anna Fino,Udhav Fowdar,Gueo Grantcharov*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究带有挠率的Sasaki流形（类比Hermitian几何中的Bismut联络），刻画了co-Kähler-like和平坦的Sasaki流形，引入了∇-Einstein流形的概念（奇数维的Bismut Hermite-Einstein条件类比），构造了非紧例子并在5维和7维中研究紧∇-Einstein流形。同时发展了几乎切触度规结构的几何流一般框架，推导出保持强条件（挠率闭合）的Sasaki流形流，并证明该流与广义Ricci流规范等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类比Hermitian几何中的Bismut联络，希望建立Sasaki几何中带挠率的连通理论框架，并引入相应的Einstein型条件与几何流。

Method: 将Bismut联络的思想移植到Sasaki流形上，定义带挠率的Sasaki结构；刻画co-Kähler-like与平坦流形；引入∇-Einstein流形概念；构造非紧例子并分析5、7维紧情形；发展几乎切触度规结构的几何流框架，推导保持挠率闭合的流并证明其与广义Ricci流规范等价。

Result: 给出了co-Kähler-like与平坦Sasaki带挠率流形的完整刻画，构造了∇-Einstein流形的非紧例子，并在5维和7维中研究了紧∇-Einstein流形。建立的Sasaki带挠率流是规范等价于广义Ricci流的。

Conclusion: 成功将Bismut联络框架推广到Sasaki几何，建立了∇-Einstein流形理论并揭示了其几何流与广义Ricci流之间深刻的等价关系。

Abstract: Motivated by the analogy with the Bismut connection in Hermitian geometry, we study Sasaki with torsion manifolds. In particular, we characterize co-Kähler-like and flat Sasaki with torsion manifolds, and we introduce the notion of a $\nabla$-Einstein manifold as the odd-dimensional analogue of the Bismut Hermite-Einstein condition. We provide non-compact examples and we study compact $\nabla$-Einstein manifold in dimension $5$ and $7$. We also develop a general framework for geometric flows of almost contact metric structures. In particular, we derive a flow for Sasaki with torsion structures that preserves the strong condition, that is, the closure of the torsion. Furthermore we prove that such flow is gauge-equivalent to the generalized Ricci flow.

</details>


### [275] [Manton's Exotic Vortex Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08973)
*Takashi Ono*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究Manton奇异涡旋方程，建立其解的存在性结果，构造解的模空间，并建立该方程与具有(1,1)签名伪Hermitian度量的伪Hermitian-Yang-Mills联络之间的降维对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Manton奇异涡旋方程源于Manton对经典涡旋方程形变的研究，该方程具有独特的数学结构，值得深入探究其解的存在性、模空间结构以及与其它几何方程的联系。

Method: 本文首先研究Manton奇异涡旋方程解的存在性问题并建立存在性结果；其次构造解的模空间；最后建立降维对应关系，即证明Manton奇异涡旋方程的解与带(1,1)签名伪Hermitian度量的伪Hermitian-Yang-Mills联络之间存在一一对应。

Result: 成功建立了Manton奇异涡旋方程解的存在性定理，构造了其模空间，并建立了该方程与伪Hermitian-Yang-Mills方程之间的降维对应（对偶）关系。

Conclusion: Manton奇异涡旋方程不仅在自身层面拥有完整的存在性和模空间理论，更重要的是揭示了其与伪Hermitian-Yang-Mills方程（带(1,1)签名伪Hermitian度量）之间的深刻内在联系，为这两类方程的统一研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the equation which we call Manton's exotic vortex equation. The equation arose in Manton's study of a deformation of the classical vortex equation [Ma]. We first investigate the existence problem for Manton's exotic vortex equation and establish existence results.
  We then construct the moduli space of solutions.
  Finally, we establish a dimensional reduction for Manton's exotic vortex equation.
  More precisely, we establish a correspondence between solutions of Manton's exotic vortex equation and pseudo-Hermitian-Yang--Mills connections with pseudo-Hermitian metrics of signature (1,1).

</details>


### [276] [A global variational calculus on Fréchet manifolds and the Lagrangian structure of the Einstein evolution equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08987)
*José Antonio Vallejo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文为Fréchet流形上的曲线建立了变分法，并将其应用于爱因斯坦演化方程的拉格朗日表述，证明了欧拉-拉格朗日方程、能量守恒和无穷小Noether定理，同时推导出哈密顿约束与动量约束的传播，并证明了Maupertuis-Jacobi定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱因斯坦演化方程在度量空间上的几何结构需要合适的无穷维微分几何框架，Fréchet流形提供了这一工具。此前缺乏一个系统的变分框架来处理弱伪黎曼度量下的拉格朗日力学，特别是约束传播和Noether守恒量之间的关系尚未被严格建立。

Method: 本文在紧致流形的黎曼度量构成的Fréchet流形M上建立了变分法；对于与弱伪黎曼度量相关的力学拉格朗日量，推导了欧拉-拉格朗日方程和能量守恒；提出了不要求对称向量场生成流的无穷小Noether定理；将此框架应用于DeWitt拉格朗日量，识别动量约束为Noether动量映射的消失；推导了点态传输恒等式∂t(Hλ dvg)=2α δg(δg^-k) dvg；最后证明了弱伪黎曼度量下的Maupertuis-Jacobi定理。

Result: 建立了Fréchet流形上变分法的完整框架，从Noether定理直接得到动量约束的传播，通过传输恒等式证明了哈密顿约束的传播，并证明在标量曲率势的零点集之外，受约束的爱因斯坦演化等价于共形DeWitt度量-4αSλG^-的单位速度测地线（经重参数化）。

Conclusion: 该工作将爱因斯坦演化方程的约束结构统一在其几何变分框架下，揭示了动量约束和哈密顿约束分别作为Noether守恒量与传输恒等式的自然推论，并通过Maupertuis-Jacobi定理建立了爱因斯坦演化与共形DeWitt度量测地线之间的等价性。

Abstract: We develop a variational calculus for curves on Fréchet manifolds and apply it to the Lagrangian formulation of the Einstein evolution equations on the manifold $\Mm$ of Riemannian metrics of a compact manifold. For mechanical Lagrangians associated with weak pseudo-Riemannian metrics, we establish the Euler--Lagrange equations, energy conservation, and an infinitesimal Noether theorem that does not require the symmetry vector field to generate a flow. Applied to the DeWitt Lagrangian, this framework identifies the momentum constraint with the vanishing of a Noether momentum map and gives a direct proof of its propagation. We also derive the pointwise transport identity \[ \partial_t\bigl(\Ham_λ\,\dv_g\bigr) = 2α\,δ_g(δ_g^-k)\,\dv_g\,, \] which implies propagation of the Hamiltonian constraint. Finally, we prove a Maupertuis-- Jacobi theorem for weak pseudo-Riemannian metrics on Fréchet manifolds and show that, away from the zero set of the total scalar-curvature potential, constrained Einstein evolutions are reparametrized unit-speed geodesics of the conformal DeWitt metric $-4αS_λG^-$.

</details>


### [277] [Torus and Positive Mass stability for metrics with Ricci curvature lower bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09067)
*Edward Bryden,Zhizhang Xie*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了环面上的度量序列与渐近平坦自旋黎曼流形序列的收敛性。当环面度量序列具有一致第一稳定系统长度下界、Ricci曲率下界和直径上界，且标量曲率负部的L1范数趋于零时，该序列在测度-Gromov-Hausdorff拓扑下收敛到平坦度量。类似地，具有Ricci曲率下界、非负标量曲率以及ADM质量趋于零的渐近平坦自旋流形序列将在点态测度-Gromov-Hausdorff意义下收敛到欧氏空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立曲率条件与几何收敛性之间的关系，将稳定系统长度、Ricci曲率下界和标量曲率信息结合起来，刻画环面度量序列在退化情形下收敛到平坦度量的条件；同时将类似的紧凑性结论推广到渐近平坦自旋流形（ADM质量消失的情形收敛到欧氏空间）。

Method: 本文采用测度-Gromov-Hausdorff（mGH）收敛框架，结合Ricci曲率的一致下界与直径上界来保证预紧性（precompactness），再利用标量曲率负部L1范数消失的条件以及稳定系统长度的下界来排除退化/奇异极限，最终得到收敛到平坦环面度量的结论。对于渐近平坦情形，则利用自旋流形上的Dirac算子与ADM质量的度量性质来建立点态mGH收敛到欧氏空间。

Result: 证明了在给定条件下，环面度量序列存在子序列在测度-Gromov-Hausdorff拓扑下收敛到平坦度量；渐近平坦自旋流形序列在点态测度-Gromov-Hausdorff拓扑下收敛到欧氏空间。

Conclusion: 该结果表明：标量曲率负部能量在一序列中消失，加上稳定系统长度和Ricci曲率的下界以及直径上界，足以保证几何退化到平坦/欧氏极限，为理解标量曲率与几何刚性之间的联系提供了新的紧致性结果。

Abstract: Consider a sequence of metrics $g_i$ on the torus whose members have uniform lower bounds on their first stable systoles and Ricci curvatures, and have an uniform upper bound on their diameters. If the $L^1$ norm of the negative part of the scalar curvatures vanishes along this sequence, then we show there is a subsequence of the metrics which converges in themeasured-Gromov-Hausdorff topology to a flat metric on the torus. Something analogous is true for sequences of asymptotically flat spin Riemannian manifolds with lower bounds on their Ricci curvature, nonnegative scalar curvatures, and ADM-mass vanishing along the sequence: the sequence will pointed measured Gromov-Hausdorff converge to Euclidean space.

</details>


### [278] [Unsigned Frenet Data: Exact Fibres, Generic Rigidity, and a Quantitative Dichotomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09194)
*JiYe Liu,Wenkai Wang,Qiang Tian,Wenjun Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究三维欧氏空间中闭嵌入曲线（曲率κ>0）在失去环境定向信息后，由无符号Frenet数据(κ,|τ|)所能确定的几何信息。核心结论是：一切由扭转τ的无穷阶零点补集的连通分量数c(τ)决定，光滑有符号提升恰有2^{c(τ)}个；当c(τ)≤1时只有一对镜像类；该界是尖锐的（可构造任意2^m个同余类的例子）；简单零点的曲线在C^r嵌入空间中开且稠密，此时无符号数据足以在O(3)意义下确定曲线；进一步给出了定量稳定性估计（log-Lipschitz模）并证明该模不可改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Frenet-Serret理论表明，正曲率闭空间曲线由曲率κ和带符号扭转τ确定（差一个刚体运动）。但当观测者没有环境定向信息时，扭转的符号首先丢失。本文要回答：在丢失τ的符号信息后，曲线在多大程度上仍可被确定？哪些几何信息在定向缺失下仍保存？

Method: (1) 通过分析τ的无穷阶零点集的补集连通分量数c(τ)，刻画从|τ|恢复有符号光滑函数τ的所有可能方式（最多2^{c(τ)}种）；(2) 通过构造具体的无符号数据，利用连通和的K_i^{±1}实现2^m个同余类，证明上界尖锐；(3) 对仅有简单零点的扭转（在C^r嵌入空间中开且稠密），证明O(3)意义下的唯一性；(4) 在Δ=inf√(τ²+(τ')²)≥δ>0的层上，用平方根恢复的一维有符号函数的log-Lipschitz模给出E(3)轨道距离的定量估计，并证明log项不可去掉。

Result: (1) 光滑有符号提升数恰为2^{c(τ)}，当c(τ)≤1时归结为{τ,-τ}（即单一镜像对），该界锐利；(2) 对任意m≥1和任意纽结类型K_1,...,K_m，可构造一个无符号数据其纤维恰含2^m个同余类（所有K_i^{±1}的连通和）；(3) 扭转仅有简单零点的曲线在C^r（r≥4）嵌入空间中开且稠密，且每个这样的曲线由(κ,|τ|)在O(3)意义下唯一确定；(4) 在Δ≥δ>0的层上，轨道距离有log-Lipschitz型估计：≤Cρ(‖(κ_α,τ_α²)-(κ_β,τ_β²)‖_{C^0})，ρ(t)=t(1+log_+1/t)，最优常数随δ→0发散；log项不可删除。

Conclusion: 从带符号Frenet数据到无符号数据，丢失的只有扭转符号；但符号丢失的程度由c(τ)精确刻画——它是"应该丢失多少"的完整拓扑计数。一般情况下最多丢失2^{c(τ)}个同余类（镜像配对），而简单零点情形（典型/generic）下无符号数据完全确定曲线（O(3)意义下）。定量层面，从τ²恢复τ本质上是一个一维平方根恢复问题，具有本质的log-Lipschitz稳定性且不可改进。

Abstract: A closed space curve with positive curvature is determined up to a rigid motion by its curvature and its signed torsion. The sign of the torsion is the one place where the ambient orientation enters the Frenet data, and it is the first thing lost whenever the data are read without an orientation. We determine what survives that loss. For a closed embedded curve in $\mathbb R^3$ with $κ>0$, everything is governed by one integer, the number $c(τ)$ of connected components of the complement of the set where $τ$ vanishes to infinite order. The smooth signed lifts of $|τ|$ number exactly $2^{c(τ)}$, and reduce to $\{τ,-τ\}$ if and only if $c(τ)\le1$; hence the fibre of curves carrying $(κ,|τ|)$ along a common arclength label has at most $2^{c(τ)}$ congruence classes, and is a single mirror pair whenever $c(τ)\le1$. Both extremes occur. For every $m\ge1$ and arbitrary knot types $K_1,\dots,K_m$ we construct one unsigned datum whose fibre consists of exactly $2^m$ congruence classes, realising all connected sums of the $K_i^{\pm1}$, so the bound is sharp. Conversely, curves whose torsion has only simple zeros are open and dense in the space of parametrised $C^r$ embeddings ($r\ge4$), and each is determined up to $O(3)$ by its unsigned datum. That generic rigidity admits no uniform quantitative form, but the obstruction localises at $Δ=\inf_s\sqrt{τ^2+(τ')^2}$. On each stratum $Δ\geδ>0$, under uniform $C^5$ and curvature bounds, the orbit distance modulo $E(3)$ is at most $Cρ(\|(κ_α,τ_α^2)-(κ_β,τ_β^2)\|_{C^0})$ with $ρ(t)=t(1+\log_+1/t)$, while the optimal constant diverges as $δ\to0$. The engine is one-dimensional: a signed function is recovered from its square with a uniform log-Lipschitz modulus from $C^0$ to $L^1$, and the logarithm cannot be removed.

</details>


### [279] [On the positivity of Yamabe invariant and Paneitz operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09279)
*Mingxiang Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究n≥5维紧致黎曼流形上共形几何问题，证明了共形度量同时具有正Q-曲率与正数量曲率当且仅当Yamabe不变量和Paneitz算子均为正。在n=5时证实了Gursky-Hang-Lin的猜想，并解决了Hang-Yang提出的问题，同时表明Gursky-Malchiodi与Hang-Yang的假设条件是等价的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在n≥5维流形上，共形几何中Q-曲率与数量曲率的符号性质是核心研究问题。Gursky-Hang-Lin在2016年提出了一个猜想，Hang-Yang在2016年提出了一个公开问题，本文旨在解决这些猜想和问题，并厘清不同文献中假设条件之间的等价关系。

Method: 本文采用变分法、非线性偏微分方程（特别是Paneitz方程与Yamabe方程的临界点分析）以及共形几何中的几何分析技术，通过分析Yamabe不变量与Paneitz算子正性之间的内在联系，建立共形度量存在性与这些几何不变量正性之间的等价关系。

Result: （1）证明了共形度量具有正Q-曲率和正数量曲率等价于Yamabe不变量与Paneitz算子均为正；（2）在n=5时验证了Gursky-Hang-Lin猜想；（3）在Yamabe不变量为正、Q_g≥0且非恒为零的条件下，证明了R_g与P_g均为正，解答了Hang-Yang的问题；（4）证明了Gursky-Malchiodi与Hang-Yang的假设条件等价。

Conclusion: 本文为高维共形几何提供了一个统一的理论框架，将Yamabe不变量、Paneitz算子和两种曲率（Q-曲率与数量曲率）的正性建立起了深刻的等价关系，解决了该领域的核心猜想与公开问题，推进了对高维共形指标和曲率符号性质的理解。

Abstract: Let $(M^n,g)$ be a smooth compact Riemannian manifold of dimension $n\ge 5$. We show that the existence of a conformal metric with positive $Q$-curvature $Q_g$ and positive scalar curvature $R_g$ is equivalent to the positivity of both the Yamabe invariant $Y(M^n,[g])$ and the Paneitz operator $P_g$. For $n=5$, this equivalence confirms a conjecture of Gursky-Hang-Lin (2016, IMRN). Furthermore, assuming $Y(M^n,[g])>0$, $Q_g\ge 0$, and $Q_g\not\equiv 0$, we prove that both $R_g$ and $P_g$ are positive which resolves a problem of Hang-Yang (2016, CPAM). As a corollary, we show that the hypotheses of Gursky-Malchiodi (2015, JEMS) are equivalent to those of Hang-Yang (2016, CPAM).

</details>


### [280] [Schwarzian Residue of the Samuelson Obstruction in Tangent Lagrangian 2-Webs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09825)
*Yasuhiro Kurokawa*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究平面拉格朗日2-网的Samuelson条件的局部障碍分解。对于切线族L_t:y=tx+h(t)，Samuelson障碍在对角点(t_0,t_0)附近分解为万有极点项1/(v-u)^2与光滑对角项之和，后者称为Schwarzian留数，可表示为R_h(t,t)=h^{(4)}(t)/(3h''(t))-(4/9)(h'''(t)/h''(t))^2=(1/2){σ,t}=-κ_aff(σ)(dσ/dt)^2，其中σ为包络线的等仿射弧长参数。该留数携带包络线的仿射射影几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Samuelson条件是平面拉格朗日2-网的经典面积比条件，局部等价于∂_u∂_v log|J_F|=0。研究的动机在于理解该条件在局部如何失效——特别是障碍项S_h在对角线附近的结构，以及其几何意义。

Method: 对切线族L_t构造交映射Ψ(u,v)，定义障碍函数S_h(u,v)=∂_u∂_v log|J_Ψ(u,v)|。在对角点(t_0,t_0)处（h''(t_0)≠0）将S_h分解为万有极点部分1/(v-u)^2与光滑余项R_h(u,v)，并将R_h(t,t)（即Schwarzian留数）用h的导数、Schwarzian导数{σ,t}、以及包络线γ(t)=(-h'(t),h(t)-th'(t))的等仿射曲率κ_aff来表达。同时推导Schwarzian留数在切线参数重参数化下的变换律。

Result: 得到Schwarzian留数的精确计算公式：R_h(t,t)=h^{(4)}(t)/(3h''(t))-(4/9)(h'''(t)/h''(t))^2，该式等价于(1/2){σ,t}=-κ_aff(σ)(dσ/dt)^2，其中σ是包络线的等仿射弧长参数。万有极点1/(v-u)^2完全刻画Samuelson条件在对角线附近的局部失效。

Conclusion: Samuelson条件的局部障碍由两部分构成：一个万有极点项（不依赖于具体切线族）和Schwarzian留数（携带包络线的仿射射影几何不变量）。这种分解揭示了Samuelson条件失效的通用机制，并将经典经济/几何条件与仿射微分几何信息建立了联系。

Abstract: The Samuelson condition, a classical area-ratio condition for planar Lagrangian $2$-webs, is equivalent in local web coordinates $(u,v)$ to $\partial_u\partial_v\log|J_F(u,v)|=0$, where $F$ is the inverse web-coordinate map and $J_F$ is its Jacobian. For the tangent-line family $L_t:y=tx+h(t)$, let $Ψ(u,v)$ be the intersection map of $L_u$ and $L_v$, and set $S_h(u,v)=\partial_u\partial_v\log|J_Ψ(u,v)|$. Near each diagonal point $(t_0,t_0)$ with $h''(t_0)\neq0$, the obstruction admits the decomposition $S_h(u,v)=1/(v-u)^2+R_h(u,v)$, where $R_h$ extends smoothly across the diagonal. We call $R_h(t,t)$ the Schwarzian residue and compute $R_h(t,t)=h^{(4)}(t)/(3h''(t))-(4/9)(h'''(t)/h''(t))^2=(1/2)\{σ,t\}=-κ_{\mathrm{aff}}(σ)(dσ/dt)^2$. Here $σ$ is the equi-affine arclength parameter of the envelope $γ(t)=(-h'(t),h(t)-th'(t))$, and $κ_{\mathrm{aff}}$ is its equi-affine curvature. Thus the universal pole accounts for the local failure of the Samuelson condition, while the diagonal finite part carries affine-projective information about the envelope. We also derive the transformation law of the Schwarzian residue under reparametrization of the tangent-line parameter.

</details>


### [281] [Eigenforms and special holonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09878)
*Bobby Acharya,Lucía Cabrera,Simone Corbo,Jason D. Lotay*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有特殊和乐群（special holonomy）的完备非紧Ricci平坦流形上Hodge Laplacian的$L^2$特征形式的存在性与不存在性结果，特别关注引力瞬子（gravitational instantons），并简要讨论了这些结果在超弦理论和M-理论中的物理意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有特殊和乐群的Ricci平坦流形上的$L^2$调和形式/特征形式，对于微分几何、全局分析和超弦/M-理论中的紧化与量子引力研究至关重要。

Method: 通过分析完备非紧Ricci平坦流形（尤其是引力瞬子）的谱性质，证明$L^2$特征形式的存在性与非存在性定理，并结合物理背景阐释其含义。

Result: 对于引力瞬子等特殊和乐群流形，建立了$L^2$特征形式存在与否的判定条件。

Conclusion: 所得结果为理解特殊和乐群流形上的谱几何提供了新见解，并对超弦/M-理论中的相关物理现象有重要启示。

Abstract: We prove existence and non-existence results for $L^2$ eigenforms for the Hodge Laplacian on complete non-compact Ricci flat manifolds with special holonomy, with a particular focus on gravitational instantons. We briefly describe the physical interpretation of these results in superstring and M-theory.

</details>
